> Why do I have a folder named ".vercel" in my project?
The ".vercel" folder is created when you link a directory to a Vercel project.

> What does the "project.json" file contain?
The "project.json" file contains:
- The ID of the Vercel project that you linked ("projectId")
- The ID of the user or team your Vercel project is owned by ("orgId")

> Should I commit the ".vercel" folder?
No, you should not share the ".vercel" folder with anyone.
Upon creation, it will be automatically added to your ".gitignore" file.

FILE: app\actions\enroll.ts

'use server'

import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'

export async function enrollInCourse(courseId: string) {
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

  if (!user) return { error: 'Not logged in' }

  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('enrollments')
    .insert({ user_id: user.id, course_id: courseId })

  if (error) return { error: error.message }

  revalidatePath('/dashboard')
  return { success: true }
}

// FILE: app/admin/actions.ts  (server actions for content CRUD)

'use server'

import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'

const ADMIN_EMAILS = [
  'yassine_lek@hotmail.com',
  'stephaniederoeck.jedits@gmail.com',
]

async function requireAdmin() {
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user || !ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(user.email || '')) {
    redirect('/dashboard')
  }
  return supabase
}

function refresh() {
  revalidatePath('/admin')
  revalidatePath('/courses')
  revalidatePath('/dashboard')
}

// ---------- SAGAS ----------

export async function createSaga(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const course_id = String(formData.get('course_id') || '')
  const title = String(formData.get('title') || '').trim()
  const description = String(formData.get('description') || '').trim()
  const order_index = parseInt(String(formData.get('order_index') || '1')) || 1
  if (!course_id || !title) return

  await supabase.from('modules').insert({
    course_id, title, description: description || null, order_index,
  })
  refresh()
}

export async function updateSaga(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const id = String(formData.get('id') || '')
  const title = String(formData.get('title') || '').trim()
  const description = String(formData.get('description') || '').trim()
  const order_index = parseInt(String(formData.get('order_index') || '1')) || 1
  if (!id || !title) return

  await supabase.from('modules').update({
    title, description: description || null, order_index,
  }).eq('id', id)
  refresh()
}

export async function deleteSaga(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const id = String(formData.get('id') || '')
  if (!id) return
  await supabase.from('modules').delete().eq('id', id)
  refresh()
}

// ---------- MISSIONS ----------

export async function createMission(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const module_id = String(formData.get('module_id') || '')
  const title = String(formData.get('title') || '').trim()
  const description = String(formData.get('description') || '').trim()
  const order_index = parseInt(String(formData.get('order_index') || '1')) || 1
  if (!module_id || !title) return

  await supabase.from('missions').insert({
    module_id, title, description: description || null, order_index,
  })
  refresh()
}

export async function updateMission(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const id = String(formData.get('id') || '')
  const title = String(formData.get('title') || '').trim()
  const description = String(formData.get('description') || '').trim()
  const order_index = parseInt(String(formData.get('order_index') || '1')) || 1
  if (!id || !title) return

  await supabase.from('missions').update({
    title, description: description || null, order_index,
  }).eq('id', id)
  refresh()
}

export async function deleteMission(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const id = String(formData.get('id') || '')
  if (!id) return
  await supabase.from('missions').delete().eq('id', id)
  refresh()
}

// ---------- QUESTS (lessons) ----------

export async function createQuest(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const mission_id = String(formData.get('mission_id') || '')
  const title = String(formData.get('title') || '').trim()
  const order_index = parseInt(String(formData.get('order_index') || '1')) || 1
  if (!mission_id || !title) return

  // mission_id needed on lessons; grab course_id from mission â†’ module
  const { data: mission } = await supabase
    .from('missions')
    .select('module_id, modules:module_id(course_id)')
    .eq('id', mission_id)
    .single()

  const course_id = (mission as any)?.modules?.course_id

  await supabase.from('lessons').insert({
    course_id,
    mission_id,
    title,
    order_index,
  })
  refresh()
}

export async function updateQuest(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const id = String(formData.get('id') || '')
  const title = String(formData.get('title') || '').trim()
  const order_index = parseInt(String(formData.get('order_index') || '1')) || 1
  if (!id || !title) return

  await supabase.from('lessons').update({
    title, order_index,
  }).eq('id', id)
  refresh()
}

export async function deleteQuest(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const id = String(formData.get('id') || '')
  if (!id) return
  await supabase.from('lessons').delete().eq('id', id)
  refresh()
}

// ---------- COURSES ----------

export async function createCourse(formData: FormData) {
  const supabase = await requireAdmin()
  const slug = String(formData.get('slug') || '').trim()
  const title = String(formData.get('title') || '').trim()
  const description = String(formData.get('description') || '').trim()
  const track = String(formData.get('track') || 'en_to_nl')
  const level = String(formData.get('level') || 'A1')
  if (!slug || !title) return

  await supabase.from('courses').insert({
    slug, title, description: description || null, track, level, published: true,
  })
  refresh()
}

// FILE: app/admin/DeleteButton.tsx  (delete with confirmation)

'use client'

export default function DeleteButton({
  action,
  id,
  label,
}: {
  action: (formData: FormData) => void
  id: string
  label: string
}) {
  return (
    <form
      action={action}
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        if (!confirm(`Delete "${label}"? This cannot be undone.`)) {
          e.preventDefault()
        }
      }}
      className="inline"
    >
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={id} />
      <button
        type="submit"
        className="text-xs text-red-400/70 hover:text-red-400 transition"
      >
        Delete
      </button>
    </form>
  )
}

FILE: app\admin\lessons\LessonEditor.tsx
================================================================

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function LessonEditor({
  lessonId,
  title,
  orderIndex,
  videoUrl,
  content,
}: {
  lessonId: string
  title: string
  orderIndex: number
  videoUrl: string
  content: string
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [url, setUrl] = useState(videoUrl)
  const [text, setText] = useState(content)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false)

  async function handleSave() {
    setSaving(true)
    setSaved(false)
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('lessons')
      .update({ video_url: url.trim() || null, content: text.trim() || null })
      .eq('id', lessonId)

    if (!error) {
      setSaved(true)
      router.refresh()
      setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2000)
    }
    setSaving(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="border border-neutral-800 rounded-xl p-5">
      <div className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mb-1">Lesson {orderIndex}</div>
      <div className="font-medium mb-4">{title}</div>

      <label className="block text-xs text-neutral-400 mb-1">Video URL</label>
      <input
        type="text"
        value={url}
        onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=..."
        className="w-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-3 focus:border-neutral-600 outline-none text-sm"
      />

      <label className="block text-xs text-neutral-400 mb-1">Lesson content</label>
      <textarea
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
        rows={3}
        className="w-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-3 focus:border-neutral-600 outline-none text-sm resize-none"
      />

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <button
          onClick={handleSave}
          disabled={saving}
          className="bg-white text-black text-sm font-semibold rounded-full px-5 py-1.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save'}
        </button>
        {saved && <span className="text-green-400 text-sm">âœ“ Saved</span>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: app\admin\lessons\page.tsx
================================================================

import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import LessonEditor from './LessonEditor'

const ADMIN_EMAIL = 'stephaniederoeck.jedits@gmail.com'

export default async function AdminLessonsPage() {
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

  if (!user) redirect('/login')
  if (user.email !== ADMIN_EMAIL) redirect('/dashboard')

  const { data: courses } = await supabase
    .from('courses')
    .select('*')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })

  const { data: lessons } = await supabase
    .from('lessons')
    .select('*')
    .order('order_index', { ascending: true })

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-950 text-white">
      <nav className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-5 border-b border-neutral-800">
        <Link href="/" className="text-xl font-bold">Meridian Academy</Link>
        <Link href="/dashboard" className="text-sm hover:text-neutral-300">Dashboard</Link>
      </nav>

      <section className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-6 py-12">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2">Admin â€” Lesson Videos</h1>
        <p className="text-neutral-400 mb-10">
          Paste a YouTube link (unlisted is fine) or any direct video URL.
        </p>

        {courses?.map((course) => (
          <div key={course.id} className="mb-12">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-4">{course.title}</h2>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {lessons
                ?.filter((l) => l.course_id === course.id)
                .map((lesson) => (
                  <LessonEditor
                    key={lesson.id}
                    lessonId={lesson.id}
                    title={lesson.title}
                    orderIndex={lesson.order_index}
                    videoUrl={lesson.video_url || ''}
                    content={lesson.content || ''}
                  />
                ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </section>
    </main>
  )
}

// FILE: app/admin/page.tsx  (URL: /admin â€” structure editor)

import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import AppShell from '@/components/AppShell'
import DeleteButton from './DeleteButton'
import {
  createSaga, updateSaga, deleteSaga,
  createMission, updateMission, deleteMission,
  createQuest, updateQuest, deleteQuest,
} from './actions'

const ADMIN_EMAILS = [
  'yassine_lek@hotmail.com',
  'stephaniederoeck.jedits@gmail.com',
]

export default async function AdminPage() {
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

  if (!user || !ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(user.email || '')) redirect('/dashboard')

  const { data: courses } = await supabase
    .from('courses')
    .select(`
      id, slug, title, level, track,
      modules (
        id, title, description, order_index,
        missions (
          id, title, description, order_index,
          lessons (id, title, order_index)
        )
      )
    `)
    .order('created_at')

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user.email}>
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 py-12">
        <div className="m-label m-label-cyan mb-3">Admin</div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-2">
          Content Structure
        </h1>
        <p className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)] mb-4">
          Manage courses, sagas, missions, and quests.
        </p>
        <div className="flex gap-5 mb-10 text-sm">
          <Link href="/admin/lessons" className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] hover:underline">
            â†’ Video & Codex editor
          </Link>
        </div>

        {courses?.map((course: any) => {
          const sagas = (course.modules || []).sort(
            (a: any, b: any) => a.order_index - b.order_index
          )
          return (
            <section key={course.id} className="m-panel p-6 mb-8">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <div>
                  <div className="m-label m-label-cyan mb-1">
                    {course.track === 'en_to_nl' ? 'EN â†’ NL' : 'NL â†’ EN'} Â· {course.level}
                  </div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">{course.title}</h2>
                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--m-text-dim)] font-mono">
                    slug: {course.slug}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Sagas */}
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {sagas.map((saga: any, si: number) => {
                  const missions = (saga.missions || []).sort(
                    (a: any, b: any) => a.order_index - b.order_index
                  )
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={saga.id}
                      className="border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg p-4 bg-[var(--m-bg)]/40"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-3">
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <div className="m-label mb-1">
                            Saga {String(si + 1).padStart(2, '0')} Â· order {saga.order_index}
                          </div>
                          <div className="font-semibold truncate">{saga.title}</div>
                          {saga.description && (
                            <div className="text-xs text-[var(--m-text-muted)] truncate">
                              {saga.description}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">

                            <summary className="text-xs text-[var(--m-cyan)] cursor-pointer hover:underline">
                              Edit
                            </summary>
                            <form
                              action={updateSaga}
                              className="mt-3 space-y-2 bg-[var(--m-surface)] p-3 rounded-lg w-72"

                              required
                              className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-sm"
                            />
                            <input
                              name="description"
                              placeholder="Description"
                              className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-sm"
                            />
                            <input
                              name="order_index"
                              type="number"
                              defaultValue={missions.length + 1}
                              className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-sm"
                            />
                            <button className="w-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold rounded py-1.5">
                              Create Mission
                            </button>
                          </form>
                        </details>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )
                })}

                {/* Add saga */}
                <details>
                  <summary className="text-sm text-[var(--m-cyan)] cursor-pointer hover:underline">
                    + Add Saga
                  </summary>
                  <form
                    action={createSaga}
                    className="mt-3 space-y-2 bg-[var(--m-surface)] p-3 rounded-lg"
                  >
                    <input type="hidden" name="course_id" value={course.id} />
                    <input
                      name="title"
                      placeholder="Saga title"
                      required
                      className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-sm"
                    />
                    <input
                      name="description"
                      placeholder="Description"
                      className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-sm"
                    />
                    <input
                      name="order_index"
                      type="number"
                      defaultValue={sagas.length + 1}
                      className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-sm"
                    />
                    <button className="w-full bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold rounded py-1.5">
                      Create Saga
                    </button>
                  </form>
                </details>
              </div>
            </section>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  )
}

// FILE: app/community/[channel]/[threadId]/page.tsx  (single thread page)

import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import AppShell from '@/components/AppShell'
import ReplyForm from './ReplyForm'

export default async function ThreadPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ channel: string; threadId: string }>
}) {
  const { channel: channelSlug, threadId } = await params
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

  const { data: channel } = await supabase
    .from('channels')
    .select('*')
    .eq('slug', channelSlug)
    .single()

  if (!channel) notFound()

  const { data: thread } = await supabase
    .from('threads')
    .select('*, profiles:user_id(full_name, username)')
    .eq('id', threadId)
    .single()

  if (!thread) notFound()

  const { data: posts } = await supabase
    .from('posts')
    .select('*, profiles:user_id(full_name, username)')
    .eq('thread_id', threadId)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user?.email}>
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 py-12">
        <Link
          href={`/community/${channelSlug}`}
          className="text-sm text-[var(--m-text-dim)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)]"
        >
          â† #{channel.name}
        </Link>

        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight mt-8 mb-2">
          {thread.title}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-10">
          by {(thread as any).profiles?.full_name || (thread as any).profiles?.username || 'someone'} Â·{' '}
          {new Date(thread.created_at).toLocaleString()}
        </p>

        <div className="space-y-3 mb-10">
          {posts && posts.length > 0 ? (
            posts.map((post: any) => (
              <div key={post.id} className="m-panel p-5">
                <div className="text-xs text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-2">
                  {post.profiles?.full_name || post.profiles?.username || 'someone'} Â·{' '}
                  {new Date(post.created_at).toLocaleString()}
                </div>
                <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-[var(--m-text)] leading-relaxed">
                  {post.content}
                </p>
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            <p className="text-[var(--m-text-dim)] text-sm">No replies yet.</p>
          )}
        </div>

        {user ? (
          <ReplyForm threadId={threadId} />
        ) : (
          <p className="text-sm text-[var(--m-text-muted)]">
            <Link href="/login" className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] hover:underline">
              Log in
            </Link>{' '}
            to reply.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  )
}

FILE: app\community\[channel]\[threadId]\ReplyForm.tsx

// FILE: app/community/[channel]/[threadId]/ReplyForm.tsx  (reply form component)

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function ReplyForm({ threadId }: { threadId: string }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [content, setContent] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!content.trim()) return
    setLoading(true)

    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
    if (!user) {
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }

    const { error } = await supabase.from('posts').insert({
      thread_id: threadId,
      user_id: user.id,
      content: content.trim(),
    })

    if (!error) {
      setContent('')
      router.refresh()
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
      <textarea
        value={content}
        onChange={(e) => setContent(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="Write a reply..."
        rows={4}
        className="w-full bg-[var(--m-surface)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg px-4 py-3 focus:border-[var(--m-border-bright)] outline-none resize-none text-sm"
      />
      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={loading}
        className="bg-white text-black font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg px-6 py-2.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
      >
        {loading ? 'Posting...' : 'Reply'}
      </button>
    </form>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function NewThreadForm({ channelId }: { channelId: string }) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!title.trim()) return
    setLoading(true)

    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
    if (!user) {
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }

    const { error } = await supabase.from('threads').insert({
      channel_id: channelId,
      user_id: user.id,
      title: title.trim(),
    })

    if (!error) {
      setTitle('')
      router.refresh()
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex gap-2">
      <input
        type="text"
        value={title}
        onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="Start a new thread..."
        className="flex-1 bg-[var(--m-surface)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 focus:border-[var(--m-border-bright)] outline-none text-sm"
      />
      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={loading}
        className="bg-white text-black text-sm font-semibold rounded-lg px-5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
      >
        {loading ? '...' : 'Post'}
      </button>
    </form>
  )
}

                  by {thread.profiles?.full_name || thread.profiles?.username || 'someone'} Â·{' '}

          )}
        </div>

================================================================
FILE: app\courses\[slug]\CourseView.tsx
================================================================

'use client'
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'

export type Lesson = {
  id: string
  title: string
  order_index: number
  is_final_exam: boolean | null
  pass_threshold: number | null
}

export type Mission = {
  id: string
  title: string
  description: string | null
  order_index: number
  lessons: Lesson[]
}

export type Saga = {
  id: string
  title: string
  description: string | null
  order_index: number
  missions: Mission[]
}

type State = 'cleared' | 'active' | 'next' | 'hidden'
type Current = { si: number; mi: number; li: number }

type Props = {
  sagas: Saga[]
  slug: string
  completedIds: string[]
  enrolled: boolean
  defaultOpenIndex: number
  isAdmin?: boolean
}

function computeSagaState(si: number, c: Current, isAdmin: boolean): State {
  if (isAdmin) return 'cleared'
  if (c.si === -1) return 'cleared'
  if (si < c.si) return 'cleared'
  if (si === c.si) return 'active'
  if (si === c.si + 1) return 'next'
  return 'hidden'
}

function computeMissionState(si: number, mi: number, c: Current, isAdmin: boolean): State {
  if (isAdmin) return 'cleared'
  if (si < c.si) return 'cleared'
  if (si > c.si) return 'hidden'
  if (mi < c.mi) return 'cleared'
  if (mi === c.mi) return 'active'
  if (mi === c.mi + 1) return 'next'
  return 'hidden'
}

function computeLessonState(si: number, mi: number, li: number, c: Current, isAdmin: boolean): State {
  if (isAdmin) return 'cleared'
  if (si < c.si) return 'cleared'
  if (si > c.si) return 'hidden'
  if (mi < c.mi) return 'cleared'
  if (mi > c.mi) return 'hidden'
  if (li < c.li) return 'cleared'
  if (li === c.li) return 'active'
  if (li === c.li + 1) return 'next'
  return 'hidden'
}

function sagaHasLessons(saga: Saga): boolean {
  return (saga.missions ?? []).some((m) => (m.lessons ?? []).length > 0)
}

export default function CourseView({
  sagas, slug, completedIds, enrolled, defaultOpenIndex, isAdmin = false,
}: Props) {
  const completedSet = useMemo(() => new Set(completedIds), [completedIds])

  const builtSagas = useMemo(
    () => (isAdmin ? sagas : sagas.filter(sagaHasLessons)),
    [sagas, isAdmin]
  )

  const current = useMemo<Current>(() => {
    for (let si = 0; si < builtSagas.length; si++) {
      const missions = builtSagas[si].missions ?? []
      for (let mi = 0; mi < missions.length; mi++) {
        const lessons = missions[mi].lessons ?? []
        for (let li = 0; li < lessons.length; li++) {
          if (!completedSet.has(lessons[li].id)) return { si, mi, li }
        }
      }
    }
    return { si: -1, mi: -1, li: -1 }
  }, [builtSagas, completedSet])

  const initialTab = isAdmin
    ? Math.min(defaultOpenIndex, Math.max(0, builtSagas.length - 1))
    : Math.max(0, current.si >= 0 ? current.si : builtSagas.length - 1)
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(initialTab)

  const [openMissionKey, setOpenMissionKey] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAdmin) return
    if (current.si >= 0) setActiveTab(current.si)
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [current.si, isAdmin])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAdmin) {
      setOpenMissionKey(null)
      return
    }
    const s = builtSagas[activeTab]
    if (s?.missions?.[current.mi]) {
      setOpenMissionKey(s.missions[current.mi].id)
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [activeTab, isAdmin, current.mi])

  if (builtSagas.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="m-hud-panel p-12 text-center">
        <span className="m-hud-scan" />
        <div className="text-base text-[#e5e9f0] mb-2">Curriculum coming soon.</div>
        <div className="text-sm text-[#8892a6]">This course is being prepared.</div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const activeSaga = builtSagas[activeTab]

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 md:space-y-6">
      {/* â•â•â• MISSION SELECTOR (top level = Missions) â•â•â• */}
      <div
        className="relative rounded-xl overflow-hidden"
        style={{
          border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.24)',
          background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,20,28,0.85) 0%, rgba(10,14,20,0.96) 100%)',
        }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 border-b"
          style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.14)' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.35em] text-[#22d3ee]">
              MISSION SELECTOR
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#3a4250]">
            {String(activeTab + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / {String(builtSagas.length).padStart(2, '0')}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-1.5 p-1.5">
          {builtSagas.map((saga, si) => {
            const total = saga.missions.reduce((sum, m) => sum + (m.lessons?.length ?? 0), 0)
            const done = saga.missions.reduce(
              (sum, m) => sum + (m.lessons ?? []).filter(l => completedSet.has(l.id)).length,
              0
            )
            const cleared = total > 0 && done === total
            const isSelected = si === activeTab
            const state = computeSagaState(si, current, isAdmin)
            const isLocked = state === 'hidden' || (state === 'next' && !isAdmin)

            return (
              <button
                key={saga.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (isLocked) return
                  setActiveTab(si)
                }}
                className={
                  'group relative text-left rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 transition-all ' +
                  (isSelected ? '' : isLocked ? 'cursor-not-allowed' : 'cursor-pointer hover:bg-white/[0.03]')
                }
                style={{
                  border: isSelected
                    ? '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.55)'
                    : '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.10)',
                  background: isSelected
                    ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.14) 0%, rgba(139,92,246,0.05) 100%)'
                    : 'rgba(4,8,14,0.55)',
                  boxShadow: isSelected
                    ? '0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.18), inset 0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.03)'
                    : 'none',
                  opacity: isLocked && !isSelected ? 0.45 : 1,
                }}
              >
                {isSelected && (
                  <span className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 h-px"
                    style={{
                      background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, #22d3ee 50%, transparent 100%)',
                      opacity: 0.85,
                    }}
                  />
                )}

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1.5">
                  <span
                    className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.28em] tabular-nums shrink-0"
                    style={{
                      color: isSelected ? '#22d3ee' : cleared ? '#10b981' : '#566074',
                      textShadow: isSelected ? '0 0 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.5)' : 'none',
                    }}
                  >
                    MISSION {String(si + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
                  </span>
                  {cleared && !isSelected && (
                    <span className="text-[10px]" style={{ color: '#10b981' }}>âœ“</span>
                  )}
                  {isLocked && !isSelected && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-[#3a4250]">ðŸ”’</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div
                  className="text-[11px] md:text-xs font-bold tracking-tight leading-snug mb-1.5 line-clamp-2"
                  style={{
                    color: isSelected ? '#ffffff' : isLocked ? '#3a4250' : '#e5e9f0',
                    minHeight: '2.6em',
                  }}
                >
                  {saga.title}
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="flex-1 h-[2px] rounded-full overflow-hidden"
                    style={{ background: 'rgba(30,38,51,0.9)' }}>
                    <div
                      className="h-full transition-all"
                      style={{
                        width: (total > 0 ? (done / total) * 100 : 0) + '%',
                        background: cleared
                          ? '#10b981'
                          : 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <span
                    className="text-[9px] font-mono tabular-nums shrink-0"
                    style={{ color: isSelected ? '#22d3ee' : '#566074' }}
                  >
                    {String(done).padStart(2, '0')}/{String(total).padStart(2, '0')}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* â•â•â• ACTIVE MISSION CONTENT â•â•â• */}
      {activeSaga && (
        <SagaChapter
          key={activeSaga.id}
          saga={activeSaga}
          slug={slug}
          index={activeTab}
          state={computeSagaState(activeTab, current, isAdmin)}
          isOpen={true}
          onToggle={() => {}}
          completedSet={completedSet}
          enrolled={enrolled}
          current={current}
          openMissionKey={openMissionKey}
          setOpenMissionKey={setOpenMissionKey}
          isAdmin={isAdmin}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function SagaChapter({
  saga, slug, index, state,
  completedSet, enrolled, current, openMissionKey, setOpenMissionKey, isAdmin,
}: {
  saga: Saga
  slug: string
  index: number
  state: State
  isOpen: boolean
  onToggle: () => void
  completedSet: Set<string>
  enrolled: boolean
  current: Current
  openMissionKey: string | null
  setOpenMissionKey: (k: string | null) => void
  isAdmin: boolean
}) {
  const isCleared = state === 'cleared'
  const isActive = state === 'active'
  const accent = isCleared ? '#10b981' : '#22d3ee'

  const total = saga.missions.reduce((sum, m) => sum + (m.lessons?.length ?? 0), 0)
  const done = saga.missions.reduce(
    (sum, m) => sum + (m.lessons ?? []).filter(l => completedSet.has(l.id)).length,
    0
  )
  const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((done / total) * 100) : 0

  return (
    <div
      className={`m-hud-panel relative overflow-hidden ${isActive ? 'm-active-pulse' : ''}`}
      style={{
        borderColor: accent + '66',
      }}
    >
      <span className="m-hud-scan" />
      <div className="px-4 md:px-6 py-4 md:py-5 flex items-start gap-4 md:gap-5">
        <div className="shrink-0 pt-0.5">
          {isCleared ? (
            <div
              className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-md"
              style={{
                width: 34, height: 34,
                border: '1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.5)',
                background: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.08)',
                color: '#10b981',
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: 800,
              }}
            >
              âœ“
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div
              className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-md"
              style={{
                width: 34, height: 34,
                border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.55)',
                background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)',
                boxShadow: '0 0 16px rgba(34,211,238,0.25)',
              }}
            >
              <span
                className="inline-block"
                style={{
                  width: 9, height: 9,
                  background: '#22d3ee',
                  transform: 'rotate(45deg)',
                  boxShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.75)',
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-1.5 flex-wrap">
            <span
              className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.35em]"
              style={{ color: accent }}
            >
              MISSION {String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
            </span>
            {isCleared && (
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#10b981]">
                Â· CLEARED
              </span>
            )}
            {isActive && (
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                Â· <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" /> ACTIVE
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="text-lg md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-2" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
            {saga.title}
          </div>

          {saga.description && (
            <div className="text-xs md:text-sm leading-relaxed mb-3 text-[#8892a6]">
              {saga.description}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4">
            <div
              className="flex-1 h-[3px] rounded-full overflow-hidden"
              style={{ background: 'rgba(30,38,51,0.85)' }}
            >
              <div
                className="h-full transition-all duration-500"
                style={{
                  width: pct + '%',
                  background: pct === 100
                    ? '#10b981'
                    : 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
                  boxShadow: pct === 100
                    ? '0 0 8px rgba(16,185,129,0.5)'
                    : '0 0 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.5)',
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <span
              className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] tabular-nums shrink-0 w-14 text-right"
              style={{ color: isCleared ? '#10b981' : '#566074' }}
            >
              {String(done).padStart(2, '0')}/{String(total).padStart(2, '0')}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.10)' }}>
        <div className="py-3 md:py-4">
          {saga.missions.map((mission, mi) => {
            const mState = computeMissionState(index, mi, current, isAdmin)
            if (mState === 'hidden') return null
            return (
              <MissionBlock
                key={mission.id}
                mission={mission}
                slug={slug}
                sagaIndex={index}
                missionIndex={mi}
                state={mState}
                completedSet={completedSet}
                enrolled={enrolled}
                isOpen={isAdmin ? true : openMissionKey === mission.id && mState !== 'next'}
                onToggle={() => {
                  if (isAdmin) return
                  if (mState === 'next') return
                  setOpenMissionKey(openMissionKey === mission.id ? null : mission.id)
                }}
                current={current}
                isAdmin={isAdmin}
              />
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function MissionBlock({
  mission, slug, sagaIndex, missionIndex, state,
  completedSet, enrolled, isOpen, onToggle, current, isAdmin,
}: {
  mission: Mission
  slug: string
  sagaIndex: number
  missionIndex: number
  state: State
  completedSet: Set<string>
  enrolled: boolean
  isOpen: boolean
  onToggle: () => void
  current: Current
  isAdmin: boolean
}) {
  if (state === 'next') {
    return (
      <div
        className="mx-3 md:mx-5 mb-3 rounded-lg px-4 md:px-5 py-3.5 flex items-center gap-3 m-locked-scan"
        style={{
          border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.10)',
          background: 'rgba(4,8,14,0.5)',
          opacity: 0.65,
        }}
      >
        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] shrink-0 opacity-60">
          S{String(missionIndex + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
        </span>
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <span className="m-locked-bar" style={{ width: '45%', height: 12, display: 'inline-block' }}>
            &nbsp;
          </span>
        </div>
        <span className="text-[10px] text-[#22d3ee] shrink-0 opacity-60">ðŸ”’</span>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const isCleared = state === 'cleared'
  const isActive = state === 'active'
  const accent = isCleared ? '#10b981' : '#22d3ee'

  const lessons = mission.lessons ?? []
  const done = lessons.filter(l => completedSet.has(l.id)).length
  const total = lessons.length
  const cleared = total > 0 && done === total

  return (
    <div
      className="mx-3 md:mx-5 mb-3 rounded-lg overflow-hidden"
      style={{
        border: isActive
          ? '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.28)'
          : '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.10)',
        background: 'rgba(4,8,14,0.55)',
        boxShadow: isActive
          ? '0 0 24px rgba(34,211,238,0.06), inset 0 0 30px rgba(34,211,238,0.015)'
          : 'none',
      }}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onToggle}
        className="w-full text-left px-4 md:px-5 py-3.5 flex items-center gap-3 hover:bg-white/[0.015] transition"
      >
        <span
          className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] shrink-0 tabular-nums"
          style={{ color: accent, textShadow: isActive ? '0 0 6px ' + accent + '66' : 'none' }}
        >
          S{String(missionIndex + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
        </span>
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="text-sm md:text-base font-bold truncate" style={{ color: '#e5e9f0' }}>
            {mission.title}
          </div>
          {mission.description && (
            <div className="text-[11px] md:text-xs text-[#8892a6] mt-0.5 truncate">
              {mission.description}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <span
          className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] tabular-nums shrink-0"
          style={{ color: cleared ? '#10b981' : '#566074' }}
        >
          {cleared ? 'âœ“ ' : ''}{String(done).padStart(2, '0')}/{String(total).padStart(2, '0')}
        </span>
        <span
          className="text-xs shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200"
          style={{
            color: isOpen ? accent : '#3a4250',
            transform: isOpen ? 'rotate(90deg)' : 'rotate(0deg)',
          }}
        >
          â–¸
        </span>
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="border-t" style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)' }}>
          {isActive && (
            <SagaBriefing
              mission={mission}
              missionIndex={missionIndex}
              done={done}
              total={total}
            />
          )}

          <ul className="px-3 md:px-5 pt-3 pb-4">
            {lessons.map((lesson, li) => {
              const lState = computeLessonState(sagaIndex, missionIndex, li, current, isAdmin)
              if (lState === 'hidden') return null
              return (
                <LessonRow
                  key={lesson.id}
                  lesson={lesson}
                  slug={slug}
                  index={li}
                  state={lState}
                  isFirst={li === 0}
                  isLast={li === lessons.length - 1}
                  enrolled={enrolled}
                  isAdmin={isAdmin}
                />
              )
            })}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function SagaBriefing({
  mission, missionIndex, done, total,
}: {
  mission: Mission
  missionIndex: number
  done: number
  total: number
}) {
  const pct = total > 0 ? (done / total) * 100 : 0

  return (
    <div
      className="mx-3 md:mx-5 mt-3 rounded-lg relative overflow-hidden"
      style={{
        border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.28)',
        background:
          'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.06) 0%, rgba(139,92,246,0.03) 55%, rgba(6,10,16,0.6) 100%)',
      }}
    >
      <span
        className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 h-px"
        style={{
          background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, #22d3ee 50%, transparent 100%)',
          opacity: 0.7,
        }}
      />
      <div className="px-5 py-4 md:py-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee]">
            SAGA BRIEFING / S{String(missionIndex + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold tracking-tight mb-2" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
          {mission.title}
        </div>

        {mission.description && (
          <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-[#8892a6] leading-relaxed mb-4 max-w-2xl">
            {mission.description}
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] shrink-0">
            PROGRESS
          </span>
          <div className="flex-1 h-[3px] rounded-full overflow-hidden" style={{ background: 'rgba(30,38,51,0.9)' }}>
            <div
              className="h-full transition-all duration-500"
              style={{
                width: pct + '%',
                background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
                boxShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.6)',
              }}
            />
          </div>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums text-[#22d3ee] shrink-0">
            {String(done).padStart(2, '0')} / {String(total).padStart(2, '0')}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function LessonRow({
  lesson, slug, index, state, isFirst, isLast, enrolled, isAdmin,
}: {
  lesson: Lesson
  slug: string
  index: number
  state: State
  isFirst: boolean
  isLast: boolean
  enrolled: boolean
  isAdmin: boolean
}) {
  const isCleared = state === 'cleared'
  const isActive = state === 'active'
  const isNext = state === 'next'

  const statusLabel = lesson.is_final_exam
    ? 'FINAL'
    : 'SQ' + String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')

  if (isNext) {
    return (
      <li className="flex items-stretch opacity-55">
        <div className="w-6 md:w-7 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center">
          <div className="w-px flex-1" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.15)' }} />
          <div
            className="shrink-0 rounded-full"
            style={{
              width: 10, height: 10,
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.35)',
              background: 'transparent',
              boxShadow: '0 0 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.2)',
            }}
          />
          <div className="w-px flex-1" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.15)' }} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4 px-3 md:px-4 py-2.5 m-locked-scan">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#22d3ee] shrink-0 w-11 tabular-nums opacity-60">
            {statusLabel}
          </span>
          <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <span className="m-locked-bar" style={{ width: '55%', height: 12, display: 'inline-block' }}>
              &nbsp;
            </span>
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-[#22d3ee] shrink-0 opacity-60">ðŸ”’</span>
        </div>
      </li>
    )
  }

  const href = '/courses/' + slug + '/lessons/' + lesson.id
  const locked = !enrolled && !isAdmin

  return (
    <li className="flex items-stretch">
      <div className="w-6 md:w-7 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center">
        <div
          className="w-px h-2.5"
          style={{
            background: isFirst
              ? 'transparent'
              : isCleared
              ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.30)'
              : 'rgba(30,38,51,0.85)',
          }}
        />
        {isCleared ? (
          <div
            className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full shrink-0"
            style={{
              width: 14, height: 14,
              background: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.14)',
              border: '1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.55)',
            }}
          >
            <span style={{ color: '#10b981', fontSize: 8, lineHeight: 1, fontWeight: 800 }}>âœ“</span>
          </div>
        ) : isActive ? (
          <div
            className="shrink-0"
            style={{
              width: 14, height: 14,
              background: '#22d3ee',
              borderRadius: 2,
              transform: 'rotate(45deg)',
              boxShadow: '0 0 12px rgba(34,211,238,0.55)',
            }}
          />
        ) : (
          <div
            className="shrink-0 rounded-full"
            style={{
              width: 12, height: 12,
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.45)',
              background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)',
            }}
          />
        )}
        <div
          className="w-px flex-1 min-h-[10px]"
          style={{
            background: isLast
              ? 'transparent'
              : isCleared
              ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.30)'
              : 'rgba(30,38,51,0.85)',
          }}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pb-2.5">
        {locked ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4 px-3 md:px-4 py-2.5 opacity-50">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#3a4250] shrink-0 w-11 tabular-nums">
              {statusLabel}
            </span>
            <span className="text-sm flex-1 min-w-0 truncate text-[#3a4250]">
              {lesson.title}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#3a4250] shrink-0 hidden sm:block">
              ENROLL TO UNLOCK
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Link
            href={href}
            prefetch={false}
            className={
              'group relative flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4 px-3 md:px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-colors ' +
              (isActive ? '' : 'hover:bg-white/[0.025]')
            }
            style={
              isActive
                ? {
                    border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.45)',
                    background:
                      'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.10) 0%, rgba(34,211,238,0.01) 100%)',
                    boxShadow:
                      '0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.14), inset 0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.03)',
                  }
                : { border: '1px solid transparent' }
            }
          >
            <span
              className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] shrink-0 w-11 tabular-nums transition-colors"
              style={{
                color: isActive ? '#22d3ee' : isCleared ? '#10b981' : '#8892a6',
                opacity: isCleared ? 0.75 : 1,
              }}
            >
              {statusLabel}
            </span>

            <span
              className="text-sm flex-1 min-w-0 truncate transition-colors"
              style={{
                color: isActive ? '#ffffff' : isCleared ? '#8892a6' : '#e5e9f0',
                fontWeight: isActive ? 600 : 500,
              }}
            >
              {lesson.title}
            </span>

            <span
              className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest shrink-0 hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1.5 tabular-nums transition-colors"
              style={{ color: isActive ? '#22d3ee' : '#566074' }}
            >
              {isCleared && <span style={{ color: '#10b981', opacity: 0.85 }}>âœ“</span>}
              {lesson.pass_threshold != null && lesson.pass_threshold + '%'}
            </span>

            <span
              className="text-xs shrink-0 transition-all group-hover:translate-x-0.5"
              style={{
                color: isActive ? '#22d3ee' : '#3a4250',
                opacity: isActive ? 1 : 0.6,
              }}
            >
              â–¸
            </span>
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>
    </li>
  )
}

'use client'
import { Fragment, type ReactNode } from 'react'

function repair(raw: string): string {
  let s = raw
  s = s.replace(/\|\s+\|/g, '|\n|')
  s = s.replace(/([^\n])\s+(\*\s+\*[A-Za-z])/g, '$1\n$2')
  s = s.replace(/([^\n])\s+(#{1,3}\s)/g, '$1\n$2')
  s = s.replace(/([^\n])\s+(>\s)/g, '$1\n$2')
  s = s.replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n')
  return s
}

/** Strip everything from "# ðŸŽ‰ ..." onward â€” the "Side Quest Complete" banner. */
function stripCompleteBanner(raw: string): string {
  const idx = raw.search(/^#\s*ðŸŽ‰/m)
  return idx >= 0 ? raw.slice(0, idx).trimEnd() : raw
}

function inline(text: string, keyBase: string): ReactNode[] {
  const out: ReactNode[] = []
  const regex = /(\*\*[^*]+\*\*|\*[^*\n]+\*|`[^`]+`)/g
  text.split(regex).forEach((part, idx) => {
    if (!part) return
    const k = `${keyBase}-${idx}`
    if (part.startsWith('**') && part.endsWith('**') && part.length > 4) {
      const word = part.slice(2, -2)
      const isKeyword = /^[A-Z][A-Z\s\-]{0,22}$/.test(word) && word.length <= 24
      if (isKeyword) {
        out.push(
          <span key={k} className="font-bold tracking-[0.04em]"
            style={{ color: '#a5f3fc', textShadow: '0 0 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.25)' }}>
            {word}
          </span>
        )
      } else {
        out.push(
          <strong key={k} style={{ color: '#ffffff', fontWeight: 700 }}>{word}</strong>
        )
      }
    }
    else if (part.startsWith('*') && part.endsWith('*') && part.length > 2)
      out.push(<em key={k} style={{ color: '#a5f3fc', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>{part.slice(1, -1)}</em>)
    else if (part.startsWith('`') && part.endsWith('`'))
      out.push(
        <code key={k} className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[0.9em] font-mono"
          style={{ background: 'var(--m-surface-2)', color: '#a5f3fc' }}>
          {part.slice(1, -1)}
        </code>
      )
    else out.push(<span key={k}>{part}</span>)
  })
  return out
}

function isTableRow(line: string) {
  const t = line.trim()
  return t.startsWith('|') && t.endsWith('|') && t.length > 2
}
function isTableSeparator(line: string) {
  return /^\|[\s:|-]+\|$/.test(line.trim()) && line.includes('-')
}
function splitRow(line: string): string[] {
  return line.trim().replace(/^\|/, '').replace(/\|$/, '').split('|').map(c => c.trim())
}

const ARROW_RE = /^[â†“â†‘â†’â†â¬‡â¬†â†³]+$/
const ARROW_SPLIT_RE = /^(.*?)\s*â†’\s*(.*)$/
const EQ_SPLIT_RE = /^([^=\n]+?)\s*=\s*([^=\n]+?)$/
const EMDASH_PAIR_RE = /^(.*?)\s+â€”\s+(.+)$/
const NUMBERED_RE = /^(\d+)\.\s+(.*)$/
const BULLET_PREFIX_RE = /^[-*]\s+/
const H2_SPLIT_RE = /^(.*?)(\d+)\.?\s+(.*)$/
const CIRCLED_RE = /^([â‘ â‘¡â‘¢â‘£â‘¤â‘¥â‘¦â‘§â‘¨â‘©])\s*(.*)$/

const CIRCLE_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
  'â‘ ': '01', 'â‘¡': '02', 'â‘¢': '03', 'â‘£': '04', 'â‘¤': '05',
  'â‘¥': '06', 'â‘¦': '07', 'â‘§': '08', 'â‘¨': '09', 'â‘©': '10',
}

function stripBulletPrefix(line: string): string {
  if (BULLET_PREFIX_RE.test(line)) return line.replace(BULLET_PREFIX_RE, '').trim()
  return line
}

/** âœï¸ Write-This-Down special callout */
function WriteDownBlock({ text, k }: { text: string; k: string }) {
  return (
    <div
      className="my-6 md:my-8 relative rounded-xl p-5 md:p-6 overflow-hidden"
      style={{
        background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(245,158,11,0.10) 0%, rgba(34,211,238,0.04) 100%)',
        border: '1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.35)',
        boxShadow: '0 0 30px rgba(245,158,11,0.08), inset 0 0 30px rgba(245,158,11,0.03)',
      }}
    >
      <span className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-[3px]"
        style={{ background: '#f59e0b', boxShadow: '0 0 14px rgba(245,158,11,0.6)' }} />
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
        <span className="text-base leading-none">âœï¸</span>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.35em] font-bold"
          style={{ color: '#f59e0b', textShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(245,158,11,0.5)' }}>
          WRITE THIS DOWN
        </span>
      </div>
      <p className="text-[15px] md:text-base leading-relaxed font-semibold"
        style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
        {inline(text, k)}
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

function VerticalSequence({ pairs, k }: { pairs: { value: string; label: string }[]; k: string }) {
  return (
    <div
      className="my-6 md:my-8 rounded-xl p-5 md:p-7 relative"
      style={{
        background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(15,20,28,0.65) 0%, rgba(22,32,44,0.5) 100%)',
        border: '1px solid var(--m-border)',
        borderLeft: '2px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.6)',
      }}
    >
      {pairs.map((p, i) => (
        <Fragment key={i}>
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-baseline sm:gap-4 gap-0.5">
            <span className="text-lg md:text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
              {inline(p.value, `${k}-${i}-v`)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.28em] uppercase"
              style={{ color: '#a5f3fc', opacity: 0.75 }}>
              {inline(p.label, `${k}-${i}-l`)}
            </span>
          </div>
          {i < pairs.length - 1 && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2" aria-hidden>
              <span className="w-[1px] h-5" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.35)' }} />
              <span className="text-[10px] leading-none" style={{ color: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.55)' }}>â–¼</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </Fragment>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function Pairs({ items, k }: { items: { left: string; right: string }[]; k: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="my-6 md:my-8 rounded-xl overflow-hidden"
      style={{ border: '1px solid var(--m-border)', background: 'var(--m-surface)' }}>
      {items.map((it, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="grid grid-cols-2"
          style={{ borderTop: i === 0 ? 'none' : '1px solid var(--m-border)' }}>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 md:px-5 md:py-3.5" style={{ borderRight: '1px solid var(--m-border)' }}>
            <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold tracking-tight" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
              {inline(it.left, `${k}-${i}-l`)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="px-4 py-3 md:px-5 md:py-3.5">
            <div className="text-base md:text-lg tracking-tight font-semibold"
              style={{ color: '#a5f3fc' }}>
              {inline(it.right, `${k}-${i}-r`)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function ChainBlock({ parts, k, compact = false }: { parts: string[]; k: string; compact?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className={compact ? 'my-3 md:my-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1.5' : 'my-5 md:my-7 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1.5'}>
      {parts.map((p, i) => (
        <Fragment key={i}>
          <span
            className={compact ? 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold tracking-tight' : 'px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md text-sm md:text-base font-semibold tracking-tight'}
            style={{
              background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.06)',
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.22)',
              color: '#ffffff',
            }}
          >
            {inline(p, `${k}-${i}`)}
          </span>
          {i < parts.length - 1 && (
            <span className="text-sm leading-none" style={{ color: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.55)' }}>â†’</span>
          )}
        </Fragment>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function CompactLabel({ text, k }: { text: string; k: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="my-6 flex items-center gap-3">
      <span className="inline-block w-1 h-4 rounded-full shrink-0"
        style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.6)' }} />
      <span className="text-xs md:text-sm font-mono tracking-[0.25em] uppercase"
        style={{ color: '#a5f3fc' }}>
        {inline(text, k)}
      </span>
      <span className="flex-1 h-px" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.25) 0%, transparent 100%)' }} />
    </div>
  )
}

function DataTable({ headers, rows, k }: { headers: string[]; rows: string[][]; k: string }) {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="hidden md:block my-8 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl"
        style={{ border: '1px solid var(--m-border)', background: 'var(--m-surface)' }}>
        <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[420px]">
          <thead>
            <tr style={{ background: 'var(--m-surface-2)' }}>
              {headers.map((h, hi) => (
                <th key={hi} className="text-left px-4 py-3.5 font-mono text-[11px] tracking-[0.15em] uppercase"
                  style={{ color: '#a5f3fc', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--m-border)' }}>
                  {inline(h, `th-${k}-${hi}`)}
                </th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {rows.map((row, ri) => (
              <tr key={ri} style={{ borderTop: ri === 0 ? 'none' : '1px solid var(--m-border)' }}>
                {row.map((cell, ci) => (
                  <td key={ci} className="px-4 py-3 align-top" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
                    {inline(cell, `td-${k}-${ri}-${ci}`)}
                  </td>
                ))}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div className="md:hidden my-6 space-y-2">
        {rows.map((row, ri) => (
          <div key={ri} className="relative rounded-xl p-4"
            style={{
              border: '1px solid var(--m-border)',
              background: 'var(--m-surface)',
              borderLeft: '2px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.5)',
            }}>
            <span className="absolute top-3 right-3 text-[9px] font-mono tracking-widest" style={{ color: 'var(--m-text-dim)' }}>
              {String(ri + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
            </span>
            <div className="space-y-2.5 pr-8">
              {row.map((cell, ci) => (
                <div key={ci}>
                  <div className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.22em] uppercase mb-1"
                    style={{ color: '#a5f3fc', opacity: 0.75 }}>
                    {headers[ci] ?? ''}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[15px] leading-snug font-medium" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
                    {inline(cell, `mtd-${k}-${ri}-${ci}`)}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

function BulletItem({ text, k }: { text: string; k: string }) {
  const m = text.match(ARROW_SPLIT_RE)
  if (m && m[1].length > 0 && m[1].length < 70 && m[2].length > 0 && m[2].length < 90) {
    return (
      <div
        className="rounded-lg px-4 py-4 md:py-5 flex flex-col items-center text-center"
        style={{
          background: 'rgba(15,20,28,0.55)',
          border: '1px solid var(--m-border)',
          borderLeft: '2px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.6)',
        }}
      >
        <div className="text-[15px] md:text-base font-semibold tracking-wide leading-snug" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
          {inline(m[1], `${k}-a`)}
        </div>
        <div className="my-2 text-base leading-none" style={{ color: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.7)' }}>â†“</div>
        <div className="text-[15px] md:text-base leading-snug" style={{ color: '#a5f3fc' }}>
          {inline(m[2], `${k}-b`)}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }
  return (
    <p className="text-[15px] md:text-base leading-relaxed py-1.5" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
      {inline(text, k)}
    </p>
  )
}

function tryVerticalSequence(lines: string[], start: number): { pairs: { value: string; label: string }[]; end: number } | null {
  const pairs: { value: string; label: string }[] = []
  let i = start
  while (i + 2 < lines.length) {
    const v = lines[i].trim()
    const arrow = lines[i + 1].trim()
    const label = lines[i + 2].trim()
    if (!v || !label) break
    if (!ARROW_RE.test(arrow)) break
    if (v.startsWith('#') || v.startsWith('>') || /^[-*] /.test(v)) break
    if (isTableRow(v)) break
    if (v.length > 40 || label.length > 30) break
    pairs.push({ value: v, label })
    i += 3
    while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim() === '') i++
  }
  if (pairs.length < 2) return null
  return { pairs, end: i }
}

function tryEqualsGroup(lines: string[], start: number): { items: { left: string; right: string }[]; end: number } | null {
  const items: { left: string; right: string }[] = []
  let i = start
  while (i < lines.length) {
    const raw = lines[i].trim()
    if (raw === '') { i++; continue }
    const line = stripBulletPrefix(raw)
    const m = line.match(EQ_SPLIT_RE)
    if (!m) break
    const left = m[1].trim()
    const right = m[2].trim()
    if (!left || !right) break
    if (left.length > 40 || right.length > 40) break
    items.push({ left, right })
    i++
    while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim() === '') i++
  }
  if (items.length < 2) return null
  return { items, end: i }
}

function tryEmDashPairs(lines: string[], start: number): { items: { left: string; right: string }[]; end: number } | null {
  const items: { left: string; right: string }[] = []
  let i = start
  while (i < lines.length) {
    const raw = lines[i].trim()
    if (raw === '') { i++; continue }
    const line = stripBulletPrefix(raw)
    const m = line.match(EMDASH_PAIR_RE)
    if (!m) break
    const left = m[1].trim()
    const right = m[2].trim()
    if (!left || !right) break
    if (left.length > 60 || right.length > 60) break
    if (left.includes('â†’') || right.includes('â†’')) break
    items.push({ left, right })
    i++
    while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim() === '') i++
  }
  if (items.length < 2) return null
  return { items, end: i }
}

function tryNumberedList(lines: string[], start: number): { items: string[]; end: number } | null {
  const items: string[] = []
  let i = start
  while (i < lines.length) {
    const line = lines[i].trim()
    if (line === '') { i++; continue }
    const m = line.match(NUMBERED_RE)
    if (!m) break
    items.push(m[2].trim())
    i++
    while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim() === '') i++
  }
  if (items.length < 2) return null
  return { items, end: i }
}

function SectionHeading({ text, k }: { text: string; k: string }) {
  const m = text.match(H2_SPLIT_RE)
  const prefix = m ? m[1].trim() : ''
  const number = m ? m[2] : ''
  const titleText = m ? m[3] : text

  return (
    <div className="mt-12 md:mt-16 mb-6 md:mb-7">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4">
        {prefix && (
          <span
            className="shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md"
            style={{
              width: 36,
              height: 36,
              background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.06)',
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.3)',
              fontSize: 18,
              lineHeight: 1,
            }}
          >
            {prefix}
          </span>
        )}

        {number && (
          <span
            className="shrink-0 font-mono tabular-nums"
            style={{
              color: '#a5f3fc',
              fontSize: 11,
              letterSpacing: '0.15em',
              fontWeight: 700,
              opacity: 0.9,
            }}
          >
            {number.padStart(2, '0')}
          </span>
        )}

        <span className="w-px h-4 shrink-0" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.3)' }} />

        <span
          className="text-lg sm:text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight"
          style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}
        >
          {inline(titleText, 'h2t-' + k)}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        <span className="w-6 h-px shrink-0" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.55)' }} />
        <span className="flex-1 h-px"
          style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.22) 0%, rgba(139,92,246,0.12) 55%, transparent 100%)' }} />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function StepHeading({ num, text, k }: { num: string; text: string; k: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="my-5 md:my-6 flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4">
      <div
        className="shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center font-mono tabular-nums"
        style={{
          width: 30,
          height: 30,
          background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.06)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.35)',
          borderRadius: 6,
          color: '#a5f3fc',
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: 700,
          letterSpacing: '0.06em',
        }}
      >
        {num}
      </div>
      <span
        className="text-sm md:text-base font-bold tracking-[0.14em] uppercase"
        style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}
      >
        {inline(text, `step-${k}`)}
      </span>
      <span
        className="h-px flex-1"
        style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.28) 0%, transparent 100%)' }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

export default function CodexContent({ content }: { content: string }) {
  // Strip the ðŸŽ‰ side-quest-complete banner from all lessons
  const trimmedContent = stripCompleteBanner(content)
  const cleaned = repair(trimmedContent)
  const lines = cleaned.split('\n')
  const blocks: ReactNode[] = []
  let i = 0
  let key = 0

  while (i < lines.length) {
    const line = lines[i]
    const trimmed = line.trim()

    if (trimmed === '') { i++; continue }

    if (isTableRow(line) && i + 1 < lines.length && isTableSeparator(lines[i + 1])) {
      const headers = splitRow(line)
      i += 2
      const rows: string[][] = []
      while (i < lines.length && isTableRow(lines[i])) {
        const cells = splitRow(lines[i])
        if (cells.some(c => c.length > 0)) rows.push(cells)
        i++
      }
      blocks.push(<DataTable key={key++} headers={headers} rows={rows} k={String(key)} />)
      continue
    }

    if (trimmed.startsWith('### ')) {
      blocks.push(
        <h3 key={key++} className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold tracking-wide mt-10 md:mt-12 mb-3"
          style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
          {inline(trimmed.slice(4), `h3-${key}`)}
        </h3>
      )
      i++
      continue
    }
    if (trimmed.startsWith('## ')) {
      blocks.push(<SectionHeading key={key++} text={trimmed.slice(3)} k={String(key)} />)
      i++
      continue
    }
    if (trimmed.startsWith('# ')) {
      blocks.push(
        <h1 key={key++}
          className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight mt-4 md:mt-6 mb-5 md:mb-6"
          style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
          {inline(trimmed.slice(2), `h1-${key}`)}
        </h1>
      )
      i++
      continue
    }

    if (trimmed === '---') {
      blocks.push(
        <div key={key++} className="my-10 md:my-12 flex items-center gap-3" aria-hidden>
          <span className="flex-1 h-px" style={{ background: 'var(--m-border)' }} />
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.6)' }} />
          <span className="flex-1 h-px" style={{ background: 'var(--m-border)' }} />
        </div>
      )
      i++
      continue
    }

    if (trimmed.startsWith('> ')) {
      const quoted: string[] = []
      while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim().startsWith('> ')) {
        quoted.push(lines[i].trim().slice(2))
        i++
      }

      const single = quoted.length === 1 ? quoted[0] : ''

      // âœï¸ WRITE THIS DOWN callout
      if (quoted.length === 1 && /^âœï¸/.test(single)) {
        const body = single.replace(/^âœï¸\s*(WRITE THIS DOWN[:\-â€”]?\s*)?/i, '').trim()
        blocks.push(<WriteDownBlock key={key++} text={body} k={`wd-${key}`} />)
        continue
      }

      const hasStructure = /[â†’â†â†“â†‘=]/.test(single)
      const isCompact = quoted.length === 1 && single.length > 0 && single.length <= 55 && !hasStructure

      if (quoted.length === 1 && single.includes('â†’') && single.length <= 90) {
        const chainParts = single.split('â†’').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
        if (chainParts.length >= 2 && chainParts.length <= 6 && chainParts.every(p => p.length <= 30)) {
          blocks.push(<ChainBlock key={key++} parts={chainParts} k={`ch-${key}`} />)
          continue
        }
      }

      if (isCompact) {
        blocks.push(<CompactLabel key={key++} text={single} k={`cl-${key}`} />)
        continue
      }

      blocks.push(
        <blockquote key={key++}
          className="my-6 md:my-8 relative rounded-xl px-5 md:px-6 py-4 md:py-5 overflow-hidden"
          style={{
            background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.07) 0%, rgba(59,130,246,0.02) 100%)',
            border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.22)',
          }}>
          <span className="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-[2px]"
            style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.55)' }} />
          {quoted.map((q, qi) => (
            <p key={qi} className="text-[15px] md:text-base leading-relaxed" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
              {inline(q, `q-${key}-${qi}`)}
            </p>
          ))}
        </blockquote>
      )
      continue
    }

    const vs = tryVerticalSequence(lines, i)
    if (vs) {
      blocks.push(<VerticalSequence key={key++} pairs={vs.pairs} k={`vs-${key}`} />)
      i = vs.end
      continue
    }

    const eq = tryEqualsGroup(lines, i)
    if (eq) {
      blocks.push(<Pairs key={key++} items={eq.items} k={`eq-${key}`} />)
      i = eq.end
      continue
    }

    const ed = tryEmDashPairs(lines, i)
    if (ed) {
      blocks.push(<Pairs key={key++} items={ed.items} k={`ed-${key}`} />)
      i = ed.end
      continue
    }

    const nl = tryNumberedList(lines, i)
    if (nl) {
      blocks.push(
        <ol key={key++} className="my-5 md:my-6 space-y-2.5 md:space-y-3">
          {nl.items.map((it, ii) => (
            <li key={ii} className="flex items-start gap-3 md:gap-4">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-mono tracking-widest shrink-0 pt-[3px] w-6 tabular-nums"
                style={{ color: '#a5f3fc', opacity: 0.9 }}>
                {String(ii + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[15px] md:text-base leading-relaxed flex-1" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
                {inline(it, `nl-${key}-${ii}`)}
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      )
      i = nl.end
      continue
    }

    if (/^[-*] /.test(trimmed)) {
      const items: string[] = []
      while (i < lines.length && /^[-*] /.test(lines[i].trim()) && !isTableRow(lines[i])) {
        items.push(lines[i].trim().slice(2))
        i++
      }
      blocks.push(
        <div key={key++} className="my-5 md:my-6 relative pl-6 md:pl-8 space-y-2"
          style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid var(--m-border-bright)' }}>
          {items.map((it, ii) => (
            <div key={ii} className="relative">
              <span className="absolute -left-[27px] md:-left-[35px] top-[18px] w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full"
                style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.75)' }} />
              <BulletItem text={it} k={`li-${key}-${ii}`} />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )
      continue
    }

    if (ARROW_RE.test(trimmed)) {
      blocks.push(
        <div key={key++} className="flex my-3 pl-4">
          <span className="text-xl leading-none" style={{ color: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.5)' }}>â†“</span>
        </div>
      )
      i++
      continue
    }

    const circM = trimmed.match(CIRCLED_RE)
    if (circM) {
      const num = CIRCLE_MAP[circM[1]] ?? circM[1]
      const label = circM[2].replace(/\*\*/g, '').replace(/\*/g, '').trim()
      if (label.length > 0 && label.length <= 40) {
        blocks.push(<StepHeading key={key++} num={num} text={label} k={String(key)} />)
        i++
        continue
      }
    }

    const chainParts = trimmed.split('â†’').map(s => s.trim())
    const isCompact2 = chainParts.length === 2 &&
      chainParts[0].length > 0 && chainParts[0].length <= 25 &&
      chainParts[1].length > 0 && chainParts[1].length <= 25
    const isBigChain = chainParts.length >= 3 &&
      chainParts.length <= 5 &&
      chainParts.every(p => p.length > 0 && p.length < 40)
    if (isBigChain || isCompact2) {
      blocks.push(<ChainBlock key={key++} parts={chainParts} k={`ch-${key}`} compact={isCompact2} />)
      i++
      continue
    }

    const para: string[] = [trimmed]
    i++
    while (
      i < lines.length &&
      lines[i].trim() !== '' &&
      !lines[i].trim().startsWith('#') &&
      !lines[i].trim().startsWith('>') &&
      !/^[-*] /.test(lines[i].trim()) &&
      lines[i].trim() !== '---' &&
      !isTableRow(lines[i]) &&
      !ARROW_RE.test(lines[i].trim()) &&
      !CIRCLED_RE.test(lines[i].trim())
    ) {
      para.push(lines[i].trim())
      i++
    }
    blocks.push(
      <p key={key++} className="my-4 md:my-5 text-[15px] md:text-base leading-relaxed" style={{ color: '#ffffff' }}>
        {inline(para.join(' '), `p-${key}`)}
      </p>
    )
  }

  return <div className="max-w-[68ch]">{blocks}</div>
}
