// FILE: app/courses/[slug]/lessons/[lessonId]/CompleteButton.tsx  (quest completion)

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function CompleteButton({
  lessonId,
  completed: initial,
}: {
  lessonId: string
  completed: boolean
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [completed, setCompleted] = useState(initial)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  async function handleComplete() {
    if (completed || loading) return
    setLoading(true)

    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
    if (!user) {
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }

    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('lesson_progress')
      .upsert(
        {
          user_id: user.id,
          lesson_id: lessonId,
          completed: true,
          completed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
        { onConflict: 'user_id,lesson_id' }
      )

    if (!error) {
      setCompleted(true)
      router.refresh()
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  if (completed) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border border-green-800/50 bg-green-950/30 rounded-lg px-5 py-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-green-400 font-semibold">
          <span className="text-xl">âœ“</span>
          <span>Quest Complete</span>
        </div>
        <div className="text-sm text-green-400 font-mono">+50 XP</div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleComplete}
      disabled={loading}
      className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-lg py-3.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
    >
      {loading ? 'Completing...' : 'âœ“ Complete Side Quest'}
    </button>
  )
}

FILE: app\courses\[slug]\lessons\[lessonId]\page.tsx

// FILE: app/courses/[slug]/lessons/[lessonId]/page.tsx
import { redirect, notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import { getCurrentUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import AppShell from '@/components/AppShell'
import QuestPlayer from './QuestPlayer'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
export const revalidate = 0

export default async function LessonPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ slug: string; lessonId: string }>
}) {
  const { slug, lessonId } = await params
  const user = await getCurrentUser()
  if (!user) redirect('/login')

  const supabase = await createClient()

  const { data: course } = await supabase
    .from('courses')
    .select('id, slug, title')
    .eq('slug', slug)
    .maybeSingle()
  if (!course) notFound()

  const { data: lesson } = await supabase
    .from('lessons')
    .select(
      'id, title, order_index, video_url, content, is_final_exam, pass_threshold, mission_id, mission:missions(id, order_index, module_id, module:modules(id, order_index))'
    )
    .eq('id', lessonId)
    .maybeSingle()
  if (!lesson) notFound()

  const mission = (lesson as any).mission as
    | { id: string; order_index: number; module_id: string; module: { id: string; order_index: number } | null }
    | null

  let nextHref: string | null = null
  let nextTitle: string | null = null

  if (mission) {
    const { data: siblings } = await supabase
      .from('lessons')
      .select('id, title')
      .eq('mission_id', mission.id)
      .gt('order_index', lesson.order_index)
      .order('order_index', { ascending: true })
      .limit(1)

    if (siblings && siblings.length > 0) {
      nextHref = `/courses/${slug}/lessons/${siblings[0].id}`
      nextTitle = siblings[0].title
    } else {
      const { data: nextMissions } = await supabase
        .from('missions')
        .select('id')
        .eq('module_id', mission.module_id)
        .gt('order_index', mission.order_index)
        .order('order_index', { ascending: true })
        .limit(1)

      if (nextMissions && nextMissions.length > 0) {
        const { data: firstLesson } = await supabase
          .from('lessons')
          .select('id, title')
          .eq('mission_id', nextMissions[0].id)
          .order('order_index', { ascending: true })
          .limit(1)

        if (firstLesson && firstLesson.length > 0) {
          nextHref = `/courses/${slug}/lessons/${firstLesson[0].id}`
          nextTitle = firstLesson[0].title
        }
      } else {
        const currentSagaOrder = mission.module?.order_index ?? 0
        const { data: nextSagas } = await supabase
          .from('modules')
          .select('id')
          .eq('course_id', course.id)
          .gt('order_index', currentSagaOrder)
          .order('order_index', { ascending: true })
          .limit(1)

        if (nextSagas && nextSagas.length > 0) {
          const { data: nextMissions2 } = await supabase
            .from('missions')
            .select('id')
            .eq('module_id', nextSagas[0].id)
            .order('order_index', { ascending: true })
            .limit(1)

          if (nextMissions2 && nextMissions2.length > 0) {
            const { data: firstLesson2 } = await supabase
              .from('lessons')
              .select('id, title')
              .eq('mission_id', nextMissions2[0].id)
              .order('order_index', { ascending: true })
              .limit(1)

            if (firstLesson2 && firstLesson2.length > 0) {
              nextHref = `/courses/${slug}/lessons/${firstLesson2[0].id}`
              nextTitle = firstLesson2[0].title
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const { data: progress } = await supabase
    .from('lesson_progress')
    .select('completed')
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .eq('lesson_id', lessonId)
    .maybeSingle()

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user.email ?? undefined}>
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto pb-12 md:pb-20">
        <QuestPlayer
          lessonId={lesson.id}
          lessonTitle={lesson.title}
          lessonIndex={lesson.order_index}
          videoUrl={lesson.video_url}
          content={lesson.content}
          isFinalExam={lesson.is_final_exam ?? false}
          passThreshold={lesson.pass_threshold ?? 80}
          initialCompleted={progress?.completed ?? false}
          slug={slug}
          nextHref={nextHref}
          nextTitle={nextTitle}
        />
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  )
}

================================================================
FILE: app\courses\[slug]\lessons\[lessonId]\QuestPlayer.tsx

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import CodexContent from './CodexContent'
import DisciplineScreen from '@/components/DisciplineScreen'
import { triggerNav } from '@/components/NavLoader'
import { playPassSound, playFailSound } from '@/lib/sounds'

type Question = {
  id: string
  question: string
  options: string[]
  order_index: number
}

type ReviewItem = {
  id: string
  user_index: number | null
  correct_index: number
  order_index: number
}

type QuizResult = {
  score: number
  passed: boolean
  correct: number
  total: number
  best_score?: number
  previous_best?: number
  is_new_best?: boolean
  attempts?: number
  xp_base?: number
  xp_bonus?: number
  xp_total?: number
  review?: ReviewItem[]
}

type Phase = 'briefing' | 'mission' | 'exam' | 'result'

function scrollMainToTop(smooth = false) {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return
  const main = document.querySelector('main')
  if (main) {
    main.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: smooth ? 'smooth' : 'auto' })
  } else {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: smooth ? 'smooth' : 'auto' })
  }
}

export default function QuestPlayer({
  lessonId,
  lessonTitle,
  lessonIndex,
  videoUrl,
  content,
  isFinalExam,
  passThreshold,
  initialCompleted,
  slug,
  nextHref,
  nextTitle,
}: {
  lessonId: string
  lessonTitle: string
  lessonIndex: number
  videoUrl: string | null
  content: string | null
  isFinalExam: boolean
  passThreshold: number
  initialCompleted: boolean
  slug: string
  nextHref: string | null
  nextTitle: string | null
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()

  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>(initialCompleted ? 'mission' : 'briefing')
  const [cameFromResult, setCameFromResult] = useState(false)
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<Question[] | null>(null)
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, number>>({})
  const [examIndex, setExamIndex] = useState(0)
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [result, setResult] = useState<QuizResult | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [completed, setCompleted] = useState(initialCompleted)
  const [showDiscipline, setShowDiscipline] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (questions !== null) return
    async function load() {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('get_quiz_questions', {
        p_lesson_id: lessonId,
      })
      if (error) { setError(error.message); setQuestions([]); return }
      setQuestions((data as Question[]) || [])
    }
    load()
  }, [lessonId, questions, supabase])

  useEffect(() => {
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => scrollMainToTop(false))
    })
  }, [phase])

  function scrollTop() { scrollMainToTop(true) }
  function startMission() { setPhase('mission'); scrollTop() }
  function finishMission() {
    setPhase('exam'); setAnswers({}); setResult(null); setError(null)
    setExamIndex(0); setCameFromResult(false); scrollTop()
  }
  function retry() {
    setPhase('exam'); setAnswers({}); setResult(null); setError(null)
    setExamIndex(0); setCameFromResult(false); scrollTop()
  }
  function backToCodexFromResult() {
    setPhase('mission')
    setCameFromResult(true)
    setError(null)
    requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => scrollMainToTop(false)))
  }
  function backToResult() {
    setPhase('result')
    setCameFromResult(false)
    requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => scrollMainToTop(false)))
  }

  /** Hard navigate â€” bypasses router cache so the course page re-fetches fresh data */
  function goNext(href: string) {
    triggerNav()
    setTimeout(() => {
      window.location.href = href
    }, 300)
  }

  async function submit() {
    setSubmitting(true); setError(null)
    const payload = questions && questions.length > 0 ? answers : {}
    const { data, error: rpcError } = await supabase.rpc('submit_quiz', {
      p_lesson_id: lessonId, p_answers: payload,
    })
    if (rpcError) { setError(rpcError.message); setSubmitting(false); return }

    let parsed: unknown = data
    if (typeof parsed === 'string') { try { parsed = JSON.parse(parsed) } catch { parsed = null } }
    let r: QuizResult | null = null
    if (Array.isArray(parsed)) r = (parsed[0] as QuizResult) ?? null
    else if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && 'passed' in (parsed as object)) r = parsed as QuizResult

    if (!r || typeof r.passed !== 'boolean') {
      setError('Exam result was malformed. Please retry.'); setSubmitting(false); return
    }

    if (r.passed) {
      playPassSound()
      setCompleted(true)
      setShowDiscipline(true)
      // Refresh server component so `completedIds` updates on the course page
      router.refresh()
    } else {
      playFailSound()
    }

    setResult(r)
    setPhase('result')
    setSubmitting(false)
    requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => scrollMainToTop(false)))
  }

  const allAnswered =
    questions !== null && questions.length > 0 &&
    questions.every(q => answers[q.id] !== undefined)

  const unansweredCount =
    questions && questions.length > 0
      ? questions.length - Object.keys(answers).length
      : 0

  /* BRIEFING */
  if (phase === 'briefing') {
    return (
      <div className="m-hud-panel">
        <span className="m-hud-scan" />
        <div className="m-hud-header">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
            MISSION BRIEF
            <span style={{ color: '#3a4250' }}>/ {isFinalExam ? 'FINAL' : 'SQ ' + String(lessonIndex).padStart(2, '0')}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
            STANDBY
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="p-6 sm:p-8 md:p-10">
        <div className="m-label m-label-cyan mb-3">
          {isFinalExam ? 'Final Exam' : 'Side Quest ' + String(lessonIndex).padStart(2, '0')}
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-8">{lessonTitle}</h2>
        <div className="border-t border-[var(--m-border)] mb-6" />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-5 mb-10">
          <div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-1">BRIEFING</div>
            <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--m-text)]">{videoUrl ? 'READY' : 'TEXT ONLY'}</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-1">EXAM</div>
            <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--m-text)]">
              {questions === null ? '...' : questions.length === 0 ? 'NONE' : questions.length + ' Q'}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-1">PASS GATE</div>
            <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--m-cyan)]">{passThreshold}%</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="m-label mb-4">Objectives</div>
        <ul className="space-y-2 mb-10 text-sm">
          <li className="flex gap-3 text-[var(--m-text)]"><span className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] shrink-0">â–¸</span><span>Study the Quest Briefing</span></li>
          <li className="flex gap-3 text-[var(--m-text)]"><span className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] shrink-0">â–¸</span><span>Read the Quest Codex</span></li>
          <li className="flex gap-3 text-[var(--m-text)]"><span className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] shrink-0">â–¸</span><span>Score {passThreshold}% or higher on the exam</span></li>
          <li className="flex gap-3 text-[var(--m-text)]"><span className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] shrink-0">â–¸</span><span>Unlock the next quest â€” +50 XP</span></li>
        </ul>
        <button onClick={startMission} className="m-pulse w-full md:w-auto bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-sm rounded-lg px-10 py-4 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition">
          â–¶ START MISSION
        </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  /* MISSION */
  if (phase === 'mission') {
    const showReview = cameFromResult && result?.review && questions

    if (showReview) {
      return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="m-panel relative overflow-hidden">
            <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-4 h-4 z-10" style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
            <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 w-4 h-4 z-10" style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
            <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-4 h-4 z-10" style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
            <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-4 h-4 z-10" style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
            <span className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 h-px z-10" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, #22d3ee 50%, transparent 100%)', opacity: 0.75 }} />
            <div className="px-5 md:px-7 py-4 md:py-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 flex-wrap">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                  <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: '#22d3ee', textShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.55)' }}>
                    {isFinalExam ? 'FINAL EXAM' : 'SIDE QUEST ' + String(lessonIndex).padStart(2, '0')}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-base md:text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[var(--m-text)] truncate">
                  {lessonTitle}
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={backToResult}
                className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#22d3ee] hover:text-white transition border border-[rgba(34,211,238,0.4)] rounded-lg px-4 py-2 shrink-0">
                â† BACK TO RESULT
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="text-center py-2">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.4em] text-[var(--m-cyan)]">
              <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[var(--m-cyan)] animate-pulse" />
              EXAM REVIEW Â· LEARN FROM YOUR ANSWERS
              <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[var(--m-cyan)] animate-pulse" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {questions.map((q, qi) => {
            const rv = result.review!.find(r => r.id === q.id)
            if (!rv) return null
            const userIdx = rv.user_index
            const correctIdx = rv.correct_index
            const isCorrect = userIdx === correctIdx
            return (
              <div key={q.id} className="m-panel relative p-4 sm:p-6"
                style={{ borderColor: isCorrect ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.4)' : 'rgba(239,68,68,0.4)' }}>
                <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-3 h-3"
                  style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid ' + (isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444'), borderLeft: '1.5px solid ' + (isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444') }} />
                <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 w-3 h-3"
                  style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid ' + (isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444'), borderRight: '1.5px solid ' + (isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444') }} />
                <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-3 h-3"
                  style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid ' + (isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444'), borderLeft: '1.5px solid ' + (isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444') }} />
                <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3 h-3"
                  style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid ' + (isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444'), borderRight: '1.5px solid ' + (isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444') }} />

                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)]">
                      Q{String(qi + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-lg" style={{ color: isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444' }}>
                      {isCorrect ? 'âœ“' : 'âœ—'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest"
                    style={{ color: isCorrect ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444' }}>
                    {isCorrect ? 'CORRECT' : 'INCORRECT'}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="text-sm md:text-base font-semibold mb-4 text-[var(--m-text)]">
                  {q.question}
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-1.5">
                  {q.options.map((opt, oi) => {
                    const isUser = oi === userIdx
                    const isAnswer = oi === correctIdx
                    let border = 'var(--m-border)'
                    let bg = 'transparent'
                    let color = 'var(--m-text-muted)'
                    let marker = ''
                    if (isAnswer) { border = 'rgba(16,185,129,0.6)'; bg = 'rgba(16,185,129,0.08)'; color = '#10b981'; marker = 'âœ“ correct' }
                    else if (isUser && !isCorrect) { border = 'rgba(239,68,68,0.6)'; bg = 'rgba(239,68,68,0.08)'; color = '#ef4444'; marker = 'âœ— your answer' }
                    return (
                      <div key={oi} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs md:text-sm rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5"
                        style={{ border: '1px solid ' + border, background: bg, color }}>
                        <span className="font-mono shrink-0 w-5">{String.fromCharCode(65 + oi)}</span>
                        <span className="flex-1">{opt}</span>
                        {marker && <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-wider shrink-0">{marker}</span>}
                      </div>
                    )
                  })}
                </div>

                {!isCorrect && (
                  <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t text-xs md:text-sm" style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.20)' }}>
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] font-mono tracking-widest shrink-0">ðŸ’¡ TIP</span>
                      <span className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)]">
                        Correct answer is <span style={{ color: '#10b981' }}>{String.fromCharCode(65 + correctIdx)}</span>. Re-read the question â€” the pattern matters.
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}

          <button onClick={backToResult}
            className="w-full border border-[var(--m-cyan)]/40 text-[var(--m-cyan)] font-bold tracking-widest text-sm rounded-lg px-8 py-4 hover:bg-[var(--m-cyan-soft)] transition">
            â† BACK TO RESULT
          </button>
        </div>
      )
    }

    return (
      <div className="space-y-8 md:space-y-10">
        <div className="m-panel relative overflow-hidden">
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-4 h-4 z-10" style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 w-4 h-4 z-10" style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-4 h-4 z-10" style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-4 h-4 z-10" style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 h-px z-10" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, #22d3ee 50%, transparent 100%)', opacity: 0.75 }} />

          <div className="px-5 md:px-7 py-4 md:py-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 flex-wrap">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em]" style={{ color: '#22d3ee', textShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.55)' }}>
                  {isFinalExam ? 'FINAL EXAM' : 'SIDE QUEST ' + String(lessonIndex).padStart(2, '0')}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-base md:text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[var(--m-text)] truncate">
                {lessonTitle}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[var(--m-cyan)] shrink-0">
              <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--m-cyan)] animate-pulse" />
              IN PROGRESS
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {videoUrl && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4 md:mb-5">
              <span className="inline-block w-1 h-5 rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ background: '#22d3ee', boxShadow: '0 0 12px rgba(34,211,238,0.7)' }} />
              <span className="text-xs md:text-sm font-mono tracking-[0.3em] uppercase" style={{ color: '#22d3ee' }}>
                QUEST BRIEFING
              </span>
              <span className="flex-1 h-px" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.4) 0%, transparent 100%)' }} />
            </div>
            <div className="w-full aspect-video rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-black border border-[var(--m-border)]">
              {videoUrl.includes('youtube') || videoUrl.includes('youtu.be') ? (
                <iframe src={'https://www.youtube.com/embed/' + extractYouTubeId(videoUrl)} title={lessonTitle}
                  allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                  allowFullScreen className="w-full h-full" />
              ) : (<video src={videoUrl} controls className="w-full h-full" />)}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {content && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4 md:mb-5">
              <span className="inline-block w-1 h-5 rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ background: '#22d3ee', boxShadow: '0 0 12px rgba(34,211,238,0.7)' }} />
              <span className="text-xs md:text-sm font-mono tracking-[0.3em] uppercase" style={{ color: '#22d3ee' }}>
                {isFinalExam ? 'EXAM INSTRUCTIONS' : 'QUEST CODEX'}
              </span>
              <span className="flex-1 h-px" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.4) 0%, transparent 100%)' }} />
            </div>
            <div className="m-hud-panel">
              <span className="m-hud-scan" />
              <div className="m-hud-header">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
                  CODEX
                  <span style={{ color: '#3a4250' }}>/ {isFinalExam ? 'EXAM BRIEF' : 'LESSON BODY'}</span>
                </div>
                <span style={{ color: '#3a4250', opacity: 0.7 }}>MERIDIAN / v1</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-5 sm:p-6 md:p-8"><CodexContent content={content} /></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {completed ? (
          <div className="m-panel border-green-800/50 bg-green-950/20 p-5 md:p-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-green-400 font-semibold">
              <span className="text-2xl">âœ“</span>
              <span className="tracking-wider">MISSION COMPLETE</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={finishMission}
                className="text-sm text-green-400/70 hover:text-green-400 underline text-center px-4 py-2">
                Retake exam
              </button>
              {nextHref && (
                <button onClick={() => goNext(nextHref)}
                  className="bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-xs rounded-lg px-6 py-3 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition text-center">
                  NEXT: {(nextTitle?.toUpperCase() || 'CONTINUE')} â†’
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button onClick={finishMission}
            className="m-hud-corners w-full bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-sm rounded-lg px-10 py-4 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition">
            âœ“ FINISH MISSION â€” TAKE EXAM â†’
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    )
  }

  /* EXAM */
  if (phase === 'exam') {
    if (questions === null) {
      return (
        <div className="m-panel m-hud-corners p-12 text-center text-[var(--m-text-dim)] font-mono text-sm">
          LOADING EXAM...
        </div>
      )
    }
    if (questions.length === 0) {
      return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="m-panel m-hud-corners p-6 md:p-8">
            <div className="m-label mb-2">{isFinalExam ? 'Final Exam' : 'Practice Exam'}</div>
            <p className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)] mb-6">No questions yet. The instructor is preparing this exam.</p>
            <button onClick={submit} disabled={submitting}
              className="bg-white text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-sm rounded-lg px-8 py-3 hover:bg-neutral-200">
              {submitting ? 'COMPLETING...' : 'âœ“ COMPLETE MISSION'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )
    }

    const answeredCount = Object.keys(answers).length
    const progressPct = Math.round((answeredCount / questions.length) * 100)
    const q = questions[examIndex]
    const isLast = examIndex === questions.length - 1
    const isFirst = examIndex === 0
    const selectedForQ = answers[q.id]

    return (
      <div className="space-y-3 md:space-y-6">
        <div className="m-panel relative overflow-hidden">
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-3 h-3 z-10" style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 w-3 h-3 z-10" style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-3 h-3 z-10" style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3 h-3 z-10" style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />

          <div className="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 border-b" style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.15)' }}>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2 md:mb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[var(--m-cyan)]">
                <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[var(--m-cyan)] animate-pulse" />
                {isFinalExam ? 'FINAL EXAM' : 'PRACTICE EXAM'}
              </div>
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[var(--m-text-dim)]">
                Q{String(examIndex + 1).padStart(2, '0')}/{String(questions.length).padStart(2, '0')}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="h-[3px] rounded-full overflow-hidden" style={{ background: '#1e2633' }}>
              <div className="h-full transition-all duration-300"
                style={{ width: progressPct + '%', background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)', boxShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.6)' }} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-2 text-[9px] md:text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)]">
              <span>{answeredCount}/{questions.length} ANSWERED</span>
              <span>PASS {passThreshold}%</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="px-4 md:px-8 py-5 md:py-8">
            <div className="text-base md:text-xl font-bold mb-5 md:mb-6 leading-relaxed text-[var(--m-text)]">
              {q.question}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {q.options.map((opt, idx) => {
                const selected = selectedForQ === idx
                return (
                  <button key={idx} type="button"
                    onClick={() => setAnswers(a => ({ ...a, [q.id]: idx }))}
                    className={'w-full text-left text-sm md:text-base rounded-lg px-3 md:px-4 py-3 md:py-3.5 border transition-all flex items-center gap-3 ' + (selected ? 'border-[var(--m-cyan)] bg-[rgba(34,211,238,0.10)] text-[var(--m-text)]' : 'border-[var(--m-border)] hover:border-[var(--m-border-bright)] text-[var(--m-text-muted)] hover:bg-white/[0.02]')}
                    style={{ boxShadow: selected ? '0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.20)' : 'none' }}>
                    <span className={'shrink-0 w-6 h-6 md:w-7 md:h-7 rounded flex items-center justify-center font-mono text-[10px] md:text-xs border ' + (selected ? 'bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black border-[var(--m-cyan)]' : 'border-[var(--m-border)] text-[var(--m-text-dim)]')}>
                      {String.fromCharCode(65 + idx)}
                    </span>
                    <span className="flex-1">{opt}</span>
                    {selected && <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-wider text-[var(--m-cyan)] shrink-0 hidden sm:inline">âœ“</span>}
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {error && <div className="text-red-400 text-sm font-mono">ERROR: {error}</div>}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 md:gap-3">
          <button onClick={() => { setExamIndex(i => Math.max(0, i - 1)); scrollTop() }}
            disabled={isFirst}
            className="flex-1 border border-[var(--m-border)] text-[var(--m-text-muted)] font-bold tracking-widest text-xs md:text-sm rounded-lg px-3 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 transition hover:border-[var(--m-border-bright)] disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
            â† PREV
          </button>
          {isLast ? (
            <button onClick={submit} disabled={submitting}
              className={'flex-[2] font-bold tracking-widest text-xs md:text-sm rounded-lg px-3 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 transition ' + (submitting ? 'bg-[var(--m-surface)] text-[var(--m-text-dim)] border border-[var(--m-border)] cursor-not-allowed' : allAnswered ? 'bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black hover:bg-cyan-300 m-pulse' : 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 hover:bg-amber-500/30')}>
              {submitting ? 'ANALYZING...' : allAnswered ? 'â–¸ SUBMIT' : 'â–¸ SUBMIT (' + unansweredCount + ')'}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button onClick={() => { setExamIndex(i => Math.min(questions.length - 1, i + 1)); scrollTop() }}
              className="flex-[2] bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-xs md:text-sm rounded-lg px-3 md:px-6 py-3 md:py-4 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition">
              NEXT â†’
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  /* RESULT */
  if (phase === 'result' && result) {
    const passed = result.passed
    const isNewBest = !!result.is_new_best
    const previousBest = result.previous_best ?? 0
    const bestScore = result.best_score ?? result.score
    const attempts = result.attempts ?? 1
    const xpTotal = result.xp_total ?? 0
    const xpBase = result.xp_base ?? 0
    const xpBonus = result.xp_bonus ?? 0

    return (
      <>
        {showDiscipline && <DisciplineScreen onDone={() => {
          setShowDiscipline(false)
          requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => scrollMainToTop(false)))
        }} />}
        <div className={'m-panel m-hud-corners p-8 md:p-14 text-center relative overflow-hidden ' + (passed ? 'border-green-700/50' : 'border-red-800/50')}>
          {isNewBest && passed && (
            <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md border"
              style={{ color: '#22d3ee', borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.5)', background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)' }}>
              âš¡ NEW HIGHSCORE
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="text-5xl md:text-6xl mb-4">{passed ? 'ðŸ†' : 'âœ—'}</div>
          <div className={'text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] mb-3 ' + (passed ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400')}>
            {passed ? 'MISSION STATUS: CLEARED' : 'MISSION STATUS: FAILED'}
          </div>
          <h2 className={'text-2xl md:text-5xl font-bold tracking-tight mb-8 md:mb-10 ' + (passed ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400')}>
            {passed ? 'MISSION COMPLETE' : 'MISSION FAILED'}
          </h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 md:gap-4 max-w-3xl mx-auto mb-8 md:mb-10">
            <div className="border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg p-3 md:p-4">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-1">SCORE</div>
              <div className={'text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums ' + (passed ? 'text-green-400' : 'text-red-400')}>{result.score}%</div>
            </div>
            <div className="border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg p-3 md:p-4">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-1">CORRECT</div>
              <div className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums text-[var(--m-text)]">{result.correct}/{result.total}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg p-3 md:p-4">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-1">BEST</div>
              <div className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums" style={{ color: '#22d3ee' }}>{bestScore}%</div>
            </div>
            <div className="border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg p-3 md:p-4">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-1">ATTEMPTS</div>
              <div className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums text-[var(--m-text)]">{attempts}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          {passed && (
            <div className="max-w-md mx-auto mb-8 md:mb-10 border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg p-4 text-left">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mb-3">XP BREAKDOWN</div>
              {xpBase > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                  <span className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)]">First clear</span>
                  <span className="text-green-400 font-mono">+{xpBase} XP</span>
                </div>
              )}
              {xpBonus > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                  <span className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)]">New highscore bonus</span>
                  <span className="font-mono" style={{ color: '#22d3ee' }}>+{xpBonus} XP</span>
                </div>
              )}
              {xpTotal === 0 && <div className="text-sm text-[var(--m-text-dim)]">No new XP this attempt â€” already cleared.</div>}
              {xpTotal > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-[var(--m-border)] font-bold">
                  <span className="text-[var(--m-text)]">Total</span>
                  <span className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] font-mono">+{xpTotal} XP</span>
                </div>
              )}
              {previousBest > 0 && isNewBest && (
                <div className="text-xs mt-3" style={{ color: '#22d3ee' }}>Beat your previous best of {previousBest}%</div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
          {passed ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row flex-wrap gap-3 justify-center">
              <button onClick={backToCodexFromResult}
                className="text-sm text-[var(--m-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg px-6 py-3 transition">
                REVIEW QUESTIONS
              </button>
              <button onClick={retry}
                className="text-sm text-[var(--m-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg px-6 py-3 transition">
                REPLAY FOR HIGHSCORE
              </button>
              {nextHref ? (
                <button onClick={() => goNext(nextHref)}
                  className="bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-sm rounded-lg px-8 py-3 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition text-center">
                  NEXT: {(nextTitle?.toUpperCase() || 'CONTINUE')} â†’
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button onClick={() => goNext('/courses/' + slug)}
                  className="bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-sm rounded-lg px-8 py-3 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition text-center">
                  RETURN TO COURSE â†’
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <p className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)] mb-8 md:mb-10 max-w-md mx-auto">
                You need {passThreshold}% to clear this mission. Review your answers and try again.
              </p>
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row flex-wrap gap-3 justify-center">
                <button onClick={backToCodexFromResult}
                  className="text-sm text-[var(--m-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg px-6 py-3 transition">
                  REVIEW QUESTIONS
                </button>
                <button onClick={retry}
                  className="bg-red-500 text-white font-bold tracking-widest text-sm rounded-lg px-8 py-3 hover:bg-red-400 transition m-pulse">
                  RETRY MISSION
                </button>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </>
    )
  }

  return null
}

function extractYouTubeId(url: string): string | null {
  const match = url.match(
    /(?:youtube\.com\/(?:watch\?v=|embed\/|shorts\/)|youtu\.be\/)([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{11})/
  )
  return match ? match[1] : null
}

================================================================
FILE: app\courses\[slug]\lessons\[lessonId]\QuizSection.tsx
================================================================

// FILE: app/courses/[slug]/lessons/[lessonId]/QuizSection.tsx  (exam + progress gate)

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

type Question = {
  id: string
  question: string
  options: string[]
  order_index: number
}

type QuizResult = {
  score: number
  passed: boolean
  correct: number
  total: number
}

export default function QuizSection({
  lessonId,
  initialCompleted,
}: {
  lessonId: string
  initialCompleted: boolean
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()

  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<Question[] | null>(null)
  const [completed, setCompleted] = useState(initialCompleted)
  const [started, setStarted] = useState(false)
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, number>>({})
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [result, setResult] = useState<QuizResult | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('get_quiz_questions', {
        p_lesson_id: lessonId,
      })
      if (error) {
        setError(error.message)
        setQuestions([])
        return
      }
      setQuestions((data as Question[]) || [])
    }
    load()
  }, [lessonId])

  async function submit(finalAnswers: Record<string, number>) {
    setSubmitting(true)
    setError(null)

    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('submit_quiz', {
      p_lesson_id: lessonId,
      p_answers: finalAnswers,
    })

    if (error) {
      setError(error.message)
      setSubmitting(false)
      return
    }

    const r = (data as QuizResult[])[0]
    setResult(r)
    if (r.passed) {
      setCompleted(true)
      router.refresh()
    }
    setSubmitting(false)
  }

  // Completed
  if (completed) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border border-green-800/50 bg-green-950/30 rounded-lg px-5 py-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-green-400 font-semibold">
          <span className="text-xl">âœ“</span>
          <span>Quest Complete</span>
        </div>
        <div className="text-sm text-green-400 font-mono">
          {result ? `${result.score}% Â· ` : ''}+50 XP
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // Loading
  if (questions === null) {
    return (
      <div className="m-panel p-6 text-center text-[var(--m-text-dim)] text-sm">
        Loading practice exam...
      </div>
    )
  }

  // No quiz â€” one-click complete
  if (questions.length === 0) {
    return (
      <>
        {error && (
          <div className="mb-3 text-red-400 text-sm">Error: {error}</div>
        )}
        <button
          onClick={() => submit({})}
          disabled={submitting}
          className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-lg py-3.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
        >
          {submitting ? 'Completing...' : 'âœ“ Complete Side Quest'}
        </button>
      </>
    )
  }

  // Failed state
  if (result && !result.passed) {
    return (
      <div className="m-panel p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 m-label">
          <span>ðŸ§ </span> Practice Exam
        </div>
        <div className="text-red-400 font-semibold mb-1">
          âœ— Not passed â€” {result.correct} / {result.total} ({result.score}%)
        </div>
        <p className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)] text-sm mb-5">
          You need 80% to unlock the next quest. Review the Codex and try again.
        </p>
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            setResult(null)
            setAnswers({})
            setStarted(true)
          }}
          className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-lg py-3.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 transition"
        >
          Retake Exam â†’
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // Intro state
  if (!started) {
    return (
      <div className="m-panel p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 m-label">
          <span>ðŸ§ </span> Practice Exam
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold mb-2">
          {questions.length} question{questions.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}
        </h3>
        <p className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)] text-sm mb-5 leading-relaxed">
          You need <span className="text-[var(--m-cyan)] font-semibold">80%</span>{' '}
          or higher to pass and unlock the next quest.
        </p>
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            setStarted(true)
            setResult(null)
            setAnswers({})
          }}
          className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-lg py-3.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 transition"
        >
          Start Exam â†’
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  // Taking exam
  const allAnswered = questions.every((q) => answers[q.id] !== undefined)

  return (
    <div className="m-panel p-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 m-label">
          <span>ðŸ§ </span> Practice Exam
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-[var(--m-text-dim)] font-mono">
          {Object.keys(answers).length} / {questions.length} answered
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-6 mb-6">
        {questions.map((q, i) => (
          <div key={q.id}>
            <div className="text-sm font-medium mb-3 leading-relaxed">
              <span className="text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mr-2 font-mono">
                Q{i + 1}.
              </span>
              {q.question}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {q.options.map((opt, idx) => {
                const selected = answers[q.id] === idx
                return (
                  <button
                    key={idx}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setAnswers((a) => ({ ...a, [q.id]: idx }))
                    }
                    className={`w-full text-left text-sm rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 border transition ${
                      selected
                        ? 'border-cyan-500/60 bg-cyan-500/[0.08] text-[var(--m-text)]'
                        : 'border-[var(--m-border)] hover:border-[var(--m-border-bright)] text-[var(--m-text-muted)]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className="font-mono text-xs text-[var(--m-text-dim)] mr-2">
                      {String.fromCharCode(65 + idx)}
                    </span>
                    {opt}
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {error && <div className="mb-4 text-red-400 text-sm">Error: {error}</div>}

      <button
        onClick={() => submit(answers)}
        disabled={!allAnswered || submitting}
        className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-lg py-3.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-40 transition"
      >
        {submitting
          ? 'Checking...'
          : allAnswered
          ? 'Submit Exam'
          : `Answer all ${questions.length} questions`}
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: app\courses\[slug]\page.tsx

// FILE: app/courses/[slug]/page.tsx
import Link from 'next/link'
import { redirect, notFound } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import { getCurrentUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import AppShell from '@/components/AppShell'
import EnrollButton from '@/app/dashboard/EnrollButton'
import CourseView, { type Saga } from './CourseView'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
export const revalidate = 0

export default async function CourseDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>
}) {
  const { slug } = await params
  const user = await getCurrentUser()
  if (!user) redirect('/login')

  const supabase = await createClient()

  const { data: course } = await supabase
    .from('courses')
    .select('id, slug, title, description, track, level')
    .eq('slug', slug)
    .maybeSingle()
  if (!course) notFound()

  // Read is_admin directly off the profile row
  const { data: profile } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('is_admin')
    .eq('id', user.id)
    .maybeSingle()

  const isAdmin = profile?.is_admin === true

  const { data: enrollment } = await supabase
    .from('enrollments')
    .select('id, progress')
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .eq('course_id', course.id)
    .maybeSingle()

  const { data: sagasData } = await supabase
    .from('modules')
    .select(`
      id, title, description, order_index,
      missions(
        id, title, description, order_index,
        lessons(id, title, order_index, is_final_exam, pass_threshold)
      )
    `)
    .eq('course_id', course.id)
    .order('order_index', { ascending: true })

  const sagas = (sagasData ?? []) as unknown as Saga[]

  const { data: progressData } = await supabase
    .from('lesson_progress')
    .select('lesson_id')
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .eq('completed', true)

  const completedIds = (progressData ?? []).map((p: { lesson_id: string }) => p.lesson_id)
  const completedSet = new Set<string>(completedIds)

  sagas.forEach(s => {
    s.missions = (s.missions ?? []).sort((a, b) => a.order_index - b.order_index)
    s.missions.forEach(m => {
      m.lessons = (m.lessons ?? []).sort((a, b) => a.order_index - b.order_index)
    })
  })

  const totalLessons = sagas.reduce(
    (sum, s) => sum + s.missions.reduce((a, m) => a + (m.lessons?.length ?? 0), 0),
    0
  )
  const doneLessons = sagas.reduce(
    (sum, s) =>
      sum +
      s.missions.reduce(
        (a, m) => a + (m.lessons ?? []).filter(l => completedSet.has(l.id)).length,
        0
      ),
    0
  )
  const overallPct = totalLessons > 0 ? Math.round((doneLessons / totalLessons) * 100) : 0

  let defaultOpenIndex = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < sagas.length; i++) {
    const hasIncomplete = sagas[i].missions.some(m =>
      (m.lessons ?? []).some(l => !completedSet.has(l.id))
    )
    if (hasIncomplete) {
      defaultOpenIndex = i
      break
    }
    if (i === sagas.length - 1) defaultOpenIndex = i
  }

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user.email ?? undefined}>
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        <Link
          href="/courses"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] hover:text-[#22d3ee] transition mb-8"
        >
          â† BACK TO COURSES
        </Link>

        <div className="mb-10 md:mb-14">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <span
              className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] px-2 py-1 rounded"
              style={{
                color: '#22d3ee',
                background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)',
                border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.28)',
              }}
            >
              {course.track === 'en_to_nl' ? 'EN â†’ NL' : course.track === 'nl_to_en' ? 'NL â†’ EN' : course.track?.toUpperCase()}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#566074]">
              {course.level}
            </span>
            {enrollment && (
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#10b981] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#10b981]" />
                ENROLLED
              </span>
            )}
            {isAdmin && (
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#f59e0b] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#f59e0b]" />
                ADMIN Â· ALL UNLOCKED
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl md:text-5xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[#e5e9f0] mb-4 leading-tight">
            {course.title}
          </h1>

          {course.description && (
            <p className="text-sm md:text-base leading-relaxed text-[#8892a6] max-w-2xl mb-8">
              {course.description}
            </p>
          )}

          {!enrollment ? (
            <EnrollButton courseId={course.id} enrolled={false} />
          ) : (
            <div className="max-w-lg">
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between mb-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#566074]">
                  COURSE PROGRESS
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#22d3ee] tabular-nums">
                  {doneLessons} / {totalLessons} Â· {overallPct}%
                </span>
              </div>
              <div
                className="h-[3px] rounded-full overflow-hidden"
                style={{ background: 'rgba(30,38,51,0.9)' }}
              >
                <div
                  className="h-full"
                  style={{
                    width: `${overallPct}%`,
                    background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
                    boxShadow: '0 0 12px rgba(34,211,238,0.5)',
                    transition: 'width 0.4s ease',
                  }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {sagas.length === 0 ? (
          <div
            className="rounded-2xl p-8 md:p-12 text-center"
            style={{
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.18)',
              background: 'rgba(6,10,16,0.6)',
            }}
          >
            <div className="text-base md:text-lg text-[#e5e9f0] mb-2">
              Curriculum coming soon.
            </div>
            <div className="text-sm text-[#8892a6]">
              This course is being prepared.
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <CourseView
            sagas={sagas}
            slug={slug}
            completedIds={completedIds}
            enrolled={!!enrollment}
            defaultOpenIndex={defaultOpenIndex}
            isAdmin={isAdmin}
          />
        )}

        <div className="mt-14 md:mt-20 flex items-center justify-center gap-6 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#566074] flex-wrap">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ background: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.6)' }} />
            <span>CLEARED</span>
          </span>
          <span className="w-px h-3" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.25)' }} />
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="inline-block w-2 h-2 rotate-45" style={{ background: '#22d3ee' }} />
            <span>CURRENT</span>
          </span>
          <span className="w-px h-3" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.25)' }} />
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ border: '1px solid #566074' }} />
            <span>LOCKED</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  )
}

import Link from 'next/link'
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import { getCurrentUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import AppShell from '@/components/AppShell'
import ContentArrowNav from '@/components/ContentArrowNav'

type Course = {
  id: string
  slug: string
  title: string
  description: string | null
  track: string
  level: string
}

type Enrollment = {
  course_id: string
  progress: number | null
}

// Courses temporarily hidden from the catalog until their content is ready.
// Remove a slug from here once the course is complete.
// Long-term clean fix: set courses.published = false in Supabase.
const HIDDEN_SLUGS = new Set<string>([
  'english-b1-fluency',
])

export default async function CoursesPage() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser()
  if (!user) redirect('/login')

  const supabase = await createClient()

  const { data: courses } = await supabase
    .from('courses')
    .select('id, slug, title, description, track, level')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })

  const visibleCourses = ((courses ?? []) as Course[]).filter(
    c => !HIDDEN_SLUGS.has(c.slug)
  )

  const { data: enrollments } = await supabase
    .from('enrollments')
    .select('course_id, progress')
    .eq('user_id', user.id)

  const enrolledMap = new Map<string, number>()
  ;(enrollments as Enrollment[] | null)?.forEach(e => {
    enrolledMap.set(e.course_id, e.progress ?? 0)
  })

  const list = visibleCourses

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user.email ?? undefined}>
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">

        {/* Masthead */}
        <div className="mb-10 md:mb-12 relative">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] mb-3">
            <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />

          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl md:text-5xl font-bold tracking-[0.08em] text-[#e5e9f0] mb-4 leading-tight">
            SELECT YOUR PATH
          </h1>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <p className="text-sm text-[#8892a6]">
              {list.length} {list.length === 1 ? 'program' : 'programs'} available
            </p>
            <span className="flex-1 h-px" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.35) 0%, transparent 100%)' }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {list.length === 0 ? (
          <div
            className="rounded-2xl p-12 text-center relative"
            style={{
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.18)',
              background: 'rgba(6,10,16,0.6)',
            }}
          >
            <span
              className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-3 h-3"
              style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', opacity: 0.5 }}
            />
            <span
              className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3 h-3"
              style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', opacity: 0.5 }}
            />
            <div className="text-lg text-[#e5e9f0] mb-2">No programs yet.</div>
            <div className="text-sm text-[#8892a6]">New curriculum arrives soon.</div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <ContentArrowNav>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {list.map((course, i) => {
                const enrolled = enrolledMap.has(course.id)
                const progress = enrolledMap.get(course.id) ?? 0
                const isFirst = i === 0

                return (
                  <Link
                    key={course.id}
                    href={'/courses/' + course.slug}
                    data-nav-item
                    className="group block relative rounded-xl transition-all duration-300 hover:translate-x-1"
                    style={{
                      border: isFirst
                        ? '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.55)'
                        : '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.14)',
                      background: isFirst
                        ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.14) 0%, rgba(139,92,246,0.05) 100%)'
                        : 'rgba(6,10,16,0.62)',
                      boxShadow: isFirst
                        ? '0 0 50px rgba(34,211,238,0.20), inset 0 0 60px rgba(34,211,238,0.05)'
                        : 'none',
                      backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
                    }}
                  >
                    {/* HUD corners â€” larger, thicker for the active card */}
                    <span
                      className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 transition-opacity"
                      style={{
                        width: isFirst ? 14 : 12,
                        height: isFirst ? 14 : 12,
                        borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee',
                        borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee',
                        opacity: isFirst ? 1 : 0.5,
                        filter: isFirst ? 'drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(34,211,238,0.6))' : 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span
                      className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 transition-opacity"
                      style={{
                        width: isFirst ? 14 : 12,
                        height: isFirst ? 14 : 12,
                        borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee',
                        borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee',
                        opacity: isFirst ? 1 : 0.5,
                        filter: isFirst ? 'drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(34,211,238,0.6))' : 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span
                      className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 transition-opacity"
                      style={{
                        width: isFirst ? 14 : 12,
                        height: isFirst ? 14 : 12,
                        borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee',
                        borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee',
                        opacity: isFirst ? 1 : 0.5,
                        filter: isFirst ? 'drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(34,211,238,0.6))' : 'none',
                      }}
                    />
                    <span
                      className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 transition-opacity"
                      style={{
                        width: isFirst ? 14 : 12,
                        height: isFirst ? 14 : 12,
                        borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee',
                        borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee',
                        opacity: isFirst ? 1 : 0.5,
                        filter: isFirst ? 'drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(34,211,238,0.6))' : 'none',
                      }}
                    />

                    {/* Active card top scanline */}
                    {isFirst && (
                      <div
                        className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 h-px"
                        style={{
                          background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, #22d3ee 50%, transparent 100%)',
                          opacity: 0.85,
                        }}
                      />
                    )}

                    <div className="p-5 md:p-7">
                      <div className="flex items-start gap-4 md:gap-6">
                        {/* Index */}
                        <div
                          className="text-[11px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] shrink-0 pt-1 tabular-nums"
                          style={{
                            color: isFirst ? '#22d3ee' : '#3a4250',
                            textShadow: isFirst ? '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.7)' : 'none',
                            width: 26,
                          }}
                        >
                          {String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
                        </div>

                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          {/* Track row */}
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-3">
                            <span
                              className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] px-2 py-0.5 rounded"
                              style={{
                                color: '#22d3ee',
                                background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)',
                                border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.28)',
                              }}
                            >
                              {course.track === 'en_to_nl'
                                ? 'EN â†’ NL'
                                : course.track === 'nl_to_en'
                                ? 'NL â†’ EN'
                                : course.track?.toUpperCase()}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074]">
                              {course.level}
                            </span>
                            {enrolled && (
                              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#10b981] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#10b981]" />
                                ENROLLED
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          {/* Title */}
                          <h2
                            className="text-lg md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight transition-colors group-hover:text-white mb-2"
                            style={{
                              color: isFirst ? '#ffffff' : '#e5e9f0',
                              textShadow: isFirst ? '0 0 24px rgba(34,211,238,0.20)' : 'none',
                            }}
                          >
                            {course.title}
                          </h2>

                          {course.description && (
                            <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#8892a6] mb-4">
                              {course.description}
                            </p>
                          )}

                          {enrolled && (
                            <div className="mt-4 max-w-md">
                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074]">
                                  PROGRESS
                                </span>
                                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#22d3ee] tabular-nums">
                                  {String(progress).padStart(3, '0')}%
                                </span>
                              </div>
                              <div
                                className="h-[3px] rounded-full overflow-hidden"
                                style={{ background: 'rgba(30,38,51,0.9)' }}
                              >
                                <div
                                  className="h-full"
                                  style={{
                                    width: progress + '%',
                                    background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
                                    boxShadow: '0 0 12px rgba(34,211,238,0.6)',
                                  }}
                                />
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>

                        {/* Right chevron */}
                        <div
                          className="text-xl shrink-0 pt-1 transition-all group-hover:translate-x-1"
                          style={{
                            color: isFirst ? '#22d3ee' : '#3a4250',
                            textShadow: isFirst ? '0 0 12px rgba(34,211,238,0.7)' : 'none',
                          }}
                        >
                          â–¸
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Bottom accent line for active card */}
                    {isFirst && (
                      <div
                        className="h-[1px]"
                        style={{
                          background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, rgba(34,211,238,0.6) 50%, transparent 100%)',
                        }}
                      />
                    )}
                  </Link>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </ContentArrowNav>
        )}

        {/* Footer HUD hint */}
        <div className="mt-10 flex items-center justify-center gap-6 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#566074] flex-wrap">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="inline-block w-3 h-3" style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
            <span>HOVER + â†â†‘â†“â†’</span>
            <span>NAVIGATE</span>
          </span>
          <span className="w-px h-3" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.25)' }} />
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-[#22d3ee]">â†µ</span>
            <span>ENTER</span>
            <span>SELECT</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  )
}

FILE: app\dashboard\EnrollButton.tsx

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { enrollInCourse } from '@/app/actions/enroll'

export default function EnrollButton({
  courseId,
  enrolled,
}: {
  courseId: string
  enrolled: boolean
}) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  if (enrolled) {
    return (
      <span className="text-green-400 text-sm font-semibold">âœ“ Enrolled</span>
    )
  }

  async function handleClick() {
    setLoading(true)
    await enrollInCourse(courseId)
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleClick}
      disabled={loading}
      className="bg-white text-black text-sm font-semibold rounded-full px-5 py-1.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50"
    >
      {loading ? 'Enrolling...' : 'Enroll'}
    </button>
  )
}

FILE: app\dashboard\page.tsx

import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import AppShell from '@/components/AppShell'
import EnrollButton from './EnrollButton'

const DASHBOARD_HIDDEN_SLUGS = new Set<string>(['english-b1-fluency'])

export default async function DashboardPage() {
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

  if (!user) redirect('/login')

  const { data: profile } = await supabase
    .from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', user.id).single()

  const { data: courses } = await supabase
    .from('courses').select('*').eq('published', true)

  const visibleCourses = (courses ?? []).filter(
    (c) => !DASHBOARD_HIDDEN_SLUGS.has(c.slug)
  )

  const { data: enrollments } = await supabase
    .from('enrollments').select('course_id').eq('user_id', user.id)

  const enrolledIds = new Set(enrollments?.map((e) => e.course_id) || [])

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user.email}>
      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 py-12">
        <div className="mb-12">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] mb-3">
            <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
            DASHBOARD
            <span className="w-px h-3 mx-2" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.25)' }} />
            <span className="text-[#3a4250]">OVERVIEW / LIVE</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-5xl font-bold tracking-[0.06em] text-[#e5e9f0] mb-3 leading-tight">
            Welcome back, {profile?.full_name || 'learner'}
          </h1>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <p className="text-sm text-[#8892a6]">Pick a course and continue your journey.</p>
            <span className="flex-1 h-px max-w-md" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.35) 0%, transparent 100%)' }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-5">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#566074]">AVAILABLE COURSES</span>
          <span className="w-px h-3" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.2)' }} />
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#3a4250]">
            {String(visibleCourses.length).padStart(2, '0')} LOADED
          </span>
        </div>

        {visibleCourses.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="m-hud-panel p-12 text-center">
            <span className="m-hud-scan" />
            <div className="text-base text-[#e5e9f0] mb-2">No courses available yet.</div>
            <div className="text-sm text-[#8892a6]">New curriculum arrives soon.</div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
            {visibleCourses.map((course) => {
              const enrolled = enrolledIds.has(course.id)
              const trackLabel =
                course.track === 'en_to_nl' ? 'EN â†’ NL'
                : course.track === 'nl_to_en' ? 'NL â†’ EN'
                : course.track?.toUpperCase()
              return (
                <div key={course.id} className="m-hud-panel flex flex-col relative transition-all duration-300 hover:translate-x-1 group">
                  <span className="m-hud-scan" />
                  <div className="m-hud-header">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee]" />
                      <span style={{ color: '#22d3ee', textShadow: '0 0 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.5)' }}>{trackLabel}</span>
                      <span style={{ color: '#3a4250' }}>/ COURSE</span>
                    </div>
                    <span style={{ color: '#566074' }}>{course.level}</span>
                  </div>

                  <Link href={'/courses/' + course.slug} className="flex-1 block p-6 md:p-7">
                    <h3 className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[#e5e9f0] group-hover:text-white transition-colors mb-3 leading-snug">
                      {course.title}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-[#8892a6] text-sm leading-relaxed">{course.description}</p>
                  </Link>

                  <div className="px-6 md:px-7 pb-6 md:pb-7">
                    {enrolled ? (
                      <Link href={'/courses/' + course.slug}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#22d3ee] text-black text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase rounded-md px-5 py-2.5 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition">
                        Continue Course
                        <span className="text-base leading-none">â†’</span>
                      </Link>
                    ) : (
                      <EnrollButton courseId={course.id} enrolled={false} />
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  )
}

FILE: app\dashboard\SignOutButton.tsx

'use client'

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function SignOutButton() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()

  async function handleSignOut() {
    await supabase.auth.signOut()
    router.push('/')
    router.refresh()
  }

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleSignOut}
      className="text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition"
    >
      Sign out
    </button>
  )
}

FILE: app\forgot-password\page.tsx
