// FILE: app/forgot-password/page.tsx  (URL: /forgot-password)

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    setError(null)

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, {
      redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/reset-password`,
    })

    if (error) {
      setError(error.message)
    } else {
      setSent(true)
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--m-bg)] text-[var(--m-text)] m-grid-bg flex items-center justify-center px-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md m-panel p-8">
        <Link
          href="/login"
          className="text-sm text-[var(--m-text-dim)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)]"
        >
          â† Back to login
        </Link>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-4 mb-2">Reset your password</h1>

        {sent ? (
          <div className="mt-6">
            <div className="border border-green-800/50 bg-green-950/30 rounded-lg p-4 text-sm text-green-400 mb-4">
              âœ“ Reset link sent to <strong>{email}</strong>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)] text-sm">
              Check your inbox. The link will take you to a page where you can
              choose a new password.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <p className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)] text-sm mb-6">
              Enter your email and we'll send you a link to reset your password.
            </p>

            {error && (
              <p className="bg-red-950 border border-red-800 text-red-300 text-sm rounded-lg p-3 mb-4">
                {error}
              </p>
            )}

            <label className="block text-sm text-[var(--m-text-muted)] mb-1">
              Email
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              required
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-6 focus:border-[var(--m-border-bright)] outline-none"
            />

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-full py-2.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
            >
              {loading ? 'Sending...' : 'Send reset link'}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}

.m-bg-horizon { opacity: 0.18 !important; }

/* Vignette: even lighter so edges don't band */
.m-bg-vignette {
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 45%, transparent 45%, rgba(3,6,12,0.22) 100%) !important;
}

/* HUD panels: make them fully opaque so background never shows through */
.m-hud-panel {
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #1a2433 0%, #121a27 100%) !important;
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
}

/* Standard panels: solid too */
.m-panel {
  background: #121a27 !important;
}
.m-panel-raised {
  background: #1a2433 !important;

export default async function BookDetailPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
  const { slug } = await params
  const user = await getCurrentUser()
  if (!user) redirect('/login')

  const supabase = await createClient()

  const { data: book } = await supabase
    .from('library_books')
    .select('*')
    .eq('slug', slug)
    .eq('published', true)
    .maybeSingle()

  if (!book) notFound()

  const { data: chapters } = await supabase
    .from('library_chapters')
    .select('id, chapter_number, title, word_count')
    .eq('book_id', book.id)
    .order('chapter_number', { ascending: true })

  const { data: progress } = await supabase
    .from('library_progress')
    .select('chapter_id, scroll_percentage')
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .eq('book_id', book.id)
    .maybeSingle()

  const currentChapter = chapters?.find(c => c.id === progress?.chapter_id)
  const startChapterId = currentChapter ? currentChapter.id : chapters?.[0]?.id

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user.email ?? undefined}>
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-20">
        <Link href="/library/ebooks" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] hover:text-[#22d3ee] transition mb-8">
          â† BACK TO LIBRARY
        </Link>

          style={{
            background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${book.accent_color}15 0%, rgba(6,10,16,0.8) 100%)`,

              {book.subtitle}
            </h2>

              {book.description}
            </p>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
              {startChapterId && (
                <Link
                  href={`/library/ebooks/${book.slug}/read?chapter=${startChapterId}`}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-xs rounded-lg px-8 py-4 transition hover:opacity-90"

                >
                  {progress ? 'CONTINUE READING â†’' : 'START READING â†’'}

                </span>

          </div>

            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#566074]">TABLE OF CONTENTS</span>
            <span className="flex-1 h-px bg-[#1e2633]" />
          </div>

            {chapters?.map((ch) => {
              const isCurrent = progress?.chapter_id === ch.id
              
              return (
                <Link
                  key={ch.id}
                  href={`/library/ebooks/${book.slug}/read?chapter=${ch.id}`}
                  className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 rounded-xl transition-all group hover:bg-white/[0.02]"
                  style={{
                    border: isCurrent ? `1px solid ${book.accent_color}60` : '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05)',
                    background: isCurrent ? `${book.accent_color}08` : 'transparent'
                  }}
                >
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tabular-nums shrink-0" style={{ color: isCurrent ? book.accent_color : '#3a4250' }}>
                    CH {String(ch.chapter_number).padStart(2, '0')}
                  </span>

                    {ch.title}

// FILE: app/library/ebooks/[slug]/read/page.tsx
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback, use } from 'react'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import LogoEmblem from '@/components/LogoEmblem'

function playPageFlip() {
  try {
    const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext
    if (!AudioContext) return
    const ctx = new AudioContext()
    const bufferSize = ctx.sampleRate * 0.4
    const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, ctx.sampleRate)
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(0)
    for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) data[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1
    const noise = ctx.createBufferSource()
    noise.buffer = buffer
    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter()
    filter.type = 'bandpass'
    filter.frequency.value = 1000
    filter.Q.value = 0.5
    const gain = ctx.createGain()
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, ctx.currentTime)
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.1, ctx.currentTime + 0.05)
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.3)
    noise.connect(filter)
    filter.connect(gain)
    gain.connect(ctx.destination)
    noise.start()
  } catch (e) {}
}

// ðŸŽ¯ PERFECT MATH PAGINATION (1250 chars, sentence-level splitting)
function paginateBook(chapters: any[]): string[][] {
  const pages: string[][] = []
  let currentPage: string[] = []
  let currentCharCount = 0
  const MAX_CHARS_PER_PAGE = 1250 

  const pushPage = () => {
    if (currentPage.length > 0) {
      pages.push(currentPage)
      currentPage = []
      currentCharCount = 0
    }
  }

  chapters.forEach((chapter) => {
    pushPage() 
    currentPage.push(`# ${chapter.title}`)
    currentCharCount += chapter.title.length + 10

    const blocks = (chapter.content || '').split(/\n\s*\n/).map((b: string) => b.trim()).filter((b: string) => b.length > 0)

    blocks.forEach((block: string) => {
      const blockLength = block.length

      // Keep headings with their content
      if (block.startsWith('## ') || block.startsWith('### ')) {
        if (currentCharCount + blockLength > MAX_CHARS_PER_PAGE - 150) pushPage()
        currentPage.push(block)
        currentCharCount += blockLength + 5
        return
      }

      if (currentCharCount + blockLength < MAX_CHARS_PER_PAGE) {
        currentPage.push(block)
        currentCharCount += blockLength
      } else {
        // Split by sentence to perfectly fill the page
        const sentences = block.match(/[^.!?]+[.!?]+/g) || [block]
        sentences.forEach((sentence: string) => {
          if (currentCharCount + sentence.length > MAX_CHARS_PER_PAGE) {
            pushPage()
          }
          currentPage.push(sentence.trim())
          currentCharCount += sentence.length
        })
      }
    })
  })

  pushPage()
  return pages
}

export default function ReaderPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
  const { slug } = use(params)
  const supabase = createClient()

  const [book, setBook] = useState<any>(null)
  const [pages, setPages] = useState<string[][]>([])
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(0)
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false)
  const [fontSize, setFontSize] = useState(15) // Default 15px to fit more

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      const { data: b } = await supabase.from('library_books').select('*').eq('slug', slug).maybeSingle()
      if (!b) return
      setBook(b)

      const { data: chs } = await supabase
        .from('library_chapters')
        .select('title, content')
        .eq('book_id', b.id)
        .order('chapter_number', { ascending: true })
      
      if (chs) {
        setPages(paginateBook(chs))
      }
    }
    load()
  }, [slug, supabase])

  const turnPage = useCallback((direction: 'next' | 'prev') => {
    if (!isOpen) return
    if (direction === 'next' && currentPage + 2 < pages.length) {
      playPageFlip(); setCurrentPage(p => p + 2)
    } else if (direction === 'prev' && currentPage > 0) {
      playPageFlip(); setCurrentPage(p => p - 2)
    }
  }, [isOpen, currentPage, pages.length])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') turnPage('next')
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') turnPage('prev')
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
  }, [isOpen, turnPage])

  const renderBlock = (block: string, idx: number) => {
    const t = block.trim()
    if (!t) return null

    let parsed = t.replace(/\*\*(.*?)\*\*/g, '<strong style="color: #111; font-weight: 700;">$1</strong>').replace(/\*(.*?)\*/g, '<em style="color: #444;">$1</em>')

    if (t.startsWith('# ')) return <h1 key={idx} className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-6 mt-4 font-serif text-[#111] text-center tracking-tight" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: parsed.slice(2) }} />
    if (t.startsWith('## ')) return <h2 key={idx} className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold mt-8 mb-4 font-serif text-[#1a1a1a]" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: parsed.slice(3) }} />
    if (t.startsWith('### ')) return <h3 key={idx} className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold mt-6 mb-3 font-serif text-[#1a1a1a]" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: parsed.slice(4) }} />
    
    if (t.startsWith('> ')) return (
      <p key={idx} className="mb-4 leading-[1.7] font-serif italic text-[#444] text-justify pl-4 border-l-2 border-[#ccc]">
        <span dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: parsed.slice(2) }} />
      </p>
    )
    
    return (
      <p 
        key={idx} 
        className="mb-4 leading-[1.7] text-[#2a2a2a] font-serif text-[1em] text-justify" 
        dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: parsed }} 
      />
    )
  }

  if (!book) return <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#0a0f16] flex items-center justify-center text-[#22d3ee] font-mono tracking-widest animate-pulse">ESTABLISHING CONNECTION...</div>

  if (!isOpen) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#050810] flex items-center justify-center p-6" style={{ perspective: '2000px' }}>
        <div className="relative w-full max-w-md aspect-[2/3] rounded-r-xl rounded-l-md cursor-pointer group transition-all duration-700 hover:rotate-y-[-15deg] hover:scale-105"
          onClick={() => { setIsOpen(true); playPageFlip(); }}>
          <div className="absolute inset-0 translate-x-3 translate-y-3 rounded-r-xl rounded-l-md bg-black/80 blur-2xl transition-all duration-700 group-hover:translate-x-10 group-hover:translate-y-5 group-hover:blur-3xl" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-r-xl rounded-l-md bg-[#e5e0d0] translate-x-2 translate-y-2" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-r-xl rounded-l-md overflow-hidden flex flex-col border border-[#22d3ee]/30"
            style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(180deg, #0a121e 0%, #050810 100%)', boxShadow: `inset 10px 0 25px -8px rgba(0,0,0,0.95), 0 0 100px rgba(34,211,238,0.15)` }}>
            <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-40" style={{ background: `radial-gradient(circle at 50% 30%, #22d3ee30 0%, transparent 70%)` }} />
            <div className="absolute top-0 bottom-0 left-0 w-10 bg-gradient-to-r from-black/95 to-transparent z-20 mix-blend-multiply" />
            <div className="absolute top-0 bottom-0 left-10 w-px bg-white/5 z-20" />
            <div className="absolute top-10 right-10 w-12 h-12 border-t border-r border-[#22d3ee]/50" />
            <div className="absolute bottom-10 left-10 w-12 h-12 border-b border-l border-[#22d3ee]/50" />
            
            <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col h-full p-12 pl-16 justify-between items-center text-center">
              <div className="text-[11px] font-mono tracking-[0.5em] text-[#22d3ee]/80 border-b border-[#22d3ee]/30 pb-3 w-full">MERIDIAN ACADEMY</div>
              <div>
                <div className="transform group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-500 relative flex justify-center">
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[#22d3ee] blur-[50px] opacity-20 rounded-full" />
                  <LogoEmblem size={140} className="relative z-10" />
                </div>
                <h2 className="text-4xl font-serif font-bold text-white leading-tight mt-10 mb-6">{book.title}</h2>
                <div className="h-px w-24 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-[#22d3ee] to-transparent mx-auto my-6" />
                <div className="text-base font-serif italic text-[#8892a6]">{book.subtitle}</div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-mono tracking-[0.5em] text-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse">CLICK TO OPEN</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="h-screen w-screen bg-[#050810] flex flex-col relative overflow-hidden font-sans selection:bg-[#22d3ee] selection:text-black">
      <header className="h-14 bg-[#0a0f16] border-b border-[#1e2633] flex items-center justify-between px-6 shrink-0 z-50">
        <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#566074] hover:text-[#22d3ee] transition">â† CLOSE BOOK</button>
        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#e5e9f0] uppercase">{book.title}</span>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-[#1e2633] rounded bg-[#141b26] p-1">
            <button onClick={() => setFontSize(f => Math.max(12, f - 1))} className="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-[#1e2633] rounded text-[#8892a6] text-xs font-mono">A-</button>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono w-6 text-center text-[#22d3ee]">{fontSize}</span>
            <button onClick={() => setFontSize(f => Math.min(24, f + 1))} className="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-[#1e2633] rounded text-[#8892a6] text-xs font-mono">A+</button>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#22d3ee]">PAGE {currentPage + 1} / {pages.length}</div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4 md:p-8 relative overflow-hidden">
        <button onClick={() => turnPage('prev')} disabled={currentPage === 0} className="absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 left-4 md:left-10 w-12 h-24 flex items-center justify-center rounded-r-xl bg-[#0a0f16] border border-[#22d3ee]/30 text-[#22d3ee] hover:bg-[#22d3ee] hover:text-black transition-all disabled:opacity-20 z-50 text-2xl shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(34,211,238,0.15)]">â—‚</button>
        <button onClick={() => turnPage('next')} disabled={currentPage + 2 >= pages.length} className="absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 right-4 md:right-10 w-12 h-24 flex items-center justify-center rounded-l-xl bg-[#0a0f16] border border-[#22d3ee]/30 text-[#22d3ee] hover:bg-[#22d3ee] hover:text-black transition-all disabled:opacity-20 z-50 text-2xl shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(34,211,238,0.15)]">â–¸</button>

        <div className="relative w-full max-w-[1200px] h-[calc(100vh-140px)] flex shadow-[0_30px_80px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
          
          <div className="w-1/2 h-full bg-[#fcf9f2] flex flex-col relative border border-[#e5e0d0] border-r-0">
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden px-10 md:px-14 py-10">
              <div className="max-w-[60ch] mx-auto">
                {pages[currentPage]?.map((block, i) => renderBlock(block, i))}

// FILE: app/library/ebooks/page.tsx
import Link from 'next/link'

import { getCurrentUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import AppShell from '@/components/AppShell'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
export const revalidate = 0

export default async function EbooksPage() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser()
  if (!user) redirect('/login')

  const supabase = await createClient()

  const { data: books } = await supabase
    .from('library_books')
    .select('*')
    .eq('published', true)
    .order('order_index', { ascending: true })

  const { data: progress } = await supabase
    .from('library_progress')
    .select('book_id, chapter_id, scroll_percentage')
    .eq('user_id', user.id)

  const progressMap = new Map(progress?.map(p => [p.book_id, p]) || [])
  const categories = Array.from(new Set(books?.map(b => b.category) || ['Languages and Communication']))

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user.email ?? undefined}>
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto pb-24">
        <Link href="/library" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] hover:text-[#b87333] transition mb-8">
          â† BACK TO LIBRARY
        </Link>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#b87333] mb-3">
            <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#b87333] animate-pulse" />
            LIBRARY
            <span className="w-px h-3 mx-2" style={{ background: 'rgba(184,115,51,0.25)' }} />
            <span className="text-[#3a4250]">DIGITAL RENAISSANCE / 01</span>
          </div>

            FOUNDATIONAL BOOKS
          </h1>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <p className="text-sm text-[#8892a6]">
              {books?.length || 0} original manuscripts included with your membership.
            </p>
            <span className="flex-1 h-px" style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(184,115,51,0.35) 0%, transparent 100%)' }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {categories.map(category => (
          <div key={category} className="mb-16">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
              <h2 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold font-serif text-white tracking-wide">{category}</h2>
              <div className="h-px flex-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#b87333]/30 to-transparent" />
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[#566074]">
                {books?.filter(b => b.category === category).length} BOOKS
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-8 md:gap-10">
              {books?.filter(b => b.category === category).map((book, i) => {
                const hasProgress = progressMap.has(book.id)
                const prog = progressMap.get(book.id)

                return (
                  <Link 
                    key={book.id} 
                    href={`/library/ebooks/${book.slug}/read`}
                    className="group block relative flex flex-col"
                    style={{ perspective: '1000px' }}
                  >
                    
                    {/* ðŸŽ¯ UPDATED 3D BOOK COVER (Matches Reader) */}

const CATEGORIES = [
  { label: 'STUDY', title: 'Learning Specs', desc: 'Socratic learning. Test your own understanding.', count: 6, accent: '#22d3ee' },
  { label: 'RESEARCH', title: 'Research Specs', desc: 'Investigate any topic like a scholar.', count: 5, accent: '#8b5cf6' },
  { label: 'WRITING', title: 'Writing Specs', desc: 'Draft, edit, and polish any piece of writing.', count: 4, accent: '#10b981' },
  { label: 'BUSINESS', title: 'Business Specs', desc: 'Strategy, marketing, and operations.', count: 5, accent: '#f59e0b' },
  { label: 'AI', title: 'AI & Coding Specs', desc: 'Prompt engineering and systems thinking.', count: 4, accent: '#3b82f6' },
]

  if (!user) redirect('/login')

        <Link href="/library" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] hover:text-[#22d3ee] transition mb-8">
          â† BACK TO LIBRARY

FILE: app\login\page.tsx

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    setError(null)

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password })

    if (error) {
      setError(error.message)
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }

    router.push('/dashboard')
    router.refresh()
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-950 text-white flex items-center justify-center px-6">
      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-8"
      >
        <Link href="/" className="text-sm text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-300">
          â† Back
        </Link>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-4 mb-6">Log in</h1>

        {error && (
          <p className="bg-red-950 border border-red-800 text-red-300 text-sm rounded-lg p-3 mb-4">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}

        <label className="block text-sm text-neutral-400 mb-1">Email</label>
        <input
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-4 focus:border-neutral-600 outline-none"
        />

        <label className="block text-sm text-neutral-400 mb-1">Password</label>
        <input
          type="password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-6 focus:border-neutral-600 outline-none"
        />

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading}
          className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-full py-2.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loading ? 'Logging in...' : 'Log in'}
        </button>
<p className="text-sm text-center mt-4">
  <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-[var(--m-text-dim)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)] text-xs">
    Forgot your password?
  </Link>
</p>
        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500 mt-4 text-center">
          No account?{' '}
          <Link href="/signup" className="text-white hover:underline">Sign up</Link>
        </p>
      </form>
    </main>
  )
}

// FILE: app/page.tsx
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import { getCurrentUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import HomeScreen from '@/components/HomeScreen'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export default async function Home() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser()

  // ðŸŽ¯ IF NOT LOGGED IN, FORCE LOGIN SCREEN IMMEDIATELY
  if (!user) {
    redirect('/login')
  }

  // If logged in, show the main menu (or redirect to dashboard if you prefer)
  return (
    <HomeScreen 
      loggedIn={true} 
      continueHref="/dashboard" 
      continueLabel="CONTINUE" 
      continueSub="Resume your journey" 
    />
  )
}

// FILE: app/profile/page.tsx
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import { getCurrentUser } from '@/lib/auth'
import AppShell from '@/components/AppShell'
import ProfileClient from './ProfileClient'

export default async function ProfilePage() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser()
  if (!user) redirect('/login')

  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: profile } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('id, email, full_name, username, role, created_at')
    .eq('id', user.id)
    .maybeSingle()

  return (
    <AppShell userEmail={user.email ?? undefined}>
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto">
        {/* header */}
        <div className="mb-8 md:mb-10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] mb-2">
            <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
            PROFILE
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold tracking-[0.15em] text-[#e5e9f0]">
            OPERATOR
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm mt-2 text-[#8892a6]">
            Your account and identity inside Meridian Academy.
          </p>
        </div>

        <ProfileClient
          email={profile?.email ?? user.email ?? ''}
          username={profile?.username ?? ''}
          fullName={profile?.full_name ?? ''}
          role={profile?.role ?? 'member'}
          joinedAt={profile?.created_at ?? ''}
        />
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  )
}

// FILE: app/profile/ProfileClient.tsx
'use client'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

type Props = {
  email: string
  username: string
  fullName: string
  role: string
  joinedAt: string
}

export default function ProfileClient({
  email,
  username,
  fullName,
  role,
  joinedAt,
}: Props) {
  const supabase = createClient()

  const [name, setName] = useState(fullName)
  const [savingName, setSavingName] = useState(false)
  const [nameMsg, setNameMsg] = useState<{ type: 'ok' | 'err'; text: string } | null>(null)

  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [savingPw, setSavingPw] = useState(false)
  const [pwMsg, setPwMsg] = useState<{ type: 'ok' | 'err'; text: string } | null>(null)

  async function saveName() {
    setSavingName(true)
    setNameMsg(null)
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .update({ full_name: name })
      .eq('email', email)

    if (error) {
      setNameMsg({ type: 'err', text: error.message })
    } else {
      setNameMsg({ type: 'ok', text: 'Name updated.' })
    }
    setSavingName(false)
  }

  async function updatePassword() {
    if (password.length < 6) {
      setPwMsg({ type: 'err', text: 'Password must be at least 6 characters.' })
      return
    }
    setSavingPw(true)
    setPwMsg(null)
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password })

    if (error) {
      setPwMsg({ type: 'err', text: error.message })
    } else {
      setPwMsg({ type: 'ok', text: 'Password updated.' })
      setPassword('')
    }
    setSavingPw(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* IDENTITY */}
      <div
        className="relative rounded-2xl p-6 md:p-8 overflow-hidden"
        style={{
          border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.28)',
          background: 'rgba(6,10,16,0.72)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
          boxShadow: '0 0 60px rgba(34,211,238,0.10)',
        }}
      >
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-3 h-3"
          style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 w-3 h-3"
          style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-3 h-3"
          style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3 h-3"
          style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] mb-5">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
          IDENTITY
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          <InfoRow label="EMAIL" value={email} mono />
          <InfoRow label="USERNAME" value={username || 'â€”'} mono />
          <InfoRow label="ROLE" value={role.toUpperCase()} />
          {joinedAt && (
            <InfoRow
              label="JOINED"
              value={new Date(joinedAt).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
                day: 'numeric',
                month: 'long',
                year: 'numeric',
              })}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* DISPLAY NAME */}
      <div
        className="relative rounded-2xl p-6 md:p-8 overflow-hidden"
        style={{
          border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.15)',
          background: 'rgba(6,10,16,0.6)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
        }}
      >
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-2.5 h-2.5"
          style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1px solid #22d3ee' }} />
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 w-2.5 h-2.5"
          style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1px solid #22d3ee' }} />

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] mb-5">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
          DISPLAY NAME
        </div>

        <label className="o-label block mb-2 text-[10px]">FULL NAME</label>
        <input
          value={name}
          onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Your name"
          className="o-input"
        />

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-4">
          <button
            onClick={saveName}
            disabled={savingName || name === fullName}
            className="bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-xs rounded-lg px-6 py-3 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            {savingName ? 'SAVING...' : 'â–¸ SAVE'}
          </button>
          {nameMsg && (
            <span className="text-xs font-mono"
              style={{ color: nameMsg.type === 'ok' ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444' }}>
              {nameMsg.type === 'ok' ? 'âœ“ ' : 'âœ— '}{nameMsg.text}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* PASSWORD */}
      <div
        className="relative rounded-2xl p-6 md:p-8 overflow-hidden"
        style={{
          border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.15)',
          background: 'rgba(6,10,16,0.6)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
        }}
      >
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-2.5 h-2.5"
          style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1px solid #22d3ee' }} />
        <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 w-2.5 h-2.5"
          style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1px solid #22d3ee' }} />

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] mb-5">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
          SECURITY
        </div>

        <label className="o-label block mb-2 text-[10px]">NEW PASSWORD</label>
        <input
          type="password"
          value={password}
          onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="At least 6 characters"
          className="o-input"
        />

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-4">
          <button
            onClick={updatePassword}
            disabled={savingPw || password.length === 0}
            className="bg-[var(--m-cyan)] text-black font-bold tracking-widest text-xs rounded-lg px-6 py-3 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            {savingPw ? 'UPDATING...' : 'â–¸ UPDATE PASSWORD'}
          </button>
          {pwMsg && (
            <span className="text-xs font-mono"
              style={{ color: pwMsg.type === 'ok' ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444' }}>
              {pwMsg.type === 'ok' ? 'âœ“ ' : 'âœ— '}{pwMsg.text}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* console hint */}
      <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-5 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#566074] flex-wrap">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-[#22d3ee]">TAB</span>
          <span>NAVIGATE FIELDS</span>
        </span>
        <span className="w-px h-3" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.25)' }} />
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-[#22d3ee]">CLICK</span>
          <span>SAVE</span>
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function InfoRow({
  label,
  value,
  mono = false,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  mono?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 py-2 border-b last:border-b-0"
      style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)' }}>
      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] shrink-0">
        {label}
      </span>
      <span
        className={`text-sm text-right min-w-0 truncate ${mono ? 'font-mono' : ''}`}
        style={{ color: '#e5e9f0' }}
      >
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  )
}

// FILE: app/reset-password/page.tsx  (URL: /reset-password)

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('')
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => {
      if (data.user) setReady(true)
    })
  }, [supabase])

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (password !== confirm) {
      setError('Passwords do not match')
      return
    }
    if (password.length < 6) {
      setError('Password must be at least 6 characters')
      return
    }
    setLoading(true)
    setError(null)

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password })

    if (error) {
      setError(error.message)
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }
    router.push('/dashboard')
    router.refresh()
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--m-bg)] text-[var(--m-text)] m-grid-bg flex items-center justify-center px-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md m-panel p-8">
        <Link
          href="/login"
          className="text-sm text-[var(--m-text-dim)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)]"
        >
          â† Back to login
        </Link>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-4 mb-6">Choose a new password</h1>

        {!ready ? (
          <p className="text-[var(--m-text-muted)] text-sm">
            Verifying reset link...
          </p>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            {error && (
              <p className="bg-red-950 border border-red-800 text-red-300 text-sm rounded-lg p-3 mb-4">
                {error}
              </p>
            )}

            <label className="block text-sm text-[var(--m-text-muted)] mb-1">
              New password
            </label>
            <input
              type="password"
              required
              minLength={6}
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-4 focus:border-[var(--m-border-bright)] outline-none"
            />

            <label className="block text-sm text-[var(--m-text-muted)] mb-1">
              Confirm password
            </label>
            <input
              type="password"
              required
              minLength={6}
              value={confirm}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirm(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-[var(--m-bg)] border border-[var(--m-border)] rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-6 focus:border-[var(--m-border-bright)] outline-none"
            />

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-full py-2.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition"
            >
              {loading ? 'Updating...' : 'Update password'}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </main>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'

export default function SignupPage() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    setError(null)

    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
      email,
      password,
      options: {
        data: { full_name: fullName, username: email.split('@')[0] },
      },
    })

    if (error) {
      setError(error.message)
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }

    router.push('/dashboard')
    router.refresh()
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-950 text-white flex items-center justify-center px-6">
      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-8"
      >
        <Link href="/" className="text-sm text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-300">
          â† Back
        </Link>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-4 mb-6">Create your account</h1>

        {error && (
          <p className="bg-red-950 border border-red-800 text-red-300 text-sm rounded-lg p-3 mb-4">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}

        <label className="block text-sm text-neutral-400 mb-1">Full name</label>
        <input
          type="text"
          required
          value={fullName}
          onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-4 focus:border-neutral-600 outline-none"
        />

        <label className="block text-sm text-neutral-400 mb-1">Email</label>
        <input
          type="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-4 focus:border-neutral-600 outline-none"
        />

        <label className="block text-sm text-neutral-400 mb-1">Password</label>
        <input
          type="password"
          required
          minLength={6}
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 mb-6 focus:border-neutral-600 outline-none"
        />

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading}
          className="w-full bg-white text-black font-semibold rounded-full py-2.5 hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loading ? 'Creating...' : 'Create account'}
        </button>

        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500 mt-4 text-center">
          Already have one?{' '}
          <Link href="/login" className="text-white hover:underline">Log in</Link>
        </p>
      </form>
    </main>
  )
}

'use client'

type Variant = 'landing' | 'dashboard' | 'learning'

export default function AcademyBackground({
  variant = 'dashboard',
}: {
  variant?: Variant | string
}) {
  const v = (variant as Variant) || 'dashboard'
  const richer = v === 'landing'
  const quieter = v === 'learning'

  return (
    <div className="m-bg-root" aria-hidden>
      <div className="m-bg-base" />

      <div
        className="m-bg-orb m-bg-orb-a"
        style={{
          opacity: richer ? 0.75 : quieter ? 0.28 : 0.5,
        }}
      />
      <div
        className="m-bg-orb m-bg-orb-b"
        style={{
          opacity: richer ? 0.65 : quieter ? 0.22 : 0.42,
        }}
      />
      {!quieter && (
        <div
          className="m-bg-orb m-bg-orb-c"
          style={{ opacity: richer ? 0.5 : 0.28 }}
        />
      )}

      <div
        className="m-bg-grid"
        style={{
          opacity: richer ? 1 : quieter ? 0.35 : 0.7,
        }}
      />

      {!quieter && <div className="m-bg-horizon" />}

      <div className="m-bg-vignette" />
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================
FILE: components\ContentArrowNav.tsx
================================================================

'use client'
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { getActiveZone, setActiveZone, onZoneChange } from '@/lib/navZone'
import { unlockAudio, playSelectSound, playConfirmSound } from '@/lib/sounds'

export default function ContentArrowNav({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode
}) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0)
  const [zoneActive, setZoneActive] = useState(getActiveZone() === 'content')

  useEffect(() => {
    return onZoneChange(z => {
      setZoneActive(z === 'content')
      if (z !== 'content') setActiveIndex(-1)
    })
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!getActiveZone()) setActiveZone('content')
    setZoneActive(getActiveZone() === 'content')
    setActiveIndex(0)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!zoneActive) return

    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      const el = ref.current
      if (!el) return
      const items = el.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-nav-item]')
      if (items.length === 0) return

      const active = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null
      if (active) {
        const tag = active.tagName.toLowerCase()
        if (tag === 'input' || tag === 'textarea' || tag === 'select' || active.isContentEditable) {
          return
        }
      }

      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault()
        unlockAudio()
        playSelectSound()
        setActiveIndex(i => (i + 1 >= items.length ? 0 : i + 1))
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault()
        unlockAudio()
        playSelectSound()
        setActiveIndex(i => (i - 1 < 0 ? items.length - 1 : i - 1))
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        const idx = activeIndex < 0 ? 0 : activeIndex
        const target = items[idx]
        if (target) {
          e.preventDefault()
          unlockAudio()
          playConfirmSound()
          target.click()
        }
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true)
  }, [zoneActive, activeIndex])

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current
    if (!el) return
    const items = el.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-nav-item]')
    items.forEach((it, i) => {
      if (zoneActive && i === activeIndex) {
        it.setAttribute('data-nav-active', 'true')
      } else {
        it.removeAttribute('data-nav-active')
      }
    })
  }, [activeIndex, zoneActive])

  return (
    <div ref={ref} data-nav-zone="content">
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================
FILE: components\DailyGoal.tsx
================================================================

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

const GOALS = [
  { label: 'Complete 1 Side Quest', xp: 20 },
  { label: 'Review Quest Codex',    xp: 10 },
  { label: 'Practice 15 minutes',   xp: 15 },
  { label: 'Post in Community',     xp: 10 },
  { label: 'Read 1 Library entry',  xp: 15 },
  { label: 'Flashcard session',     xp: 10 },
]

export default function DailyGoal() {
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<boolean[]>([])
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const today = new Date().toDateString()
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('m-daily-goal')
    const storedDate = localStorage.getItem('m-daily-goal-date')
    if (storedDate === today && stored) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(stored) as boolean[]
        // pad if length mismatch (added goals over time)
        const normalised = GOALS.map((_, i) => !!parsed[i])
        setChecked(normalised)
      } catch {
        setChecked(GOALS.map(() => false))
      }
    } else {
      const fresh = GOALS.map(() => false)
      setChecked(fresh)
      localStorage.setItem('m-daily-goal', JSON.stringify(fresh))
      localStorage.setItem('m-daily-goal-date', today)
    }
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  function toggle(i: number) {
    const next = [...checked]
    next[i] = !next[i]
    setChecked(next)
    localStorage.setItem('m-daily-goal', JSON.stringify(next))
  }

  if (!mounted) return null

  const done = checked.filter(Boolean).length
  const earnedXp = GOALS.reduce((sum, g, i) => sum + (checked[i] ? g.xp : 0), 0)
  const totalXp = GOALS.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.xp, 0)
  const pct = (done / GOALS.length) * 100
  const allDone = done === GOALS.length

  return (
    <div className="m-hud-panel">
      <span className="m-hud-scan" />
      <div className="m-hud-header">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee]" />
          DAILY GOAL
        </div>
        <span style={{ color: allDone ? '#10b981' : '#566074' }}>
          {done}/{GOALS.length}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4">
        {/* XP + progress */}
        <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between mb-2">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074]">
            DAILY XP
          </span>
          <span className="text-sm font-bold tabular-nums" style={{ color: '#a5f3fc' }}>
            {earnedXp}
            <span className="text-[10px] text-[#3a4250] font-normal"> / {totalXp}</span>
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="h-[3px] rounded-full overflow-hidden mb-4"
          style={{ background: 'rgba(30,38,51,0.9)' }}>
          <div
            className="h-full transition-all duration-500"
            style={{
              width: pct + '%',
              background: allDone
                ? '#10b981'
                : 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
              boxShadow: allDone
                ? '0 0 10px rgba(16,185,129,0.6)'
                : '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.5)',
            }}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Goals */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          {GOALS.map((goal, i) => (
            <button
              key={i}
              onClick={() => toggle(i)}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 text-left text-xs group transition-colors rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 -mx-2 hover:bg-white/[0.02]"
            >
              <span
                className={
                  'shrink-0 w-4 h-4 rounded border flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] transition ' +
                  (checked[i]
                    ? 'bg-[#22d3ee] border-[#22d3ee] text-black'
                    : 'border-[#2d3748] group-hover:border-[#22d3ee]/60')
                }
              >
                {checked[i] && 'âœ“'}
              </span>
              <span
                className={
                  'flex-1 transition ' +
                  (checked[i] ? 'text-[#3a4250] line-through' : 'text-[#e5e9f0]')
                }
              >
                {goal.label}
              </span>
              <span
                className="text-[9px] font-mono tabular-nums shrink-0"
                style={{ color: checked[i] ? '#10b981' : '#3a4250' }}
              >
                +{goal.xp}
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {allDone && (
          <div
            className="mt-3 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-center text-[#10b981] rounded-md py-2"
            style={{ border: '1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.4)', background: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.06)' }}
          >
            âœ“ ALL GOALS COMPLETE
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// FILE: components/DisciplineScreen.tsx
'use client'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { pickQuote, type DisciplineQuote } from '@/lib/discipline'
import LogoEmblem from './LogoEmblem'

export default function DisciplineScreen({
  onDone,
  duration = 6000,
}: {
  onDone: () => void
  duration?: number
}) {
  const [quote, setQuote] = useState<DisciplineQuote | null>(null)
  const [fading, setFading] = useState(false)
  const [dots, setDots] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    setQuote(pickQuote())
    const id = setInterval(() => {
      setDots((d) => (d.length >= 3 ? '' : d + '.'))
    }, 400)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!quote) return
    const fadeTimer = setTimeout(() => setFading(true), duration - 900)
    const doneTimer = setTimeout(onDone, duration)
    return () => { clearTimeout(fadeTimer); clearTimeout(doneTimer) }
  }, [quote, duration, onDone])

  if (!quote) return null

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-8 pointer-events-none">
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0"
        style={{
          background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at center, #0a1620 0%, #050810 60%, #000 100%)',
          opacity: fading ? 0 : 1,
          transition: 'opacity 0.9s ease-out',
        }}
      />
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none"
        style={{
          background: 'radial-gradient(circle at center, rgba(34,211,238,0.06) 0%, transparent 55%)',
          opacity: fading ? 0 : 1,
          transition: 'opacity 0.9s ease-out',
        }}
      />

      <div
        className="relative flex flex-col items-center max-w-3xl text-center"
        style={{ opacity: fading ? 0 : 1, transition: 'opacity 0.9s ease-out' }}
      >
        <LogoEmblem size={200} className="mb-6" />
        <div
          className="text-2xl md:text-3xl leading-relaxed italic mb-8"
          style={{
            fontFamily: 'Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif',
            color: '#e5e9f0',
            textShadow: '0 0 24px rgba(34,211,238,0.15)',
          }}
        >
          &ldquo;{quote.text}&rdquo;
        </div>
        <div className="text-sm tracking-widest uppercase" style={{ color: '#22d3ee', letterSpacing: '0.3em' }}>
          â€” {quote.author}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        className="absolute bottom-16 flex flex-col items-center gap-3"
        style={{ opacity: fading ? 0 : 1, transition: 'opacity 0.9s ease-out' }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: 11, letterSpacing: '0.25em', textTransform: 'uppercase',
            color: '#566074', fontWeight: 500,
          }}
        >
          LOADING NEXT MISSION{dots}
        </div>
        <div className="h-px w-40 overflow-hidden" style={{ background: '#1e2633' }}>
          <div className="h-full discipline-bar-fill" style={{ background: '#22d3ee', width: '100%' }} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import Link from 'next/link'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import PomodoroTimer from './PomodoroTimer'
import QuickNotes from './QuickNotes'
import StudyStreak from './StudyStreak'
import DailyGoal from './DailyGoal'
import QuickFlashcard from './QuickFlashcard'
import { triggerNav } from './NavLoader'

type PanelMode = 'full' | 'focus' | 'progress' | 'quiet'

function getPanelMode(path: string | null): PanelMode {
  if (!path) return 'full'
  if (path.startsWith('/dashboard')) return 'full'
  if (/^\/courses\/[^/]+\/lessons\//.test(path)) return 'focus'
  if (path === '/courses' || /^\/courses\/[^/]+$/.test(path)) return 'progress'
  return 'quiet'
}

export default function ExtensionsPanel() {
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const mode = getPanelMode(pathname)
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setCollapsed(mode === 'quiet')
  }, [pathname, mode])

  if (collapsed) {
    return (
      <aside
        className="hidden xl:flex flex-col w-12 h-full shrink-0"
        style={{
          background: 'rgba(10,14,20,0.55)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
          borderLeft: '1px solid rgba(30,38,51,0.6)',
        }}
      >
        <button
          onClick={() => setCollapsed(false)}
          className="p-3 text-[#22d3ee] hover:bg-[#22d3ee]/10 transition"
          aria-label="Open extensions"
        >
          â—‚
        </button>
        <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#3a4250] rotate-90 mt-8 whitespace-nowrap tracking-[0.3em]">
          EXTENSIONS
        </div>
      </aside>
    )
  }

  return (
    <aside
      className="hidden xl:flex flex-col w-72 h-full overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar shrink-0"
      style={{
        background: 'rgba(10,14,20,0.55)',
        backdropFilter: 'blur(12px)',
        borderLeft: '1px solid rgba(30,38,51,0.6)',
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b shrink-0"
        style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(30,38,51,0.6)' }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse shrink-0" />
          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee]">
            EXTENSIONS
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setCollapsed(true)}
          className="text-[#566074] hover:text-[#e5e9f0] transition text-sm shrink-0"
          aria-label="Collapse extensions"
        >
          â–¸
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 space-y-4">

        {mode === 'full' && (
          <>
            <div className="m-hud-panel">
              <span className="m-hud-scan" />
              <div className="m-hud-header">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee]" />
                  QUICK ACTIONS
                </div>
                <span style={{ color: '#3a4250', opacity: 0.7 }}>04</span>
              </div>
              <div className="p-4">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  {[
                    { href: '/courses', label: 'Courses' },
                    { href: '/community', label: 'Community' },
                    { href: '/library', label: 'Library' },
                    { href: '/profile', label: 'Profile' },
                  ].map(l => (
                    <Link
                      key={l.href}
                      href={l.href}
                      onClick={triggerNav}
                      className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-wider text-center rounded-md py-2.5 transition-all text-[#8892a6] hover:text-[#22d3ee]"
                      style={{
                        border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.18)',
                        background: 'rgba(10,14,20,0.4)',
                      }}
                    >
                      {l.label}
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <StudyStreak />
            <DailyGoal />
            <PomodoroTimer />
            <QuickFlashcard />
            <QuickNotes />
          </>
        )}

        {mode === 'focus' && (
          <>
            <HintCard label="FOCUS SESSION" hint="Timer + notes for this lesson." />
            <PomodoroTimer />
            <QuickNotes />
          </>
        )}

        {mode === 'progress' && (
          <>
            <HintCard label="PROGRESS" hint="Your daily targets and streak." />
            <StudyStreak />
            <DailyGoal />
          </>
        )}

        <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#3a4250] text-center pt-2 pb-4 tracking-[0.3em]">
          MERIDIAN v1.0
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  )
}

function HintCard({ label, hint }: { label: string; hint: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="m-hud-panel">
      <span className="m-hud-scan" />
      <div className="m-hud-header">

          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
          {label}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="px-4 py-3">
        <div className="text-[11px] text-[#8892a6] leading-snug">{hint}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================
FILE: components\HomeScreen.tsx

'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import AcademyBackground from './AcademyBackground'
import LogoEmblem from './LogoEmblem'
import { triggerNav } from './NavLoader'
import {
  unlockAudio,
  isAudioUnlocked,
  playSelectSound,
  playConfirmSound,
  playInitializingSound,
  playTickSound,
  startAmbientHum,
} from '@/lib/sounds'

type MenuItem = {
  href: string
  label: string
  sub: string
  primary?: boolean
  disabled?: boolean
}

const LOADER_MS = 2500

export default function HomeScreen({
  loggedIn,
  continueHref = '/dashboard',
  continueLabel = 'CONTINUE',
  continueSub = 'Resume your journey',
}: {
  loggedIn: boolean
  continueHref?: string
  continueLabel?: string
  continueSub?: string
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<'loading' | 'menu'>('loading')
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0)
  const [dots, setDots] = useState('')
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(0)
  const [pulse, setPulse] = useState(0)

  const lastTickRef = useRef(0)
  const soundPlayedRef = useRef(false)
  const stopAmbientRef = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null)

  // Loading phase
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== 'loading') return

    // Play init sound if audio is already unlocked (returning visit).
    // If not, do NOT set the flag â€” wait for first gesture below.
    if (!soundPlayedRef.current && isAudioUnlocked()) {
      soundPlayedRef.current = true
      playInitializingSound(2200)
    }

    // Retry on first user gesture anywhere on the loading screen
    function retryOnGesture() {
      unlockAudio()
      if (!soundPlayedRef.current) {
        soundPlayedRef.current = true
        playInitializingSound(2200)
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', retryOnGesture, { once: true })
    window.addEventListener('keydown', retryOnGesture, { once: true })

    const start = Date.now()
    const DURATION = 2000
    const tick = setInterval(() => {
      const p = Math.min(100, Math.round(((Date.now() - start) / DURATION) * 100))
      setProgress(p)

      const step = Math.floor(p / 12.5)
      if (step > lastTickRef.current && p < 100) {
        lastTickRef.current = step
        playTickSound(p)
      }

      if (p >= 100) {
        clearInterval(tick)
        setTimeout(() => setPhase('menu'), 320)
      }
    }, 40)

    const dt = setInterval(() => setDots(d => (d.length >= 3 ? '' : d + '.')), 300)
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', retryOnGesture)
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', retryOnGesture)
      clearInterval(tick)
      clearInterval(dt)
    }
  }, [phase])

  // Ambient hum while on the menu
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== 'menu') return

    stopAmbientRef.current = startAmbientHum()

    function onGesture() {
      unlockAudio()
      if (!stopAmbientRef.current) {
        stopAmbientRef.current = startAmbientHum()
      } else {
        stopAmbientRef.current()
        stopAmbientRef.current = startAmbientHum()
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', onGesture, { once: true })
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onGesture, { once: true })

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onGesture)
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onGesture)
      if (stopAmbientRef.current) {
        stopAmbientRef.current()
        stopAmbientRef.current = null
      }
    }
  }, [phase])

  const items: MenuItem[] = loggedIn
    ? [
        { href: continueHref, label: continueLabel, sub: continueSub, primary: true },
        { href: '/courses', label: 'COURSES', sub: 'Browse the curriculum' },
        { href: '/library', label: 'LIBRARY', sub: 'E-books Â· Specs Â· Vault' },
        { href: '/community', label: 'COMMUNITY', sub: 'Discussion channels' },
        { href: '/profile', label: 'PROFILE', sub: 'Account settings' },
      ]
    : [
        { href: '/signup', label: 'NEW GAME', sub: 'Create a free account', primary: true },
        { href: '/login', label: 'CONTINUE', sub: 'Log into an existing account' },
        { href: '/courses', label: 'COURSES', sub: 'Browse the curriculum' },
        { href: '/library', label: 'LIBRARY', sub: 'E-books Â· Specs Â· Vault' },
      ]

  function navigateWithLoader(href: string) {
    unlockAudio()
    playConfirmSound()
    triggerNav()
    setTimeout(() => router.push(href), LOADER_MS)
  }

  function moveSelection(delta: number) {
    unlockAudio()
    setSelected(s => (s + delta + items.length) % items.length)
    setPulse(p => p + 1)
    playSelectSound()
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== 'menu') return

    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      const target = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null
      if (target) {
        const tag = target.tagName.toLowerCase()
        if (tag === 'input' || tag === 'textarea' || tag === 'select' || target.isContentEditable) {
          return
        }
      }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 's' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault()
        moveSelection(1)
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'w' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault()
        moveSelection(-1)
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault()
        const it = items[selected]
        if (!it.disabled) navigateWithLoader(it.href)
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true)
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [phase, selected, items.length])

  if (phase === 'loading') {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <div
          className="absolute inset-0"
          style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at center, #0a1620 0%, #050810 60%, #000 100%)' }}
        />
        <div
          className="absolute pointer-events-none"
          style={{
            top: '15%', left: '50%', transform: 'translateX(-50%)',
            width: '70vw', height: '70vw', maxWidth: 800, maxHeight: 800,
            background: 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(34,211,238,0.10) 0%, transparent 60%)',
          }}
        />
        <div className="relative flex flex-col items-center">
          <LogoEmblem size={280} className="mb-4" />
          <div className="w-72 md:w-96 mt-8">
            <div className="h-[2px] bg-[#1e2633] rounded-full overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className="h-full transition-all duration-75"
                style={{
                  width: `${progress}%`,
                  background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
                  boxShadow: '0 0 12px rgba(34,211,238,0.9)',
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between mt-3 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[#566074]">
              <span>INITIALIZING{dots}</span>
              <span>{progress}%</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="h-screen flex flex-col relative overflow-hidden">
      <AcademyBackground variant="landing" />

      <header
        className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-3 border-b backdrop-blur-md shrink-0"
        style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.10)' }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[#566074]">
          <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#10b981] animate-pulse" />
          ONLINE
        </div>
        <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-8 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[#566074]">
          <span>BUILD v1.0</span>
          <span>EU-WEST</span>
          <span>{new Date().getFullYear()}</span>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="flex-1 min-h-0 flex flex-col items-center px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar">
        <div className="w-full flex flex-col items-center my-auto">
          <LogoEmblem size={200} className="mb-4 sm:mb-6 shrink-0" />

          <div
            className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl overflow-hidden relative shrink-0"
            style={{
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.28)',
              background: 'rgba(6,10,16,0.78)',
              backdropFilter: 'blur(24px)',
              boxShadow: '0 0 80px rgba(34,211,238,0.14), inset 0 0 80px rgba(34,211,238,0.04)',
            }}
          >
            <div
              className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-2.5 border-b"
              style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.15)' }}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee]">
                <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee] animate-pulse" />
                MAIN MENU
              </div>
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#3a4250]">
                {items.length} OPTIONS
              </div>
            </div>

            <ul className="p-1.5">
              {items.map((item, i) => (
                <MenuRow
                  key={item.label}
                  item={item}
                  index={i + 1}
                  selected={selected === i}
                  pulseKey={pulse}
                  onHover={() => {
                    if (!item.disabled && selected !== i) {
                      unlockAudio()
                      setSelected(i)
                      setPulse(p => p + 1)
                      playSelectSound()
                    }
                  }}
                  onActivate={() => navigateWithLoader(item.href)}
                />
              ))}
            </ul>

            <div
              className="h-[1px]"
              style={{
                background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, rgba(34,211,238,0.5) 50%, transparent 100%)',
              }}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 mb-2 flex items-center gap-5 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#566074] shrink-0">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-[#22d3ee]">â†â†‘â†“â†’</span>
              <span>NAVIGATE</span>
            </span>
            <span className="w-px h-3" style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.25)' }} />
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-[#22d3ee]">ENTER</span>
              <span>CONFIRM</span>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      <footer
        className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-2.5 border-t backdrop-blur-md shrink-0"
        style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.10)' }}
      >
        <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[#566074]">
          Â© {new Date().getFullYear()} MERIDIAN
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-6 text-[10px] font-mono tracking-widest">
          <Link href="/about" className="text-[#566074] hover:text-[#22d3ee] transition">
            ABOUT
          </Link>
          <Link href="/faq" className="text-[#566074] hover:text-[#22d3ee] transition">
            FAQ
          </Link>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  )
}

function MenuRow({
  item, index, selected, pulseKey, onHover, onActivate,
}: {
  item: MenuItem
  index: number
  selected: boolean
  pulseKey: number
  onHover: () => void
  onActivate: () => void
}) {
  const isPrimary = item.primary

  if (item.disabled) {
    return (
      <li>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 opacity-30 cursor-not-allowed"
          style={{ border: '1px solid transparent' }}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono w-5 text-center text-[#3a4250]">
              {String(index).padStart(2, '0')}
            </span>
            <div>
              <div className="text-sm font-bold tracking-[0.2em] text-[#566074]">{item.label}</div>
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#3a4250]">{item.sub}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-widest text-[#3a4250]">LOCKED</span>
        </div>
      </li>
    )
  }

  return (
    <li className="relative">
      {selected && (
        <>
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-2.5 h-2.5 z-10"
            style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 right-0 w-2.5 h-2.5 z-10"
            style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-2.5 h-2.5 z-10"
            style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
          <span className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-2.5 h-2.5 z-10"
            style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }} />
        </>
      )}

      <button
        type="button"
        onMouseEnter={onHover}
        onClick={onActivate}
        key={pulseKey}
        className={`group w-full text-left flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.985] ${
          selected ? 'meridian-menu-flash' : ''
        }`}
        style={{
          border: selected ? '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.55)' : '1px solid transparent',
          background: selected
            ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.22) 0%, rgba(139,92,246,0.08) 100%)'
            : 'transparent',
          boxShadow: selected
            ? '0 0 30px rgba(34,211,238,0.28), inset 0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.06)'
            : 'none',
        }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 min-w-0">
          <span
            className="text-[11px] font-mono transition-all duration-150 shrink-0"
            style={{
              color: selected ? '#22d3ee' : '#3a4250',
              transform: selected ? 'translateX(0)' : 'translateX(-4px)',
              opacity: selected ? 1 : 0,
              width: 10,
              display: 'inline-block',
            }}
          >
            â–¸
          </span>

          <span
            className="text-[10px] font-mono w-5 text-center transition-colors shrink-0"
            style={{
              color: selected ? '#22d3ee' : isPrimary ? '#22d3ee' : '#3a4250',
              textShadow: selected ? '0 0 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.7)' : 'none',
            }}
          >
            {String(index).padStart(2, '0')}
          </span>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div
              className="text-sm font-bold tracking-[0.2em] truncate transition-colors"
              style={{
                color: selected ? '#ffffff' : isPrimary ? '#e5e9f0' : '#8892a6',
                textShadow: selected ? '0 0 12px rgba(34,211,238,0.4)' : 'none',
              }}
            >
              {item.label}
            </div>
            <div
              className="text-[10px] font-mono truncate transition-colors"
              style={{
                color: selected ? '#22d3ee' : isPrimary ? 'rgba(34,211,238,0.75)' : '#566074',
              }}
            >
              {item.sub}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <span
          className="text-base transition-all shrink-0 ml-3"
          style={{
            color: selected ? '#22d3ee' : '#3a4250',
            opacity: selected ? 1 : 0.6,
            transform: selected ? 'translateX(3px)' : 'translateX(0)',
          }}
        >
          â–¸
        </span>
      </button>
    </li>
  )
}
