// FILE: components/MobileNav.tsx
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import ThemeToggle from './ThemeToggle'
import LogoEmblem from './LogoEmblem'
import { triggerNav } from './NavLoader'

const navItems = [
  { href: '/dashboard', label: 'OVERVIEW' },
  { href: '/courses', label: 'MY COURSES' },
  { href: '/library', label: 'LIBRARY' },
  { href: '/community', label: 'COMMUNITY' },
  { href: '/profile', label: 'PROFILE' },
]

export default function MobileNav({ userEmail }: { userEmail?: string }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()

  async function quitToMainMenu() {
    await supabase.auth.signOut()
    router.push('/')
    router.refresh()
  }

  return (
    <>
      <header
        className="md:hidden flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b sticky top-0"
        style={{
          borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.10)',
          background: 'rgba(6,10,16,0.72)',
          zIndex: 25,
        }}
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
          aria-label="Open menu"
          className="p-2 -ml-2 text-2xl text-[#8892a6] hover:text-[#e5e9f0] active:scale-95 transition"
        >
          ☰
        </button>

        <Link href="/" onClick={triggerNav} className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <LogoEmblem size={38} />
          <span className="text-sm font-bold tracking-[0.2em] text-[#e5e9f0]">
            MERIDIAN
          </span>
        </Link>

        <ThemeToggle compact />
      </header>

      {open && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] md:hidden">
          <div
            className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm"
            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
          />
          <aside
            className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-72 flex flex-col shadow-2xl"
            style={{
              background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at top, #0a1620 0%, #050810 100%)',
              borderRight: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.15)',
            }}
          >
            <div
              className="px-6 py-5 border-b flex items-center justify-between"
              style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.10)' }}
            >
              <Link
                href="/"
                onClick={() => { setOpen(false); triggerNav() }}
                className="flex items-center gap-2.5"
              >
                <LogoEmblem size={48} />
                <div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold tracking-tight leading-none text-[#e5e9f0]">
                    MERIDIAN
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] tracking-[0.3em] text-[#22d3ee] mt-1">
                    ACADEMY
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Link>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                className="text-[#566074] text-2xl leading-none hover:text-[#e5e9f0]"
              >
                ×
              </button>
            </div>

            <nav className="flex-1 p-4 space-y-1">
              {navItems.map((item, i) => {
                const active = pathname.startsWith(item.href)
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={item.href}
                    href={item.href}
                    onClick={() => { setOpen(false); triggerNav() }}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.18em] transition-all"
                    style={{
                      color: active ? '#e5e9f0' : '#8892a6',
                      background: active
                        ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.16) 0%, rgba(139,92,246,0.05) 100%)'
                        : 'transparent',
                      border: active ? '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.35)' : '1px solid transparent',
                    }}
                  >
                    <span
                      className="text-[9px] font-mono w-4"
                      style={{ color: active ? '#22d3ee' : '#3a4250' }}
                    >
                      {String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
                    </span>
                    <span>{item.label}</span>
                    {active && (
                      <span
                        className="ml-auto w-1 h-1 rounded-full animate-pulse"
                        style={{ background: '#22d3ee' }}
                      />
                    )}
                  </Link>
                )
              })}
            </nav>

            <div
              className="p-4 border-t space-y-3"
              style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.10)' }}
            >
              <ThemeToggle />
              {userEmail && (
                <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-[#3a4250] truncate">
                  {userEmail}
                </div>
              )}
              <button
                onClick={quitToMainMenu}
                className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] hover:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-500/10 transition flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <span className="text-base leading-none">⏻</span>
                <span>QUIT TO MAIN MENU</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

================================================================
FILE: components\NavLoader.tsx

'use client'
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import { pickQuote, type DisciplineQuote } from '@/lib/discipline'
import LogoEmblem from './LogoEmblem'
import { unlockAudio, playTransitionSound } from '@/lib/sounds'

const EVENT_NAME = 'meridian:nav-start'

export function triggerNav() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
  const fn = (window as unknown as { __meridianNav?: () => void }).__meridianNav
  if (typeof fn === 'function') {
    fn()
  } else {
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event(EVENT_NAME))
  }
}

export default function NavLoader() {
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false)
  const [fading, setFading] = useState(false)
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0)
  const [quote, setQuote] = useState<DisciplineQuote | null>(null)
  const [dots, setDots] = useState('')

  const prevPathRef = useRef(pathname)
  const startedAtRef = useRef(0)
  const progressRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null)
  const fallbackRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    function clearProgress() {
      if (progressRef.current) {
        clearInterval(progressRef.current)
        progressRef.current = null
      }
    }
    function clearFallback() {
      if (fallbackRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(fallbackRef.current)
        fallbackRef.current = null
      }
    }

    function start() {
      clearProgress()
      clearFallback()
      setQuote(pickQuote())
      setProgress(0)
      setFading(false)
      setVisible(true)
      startedAtRef.current = Date.now()

      unlockAudio()
      playTransitionSound(1400)

      progressRef.current = setInterval(() => {
        const elapsed = Date.now() - startedAtRef.current
        const p = Math.min(88, Math.round((elapsed / 2200) * 88))
        setProgress(p)
        if (p >= 88 && progressRef.current) {
          clearInterval(progressRef.current)
          progressRef.current = null
        }
      }, 30)

      fallbackRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        setProgress(100)
        setFading(true)
        setTimeout(() => {
          setVisible(false)
          setFading(false)
        }, 320)
      }, 8000)
    }

    ;(window as unknown as { __meridianNav?: () => void }).__meridianNav = start
    window.addEventListener(EVENT_NAME, start)

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(EVENT_NAME, start)
      delete (window as unknown as { __meridianNav?: () => void }).__meridianNav
      clearProgress()
      clearFallback()
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (prevPathRef.current === pathname) return
    prevPathRef.current = pathname
    if (!visible) return

    const paintTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      setProgress(100)
      setFading(true)
      setTimeout(() => {
        setVisible(false)
        setFading(false)
      }, 400)
    }, 250)

    return () => clearTimeout(paintTimer)
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [pathname])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) return
    const id = setInterval(() => setDots(d => (d.length >= 3 ? '' : d + '.')), 320)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
  }, [visible])

  if (!visible || !quote) return null

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-8 pointer-events-none"
      style={{
        zIndex: 9999,
        opacity: fading ? 0 : 1,
        transition: 'opacity 0.38s ease-out',
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0"
        style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at center, #0a1620 0%, #050810 60%, #000 100%)' }}
      />
      <div
        className="absolute pointer-events-none"
        style={{
          top: '15%', left: '50%', transform: 'translateX(-50%)',
          width: '70vw', height: '70vw', maxWidth: 800, maxHeight: 800,
          background: 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(34,211,238,0.10) 0%, transparent 60%)',
        }}
      />

      <div className="relative flex flex-col items-center max-w-2xl text-center">
        <LogoEmblem size={180} className="mb-6" />

        <div
          className="text-xl md:text-2xl leading-relaxed italic mb-6"
          style={{
            fontFamily: 'Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif',
            color: '#e5e9f0',
            textShadow: '0 0 24px rgba(34,211,238,0.15)',
          }}
        >
          &ldquo;{quote.text}&rdquo;
        </div>

        <div
          className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.4em] uppercase"
          style={{ color: '#22d3ee' }}
        >
          â€” {quote.author}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="absolute bottom-16 flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: 10,
            letterSpacing: '0.4em',
            textTransform: 'uppercase',
            color: '#566074',
            fontWeight: 500,
          }}
        >
          LOADING{dots}
        </div>
        <div className="h-[2px] w-56 overflow-hidden rounded-full" style={{ background: '#1e2633' }}>
          <div
            className="h-full transition-all duration-75"
            style={{
              width: `${progress}%`,
              background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
              boxShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.9)',
            }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================
FILE: components\PomodoroTimer.tsx
================================================================

'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

type SessionType = 'quick' | 'focus' | 'deep'
const SESSIONS: Record<SessionType, { label: string; mins: number; accent: string }> = {
  quick: { label: '15', mins: 15, accent: '#10b981' },
  focus: { label: '25', mins: 25, accent: '#22d3ee' },
  deep:  { label: '50', mins: 50, accent: '#8b5cf6' },
}
const BREAK_MINS = 5

export default function PomodoroTimer() {
  const [type, setType] = useState<SessionType>('focus')
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'work' | 'break'>('work')
  const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(SESSIONS.focus.mins * 60)
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false)
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState(0)
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)
  const intervalRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const today = new Date().toDateString()
    const storedDate = localStorage.getItem('m-pomo-date')
    const storedCount = localStorage.getItem('m-pomo-count')
    if (storedDate === today && storedCount) {
      setSessions(parseInt(storedCount))
    } else {
      localStorage.setItem('m-pomo-date', today)
      localStorage.setItem('m-pomo-count', '0')
      setSessions(0)
    }
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return
    intervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      setSeconds((s) => {
        if (s <= 1) {
          if (mode === 'work') {
            // completed a work session
            const today = new Date().toDateString()
            const storedDate = localStorage.getItem('m-pomo-date')
            const storedCount = localStorage.getItem('m-pomo-count')
            const current = storedDate === today && storedCount ? parseInt(storedCount) : 0
            const next = current + 1
            localStorage.setItem('m-pomo-date', today)
            localStorage.setItem('m-pomo-count', String(next))
            setSessions(next)
            setMode('break')
            return BREAK_MINS * 60
          } else {
            setMode('work')
            return SESSIONS[type].mins * 60
          }
        }
        return s - 1
      })
    }, 1000)
    return () => {
      if (intervalRef.current) clearInterval(intervalRef.current)
    }
  }, [running, mode, type])

  function selectType(t: SessionType) {
    setRunning(false)
    setType(t)
    setMode('work')
    setSeconds(SESSIONS[t].mins * 60)
  }

  function toggle() { setRunning((r) => !r) }

  function reset() {
    setRunning(false)
    setMode('work')
    setSeconds(SESSIONS[type].mins * 60)
  }

  if (!mounted) return null

  const total = mode === 'work' ? SESSIONS[type].mins * 60 : BREAK_MINS * 60
  const elapsed = total - seconds
  const pct = (elapsed / total) * 100
  const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60)
  const secs = seconds % 60
  const isWork = mode === 'work'
  const accent = isWork ? SESSIONS[type].accent : '#f59e0b'

  // ring
  const R = 40
  const C = 2 * Math.PI * R
  const dash = (pct / 100) * C

  return (
    <div className="m-hud-panel">
      <span className="m-hud-scan" />
      <div className="m-hud-header">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span
            className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full animate-pulse"
            style={{ background: accent }}
          />
          <span style={{ color: accent }}>{isWork ? 'FOCUS' : 'BREAK'}</span>
        </div>
        <span style={{ color: '#3a4250', opacity: 0.7 }}>{sessions} DONE</span>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4">
        {/* Session type tabs */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 mb-4">
          {(Object.keys(SESSIONS) as SessionType[]).map((k) => {
            const s = SESSIONS[k]
            const active = k === type
            return (
              <button
                key={k}
                onClick={() => selectType(k)}
                className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] py-1.5 rounded transition-all"
                style={{
                  color: active ? s.accent : '#566074',
                  background: active ? 'rgba(34,211,238,0.06)' : 'transparent',
                  border: '1px solid ' + (active ? s.accent + '55' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.12)'),
                }}
              >
                {s.label} MIN
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Ring + time */}
        <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center mb-4" style={{ height: 110 }}>
          <svg width="110" height="110" className="absolute -rotate-90">
            <circle cx="55" cy="55" r={R} fill="none" stroke="rgba(30,38,51,0.9)" strokeWidth="3" />
            <circle
              cx="55" cy="55" r={R}
              fill="none"
              stroke={accent}
              strokeWidth="3"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeDasharray={C}
              strokeDashoffset={C - dash}
              style={{
                transition: 'stroke-dashoffset 0.5s linear',
                filter: 'drop-shadow(0 0 6px ' + accent + 'aa)',
              }}
            />
          </svg>
          <div className="text-center relative">
            <div
              className="text-3xl font-bold tabular-nums leading-none"
              style={{ color: '#a5f3fc', textShadow: '0 0 20px ' + accent + '66' }}
            >
              {String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:{String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#3a4250] mt-1.5">
              {isWork ? 'WORK' : 'REST'}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Controls */}
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={toggle}
            className="flex-1 text-black text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase rounded-md py-2.5 transition"
            style={{ background: accent }}
          >
            {running ? 'Pause' : 'Start'}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={reset}
            className="px-3 border border-[#2d3748] text-[#8892a6] hover:text-[#e5e9f0] hover:border-[#22d3ee]/40 text-xs rounded-md py-2 transition"
            aria-label="Reset"
          >
            â†º
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: components\QuickFlashcard.tsx

'use client'

import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from 'react'

type Cat = 'verbs' | 'nouns' | 'phrases'

const CAT_LABELS: Record<Cat, string> = {
  verbs: 'VERBS',
  nouns: 'NOUNS',
  phrases: 'PHRASES',
}

const WORDS: Record<Cat, { nl: string; en: string }[]> = {
  verbs: [
    { nl: 'leren',    en: 'to learn' },
    { nl: 'spreken',  en: 'to speak' },

    { nl: 'vriend', en: 'friend' },

    { nl: 'avond',  en: 'evening' },
    { nl: 'tijd',   en: 'time' },
    { nl: 'wereld', en: 'world' },
  ],
  phrases: [
    { nl: 'goedemorgen',   en: 'good morning' },
    { nl: 'dank je',       en: 'thank you' },
    { nl: 'alsjeblieft',   en: 'please' },
    { nl: 'tot ziens',     en: 'goodbye' },
    { nl: 'hoe gaat het?', en: 'how are you?' },
    { nl: 'goedenavond',   en: 'good evening' },
    { nl: 'het spijt me',  en: 'I am sorry' },
    { nl: 'geen probleem', en: 'no problem' },
  ],
}

export default function QuickFlashcard() {
  const [cat, setCat] = useState<Cat>('verbs')
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0)
  const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState(false)
  const [seen, setSeen] = useState<Record<Cat, Set<number>>>({
    verbs: new Set(),
    nouns: new Set(),
    phrases: new Set(),
  })

  const list = WORDS[cat]
  const card = list[index % list.length]
  const seenCount = seen[cat].size

  const next = useCallback(() => {
    setIndex((i) => (i + 1) % list.length)
    setFlipped(false)
  }, [list.length])

  function selectCat(c: Cat) {
    setCat(c)
    setIndex(0)
    setFlipped(false)
  }

  function flip() {
    setFlipped((f) => !f)
    // mark as seen when revealed
    setSeen((prev) => {
      const nextSets = { ...prev }
      const s = new Set(nextSets[cat])
      s.add(index)
      nextSets[cat] = s
      return nextSets
    })
  }

  const progress = (seenCount / list.length) * 100

  return (
    <div className="m-hud-panel">
      <span className="m-hud-scan" />
      <div className="m-hud-header">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee]" />
          FLASHCARD
        </div>
        <span style={{ color: '#566074' }}>{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}/{String(list.length).padStart(2, '0')}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4">
        {/* Category tabs */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 mb-3">
          {(Object.keys(CAT_LABELS) as Cat[]).map((k) => {
            const active = k === cat
            return (
              <button
                key={k}
                onClick={() => selectCat(k)}
                className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] py-1.5 rounded transition-all"
                style={{
                  color: active ? '#a5f3fc' : '#566074',
                  background: active ? 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)' : 'transparent',
                  border: '1px solid ' + (active ? 'rgba(34,211,238,0.45)' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.12)'),
                }}
              >
                {CAT_LABELS[k]}
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Card */}
        <button
          onClick={flip}
          className="w-full h-28 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center px-4 transition mb-3 group"
          style={{
            border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.25)',
            background: flipped
              ? 'rgba(139,92,246,0.05)'
              : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.03)',
          }}
        >
          <div className="text-center">
            {flipped ? (
              <div className="text-base font-semibold text-[#c4b5fd]">
                {card.en}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div
                className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#a5f3fc]"
                style={{ textShadow: '0 0 16px rgba(34,211,238,0.55)' }}
              >
                {card.nl}
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="text-[9px] font-mono text-[#3a4250] mt-2.5 tracking-[0.25em] group-hover:text-[#566074] transition">
              {flipped ? 'CLICK TO FLIP BACK' : 'CLICK TO REVEAL'}
            </div>
          </div>
        </button>

        {/* Progress + next */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-1">
          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] shrink-0">
            {seenCount}/{list.length}
          </span>
          <div className="flex-1 h-[2px] rounded-full overflow-hidden"
            style={{ background: 'rgba(30,38,51,0.9)' }}>
            <div
              className="h-full transition-all duration-500"
              style={{
                width: progress + '%',
                background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, #22d3ee, #8b5cf6)',
              }}
            />
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={next}
            className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#22d3ee] hover:text-white transition shrink-0"
          >
            NEXT â†’
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: components\QuickNotes.tsx

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

type Tab = 'notes' | 'dutch' | 'business'

const TABS: Record<Tab, { label: string; placeholder: string }> = {
  notes:    { label: 'NOTES',    placeholder: 'Jot down thoughts, questions, ideas...' },
  dutch:    { label: 'DUTCH',    placeholder: 'New words, phrases, grammar rules...' },
  business: { label: 'BUSINESS', placeholder: 'Concepts, frameworks, insights...' },
}

export default function QuickNotes() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('notes')
  const [texts, setTexts] = useState<Record<Tab, string>>({
    notes: '',
    dutch: '',
    business: '',
  })
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false)
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const loaded: Record<Tab, string> = {
      notes: localStorage.getItem('m-notes-notes') || '',
      dutch: localStorage.getItem('m-notes-dutch') || '',
      business: localStorage.getItem('m-notes-business') || '',
    }
    setTexts(loaded)
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mounted) return
    setSaved(false)
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      localStorage.setItem('m-notes-notes', texts.notes)
      localStorage.setItem('m-notes-dutch', texts.dutch)
      localStorage.setItem('m-notes-business', texts.business)
      setSaved(true)
      setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 1200)
    }, 600)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [texts, mounted])

  if (!mounted) return null

  const current = texts[tab]
  const words = current.trim() ? current.trim().split(/\s+/).length : 0

  function clear() {
    if (!window.confirm('Clear this note?')) return
    setTexts((t) => ({ ...t, [tab]: '' }))
  }

  return (
    <div className="m-hud-panel">
      <span className="m-hud-scan" />
      <div className="m-hud-header">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee]" />
          QUICK NOTES
        </div>
        {saved ? (
          <span style={{ color: '#10b981' }}>SAVED</span>
        ) : (
          <span style={{ color: '#3a4250', opacity: 0.7 }}>{words}w</span>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="p-4">
        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 mb-3">
          {(Object.keys(TABS) as Tab[]).map((k) => {
            const active = k === tab
            const hasContent = texts[k].trim().length > 0
            return (
              <button
                key={k}
                onClick={() => setTab(k)}
                className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] py-1.5 rounded transition-all relative"
                style={{
                  color: active ? '#a5f3fc' : '#566074',
                  background: active ? 'rgba(34,211,238,0.08)' : 'transparent',
                  border: '1px solid ' + (active ? 'rgba(34,211,238,0.45)' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.12)'),
                }}
              >
                {TABS[k].label}
                {hasContent && !active && (
                  <span
                    className="absolute top-1 right-1 rounded-full"
                    style={{ width: 4, height: 4, background: '#22d3ee' }}
                  />
                )}
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>

        <textarea
          value={current}
          onChange={(e) => setTexts((t) => ({ ...t, [tab]: e.target.value }))}
          placeholder={TABS[tab].placeholder}
          rows={6}
          className="w-full rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-[#e5e9f0] placeholder:text-[#3a4250] outline-none resize-none font-mono leading-relaxed transition-colors mb-2"
          style={{
            background: 'rgba(5,8,16,0.55)',
            border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.2)',
          }}
          onFocus={(e) => { e.target.style.borderColor = 'rgba(34,211,238,0.5)' }}
          onBlur={(e) => { e.target.style.borderColor = 'rgba(34,211,238,0.2)' }}
        />

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#3a4250]">
            {words} WORDS Â· {current.length} CHARS
          </span>
          <button
            onClick={clear}
            className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] hover:text-red-400 transition"
          >
            CLEAR
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import ThemeToggle from './ThemeToggle'
import LogoEmblem from './LogoEmblem'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/client'
import { triggerNav } from './NavLoader'
import { unlockAudio, playSelectSound, playConfirmSound } from '@/lib/sounds'
import { getActiveZone, onZoneChange } from '@/lib/navZone'

const NAV = [
  { href: '/dashboard', label: 'OVERVIEW' },
  { href: '/courses', label: 'MY COURSES' },
  { href: '/community', label: 'COMMUNITY' },
  { href: '/library', label: 'LIBRARY' },
  { href: '/profile', label: 'PROFILE' },
]

export default function Sidebar({ userEmail }: { userEmail?: string }) {
  const path = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()
  const supabase = createClient()
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(0)
  const [zoneActive, setZoneActive] = useState(getActiveZone() === 'sidebar')
  const [hovered, setHovered] = useState<number | null>(null)

  const selectedRef = useRef(0)
  const routerRef = useRef(router)

  useEffect(() => { selectedRef.current = selected }, [selected])
  useEffect(() => { routerRef.current = router }, [router])

  useEffect(() => {
    const idx = NAV.findIndex(n => path === n.href || path?.startsWith(n.href + '/'))
    if (idx >= 0) setSelected(idx)
  }, [path])

  useEffect(() => {
    return onZoneChange(z => setZoneActive(z === 'sidebar'))
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!zoneActive) return

    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      const active = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null
      if (active) {
        const tag = active.tagName.toLowerCase()
        if (tag === 'input' || tag === 'textarea' || tag === 'select' || active.isContentEditable) {
          return
        }
      }

      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault()
        unlockAudio()
        playSelectSound()
        setSelected(s => {
          const next = (s + 1) % NAV.length
          selectedRef.current = next
          return next
        })
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault()
        unlockAudio()
        playSelectSound()
        setSelected(s => {
          const next = (s - 1 + NAV.length) % NAV.length
          selectedRef.current = next
          return next
        })
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault()
        unlockAudio()
        playConfirmSound()
        const item = NAV[selectedRef.current]
        if (item) {
          triggerNav()
          routerRef.current.push(item.href)
        }
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, true)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, true)
  }, [zoneActive])

  async function quitToMainMenu() {
    await supabase.auth.signOut()
    router.push('/')
    router.refresh()
  }

  return (
    <aside
      data-nav-zone="sidebar"
      className="hidden md:flex md:flex-col w-64 shrink-0 h-full relative overflow-hidden"
      style={{
        background:
          'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(8,12,20,0.72) 0%, rgba(6,10,16,0.62) 40%, rgba(4,8,14,0.85) 100%)',
        borderRight: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.12)',
        backdropFilter: 'blur(14px)',
      }}
    >
      {/* Top scan line */}
      <span
        className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 h-px z-20"
        style={{
          background:
            'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, rgba(34,211,238,0.85) 50%, transparent 100%)',
          opacity: 0.75,
        }}
      />

      {/* Faint grid overlay */}
      <span
        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-0"
        style={{
          opacity: 0.06,
          backgroundImage:
            'linear-gradient(rgba(34,211,238,0.6) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.6) 1px, transparent 1px)',
          backgroundSize: '28px 28px',
          maskImage:
            'radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 25%, rgba(0,0,0,0.9) 0%, transparent 80%)',
          WebkitMaskImage:
            'radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 25%, rgba(0,0,0,0.9) 0%, transparent 80%)',
        }}
      />

      {/* Top-left corner bracket */}
      <span
        className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-3 h-3 z-20"
        style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.6)', borderLeft: '1.5px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.6)' }}
      />
      {/* Bottom-right corner bracket */}
      <span
        className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3 h-3 z-20"
        style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.6)', borderRight: '1.5px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.6)' }}
      />

      {/* LOGO / HEADER */}
      <div
        className="p-4 border-b shrink-0 relative z-10"
        style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.10)' }}
      >
        <Link
          href="/"
          onClick={() => {
            unlockAudio()
            playConfirmSound()
            triggerNav()
          }}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 group relative rounded-md px-2 py-2 -mx-1 transition-all hover:bg-[rgba(34,211,238,0.04)]"
        >
          <span
            className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-[2px] rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
            style={{
              height: 0,
              background: '#22d3ee',
              boxShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.8)',
            }}
          />
          <span className="relative shrink-0">
            <span
              className="absolute inset-0 rounded-full opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity pointer-events-none"
              style={{
                background: 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(34,211,238,0.28) 0%, transparent 70%)',
                filter: 'blur(10px)',
              }}
            />
            <LogoEmblem size={44} />
          </span>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.22em] text-[#e5e9f0] leading-none">
              MERIDIAN
            </div>
            <div className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.42em] text-[#22d3ee] mt-1">
              ACADEMY
            </div>
          </div>
        </Link>

        {/* HUD strip */}
        <div
          className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between px-2 py-1.5 rounded"
          style={{
            border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.14)',
            background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.03)',
          }}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span
              className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full"
              style={{ background: '#10b981', boxShadow: '0 0 6px rgba(16,185,129,0.8)' }}
            />
            <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074]">
              ONLINE
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#3a4250]">
            v1.0
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* SECTION LABEL */}
      <div className="px-5 pt-4 pb-2 relative z-10">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.35em] text-[#3a4250]">
            PRIMARY NAV
          </span>
          <span
            className="flex-1 h-px"
            style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.35) 0%, transparent 100%)' }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* NAV */}
      <nav className="flex-1 px-3 pb-4 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar relative z-10">
        {NAV.map((item, i) => {
          const active = path === item.href || path?.startsWith(item.href + '/')
          const isSelected = selected === i
          const showSelected = isSelected && zoneActive
          const isHover = hovered === i

          return (
            <Link
              key={item.href}
              href={item.href}
              onClick={() => {
                unlockAudio()
                playConfirmSound()
                triggerNav()
              }}
              onMouseEnter={() => {
                setHovered(i)
                if (selected !== i) setSelected(i)
              }}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(null)}
              className="group relative flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.18em] transition-all duration-200"
              style={{
                color: showSelected || active ? '#e5e9f0' : isHover ? '#c8d0dd' : '#8892a6',
                background: showSelected
                  ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.18) 0%, rgba(139,92,246,0.05) 100%)'
                  : active
                  ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.10) 0%, rgba(139,92,246,0.03) 100%)'
                  : isHover
                  ? 'rgba(34,211,238,0.04)'
                  : 'transparent',
                border: showSelected
                  ? '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.55)'
                  : active
                  ? '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.25)'
                  : '1px solid transparent',
                boxShadow: showSelected
                  ? '0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.20), inset 0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.03)'
                  : 'none',
                transform: showSelected || isHover ? 'translateX(2px)' : 'translateX(0)',
              }}
            >
              {/* Left rail indicator */}
              <span
                className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-[2px] rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                style={{
                  height: showSelected || active ? 18 : isHover ? 10 : 0,
                  background: showSelected || active ? '#22d3ee' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.5)',
                  boxShadow: showSelected ? '0 0 10px rgba(34,211,238,0.9)' : 'none',
                }}
              />

              {/* Corner ticks (shown when selected) */}
              {showSelected && (
                <>
                  <span
                    className="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0 w-1.5 h-1.5"
                    style={{ borderTop: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderLeft: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }}
                  />
                  <span
                    className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-1.5 h-1.5"
                    style={{ borderBottom: '1.5px solid #22d3ee', borderRight: '1.5px solid #22d3ee' }}
                  />
                </>
              )}

              {/* Number */}
              <span
                className="text-[9px] font-mono transition-colors shrink-0 w-4 tabular-nums"
                style={{
                  color: showSelected ? '#22d3ee' : active ? '#22d3ee' : isHover ? '#8892a6' : '#3a4250',
                  textShadow: showSelected ? '0 0 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.7)' : 'none',
                }}
              >
                {String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
              </span>

              {/* Label */}
              <span className="transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 flex-1 truncate">
                {item.label}
              </span>

              {/* Right state */}
              {showSelected ? (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                  <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#22d3ee]">
                    â–¸
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full animate-pulse"
                    style={{ background: '#22d3ee', boxShadow: '0 0 6px rgba(34,211,238,0.9)' }}
                  />
                </span>
              ) : active ? (
                <span
                  className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full shrink-0 animate-pulse"
                  style={{ background: '#22d3ee', boxShadow: '0 0 6px rgba(34,211,238,0.9)' }}
                />
              ) : isHover ? (
                <span className="text-[10px] text-[#22d3ee] shrink-0 opacity-60">â–¸</span>
              ) : null}
            </Link>
          )
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* FOOTER */}
      <div
        className="p-4 border-t shrink-0 relative z-10"
        style={{ borderColor: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.10)' }}
      >
        {/* Nav hints */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <span
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 rounded-md"
            style={{
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.18)',
              background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.03)',
            }}
          >
            <span className="text-[10px] leading-none text-[#22d3ee]">â†â†‘â†“â†’</span>
            <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#566074]">NAV</span>
          </span>
          <span
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 rounded-md"
            style={{
              border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.18)',
              background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.03)',
            }}
          >
            <span className="text-[10px] leading-none text-[#22d3ee]">â†µ</span>
            <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#566074]">GO</span>
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Quit button */}
        <button
          onClick={quitToMainMenu}
          className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 rounded-md transition-all group relative overflow-hidden mb-3 flex items-center gap-2.5"
          style={{
            border: '1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.18)',
            background: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.02)',
            color: '#566074',
          }}
        >
          <span
            className="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-[2px] transition-opacity opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
            style={{ background: '#ef4444', boxShadow: '0 0 10px rgba(239,68,68,0.8)' }}
          />
          <span className="text-base leading-none text-[#ef4444] opacity-70 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
            â»
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] group-hover:text-[#fca5a5] transition-colors">
            QUIT TO MAIN MENU
          </span>
        </button>

        {/* User + theme row */}
        <div
          className="rounded-md px-3 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
          style={{
            border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.14)',
            background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.02)',
          }}
        >
          <ThemeToggle />
          {userEmail && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
              <span
                className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                style={{ background: '#10b981', boxShadow: '0 0 6px rgba(16,185,129,0.7)' }}
              />
              <div
                className="text-[10px] font-mono truncate max-w-[110px] tracking-wider"
                style={{ color: '#8892a6' }}
              >
                {userEmail.split('@')[0]}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  )
}

// FILE: components/Starfield.tsx
'use client'

export default function Starfield() {
  const stars = Array.from({ length: 70 }, (_, i) => ({
    id: i,
    left: `${(i * 37 + 7) % 100}%`,
    top: `${(i * 53 + 13) % 100}%`,
    size: 1 + (i % 2),
    delay: `${(i % 8) * 0.35}s`,
    duration: `${4 + (i % 6)}s`,
    bright: i % 5 === 0,
  }))

  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none overflow-hidden">
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0"
        style={{
          background:
            'radial-gradient(ellipse at top, rgba(34,211,238,0.05) 0%, transparent 60%)',
        }}
      />
      {stars.map(s => (
        <span
          key={s.id}
          className="absolute rounded-full"
          style={{
            left: s.left,
            top: s.top,
            width: s.size,
            height: s.size,
            background: s.bright ? '#e5e9f0' : '#22d3ee',
            boxShadow: s.bright
              ? '0 0 6px rgba(229,233,240,0.7)'
              : '0 0 5px rgba(34,211,238,0.75)',
            animation: `starTwinkle ${s.duration} ease-in-out infinite`,
            animationDelay: s.delay,
          }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

const TIERS = [
  { min: 0,  label: 'INITIATE', accent: '#566074' },
  { min: 1,  label: 'BRONZE',   accent: '#cd7c4a' },
  { min: 3,  label: 'SILVER',   accent: '#a5b4c4' },
  { min: 7,  label: 'GOLD',     accent: '#f59e0b' },
  { min: 14, label: 'PLATINUM', accent: '#22d3ee' },
  { min: 30, label: 'MERIDIAN', accent: '#a5f3fc' },
]
const WEEK_LABELS = ['M', 'T', 'W', 'T', 'F', 'S', 'S']

function getTier(days: number) {
  let t = TIERS[0]
  for (const tier of TIERS) if (days >= tier.min) t = tier
  return t
}

export default function StudyStreak() {
  const [streak, setStreak] = useState(0)
  const [best, setBest] = useState(0)
  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0)
  const [checkedIn, setCheckedIn] = useState(false)
  const [week, setWeek] = useState<boolean[]>(Array(7).fill(false))
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  function buildWeek(streakVal: number, todayDone: boolean) {
    const w = Array(7).fill(false)
    // index 0 = Monday ... 6 = Sunday
    const jsDay = new Date().getDay() // 0=Sun
    const displayIdx = jsDay === 0 ? 6 : jsDay - 1
    const effective = todayDone ? streakVal : Math.max(0, streakVal)
    for (let i = 0; i < 7 && i <= displayIdx; i++) {
      if (i < effective) w[displayIdx - i] = true
    }
    return w
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    const today = new Date().toDateString()
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('m-streak')
    const lastCheck = localStorage.getItem('m-streak-last')
    const storedBest = localStorage.getItem('m-streak-best')
    const storedTotal = localStorage.getItem('m-streak-total')
    const parsed = stored ? parseInt(stored) : 0
    const bestVal = storedBest ? parseInt(storedBest) : 0
    const totalVal = storedTotal ? parseInt(storedTotal) : 0

    setBest(bestVal)
    setTotal(totalVal)

    if (lastCheck === today) {
      setStreak(parsed)
      setCheckedIn(true)
      setWeek(buildWeek(parsed, true))
    } else {
      const yesterday = new Date(Date.now() - 86400000).toDateString()
      const carried = lastCheck === yesterday ? parsed : 0
      setStreak(carried)
      setCheckedIn(false)
      setWeek(buildWeek(carried, false))
    }
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  function checkIn() {
    const today = new Date().toDateString()
    const lastCheck = localStorage.getItem('m-streak-last')
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('m-streak')
    const parsed = stored ? parseInt(stored) : 0
    const yesterday = new Date(Date.now() - 86400000).toDateString()
    const nextStreak = lastCheck === yesterday ? parsed + 1 : 1

    const newBest = Math.max(nextStreak, best)
    const newTotal = total + 1

    localStorage.setItem('m-streak', String(nextStreak))
    localStorage.setItem('m-streak-last', today)
    localStorage.setItem('m-streak-best', String(newBest))
    localStorage.setItem('m-streak-total', String(newTotal))

    setStreak(nextStreak)
    setBest(newBest)
    setTotal(newTotal)
    setCheckedIn(true)
    setWeek(buildWeek(nextStreak, true))
  }

  if (!mounted) return null
  const tier = getTier(streak)

  return (
    <div className="m-hud-panel">
      <span className="m-hud-scan" />
      <div className="m-hud-header">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-[#22d3ee]" />
          STUDY STREAK
        </div>
        <span style={{ color: tier.accent, opacity: 0.9 }}>{tier.label}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4">
        {/* Big number */}
        <div className="text-center mb-3">
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-center gap-2">
            <div
              className="text-4xl font-bold tabular-nums leading-none"
              style={{ color: tier.accent, textShadow: '0 0 22px ' + tier.accent + '88' }}
            >
              {streak}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.2em] text-[#566074]">
              {streak === 1 ? 'DAY' : 'DAYS'}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono tracking-[0.3em] text-[#3a4250] mt-1.5">
            CURRENT STREAK
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Week row */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 mb-3 px-1">
          {WEEK_LABELS.map((label, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
              <div
                className="rounded-full transition-all"
                style={{
                  width: 8,
                  height: 8,
                  background: week[i] ? '#22d3ee' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.12)',
                  boxShadow: week[i] ? '0 0 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.7)' : 'none',
                }}
              />
              <span
                className="text-[8px] font-mono tracking-widest"
                style={{ color: week[i] ? '#a5f3fc' : '#3a4250' }}
              >
                {label}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Stats row */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mb-3">
          <div
            className="rounded-md px-3 py-2"
            style={{ background: 'rgba(34,211,238,0.04)', border: '1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.14)' }}
          >
            <div className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] mb-0.5">BEST</div>
            <div className="text-sm font-bold text-[#a5f3fc] tabular-nums">{best} d</div>
          </div>
          <div
            className="rounded-md px-3 py-2"
            style={{ background: 'rgba(139,92,246,0.04)', border: '1px solid rgba(139,92,246,0.14)' }}
          >
            <div className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-[0.25em] text-[#566074] mb-0.5">TOTAL</div>
            <div className="text-sm font-bold text-[#c4b5fd] tabular-nums">{total} d</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CTA */}
        {checkedIn ? (
          <div
            className="text-[11px] font-mono tracking-[0.15em] text-center text-[#10b981] rounded-md py-2"
            style={{ border: '1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.4)', background: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.06)' }}
          >
            âœ“ CHECKED IN
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button
            onClick={checkIn}
            className="w-full bg-[#22d3ee] text-black text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase rounded-md py-2.5 hover:bg-cyan-300 transition"
          >
            Check In Today
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// FILE: components/ThemeProvider.tsx

'use client'

import { useEffect } from 'react'

export default function ThemeProvider({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = (localStorage.getItem('m-theme') as 'dark' | 'light') || 'dark'
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', stored)
  }, [])

  return <>{children}</>
}

// FILE: components/ThemeToggle.tsx

'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

export default function ThemeToggle({ compact = false }: { compact?: boolean }) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<'dark' | 'light'>('dark')
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = (localStorage.getItem('m-theme') as 'dark' | 'light') || 'dark'
    setTheme(stored)
    setMounted(true)
  }, [])

  function toggle() {
    const next = theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'
    setTheme(next)
    localStorage.setItem('m-theme', next)
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', next)
  }

  if (!mounted) return null

  if (compact) {
    return (
      <button
        onClick={toggle}
        aria-label="Toggle theme"
        className="p-2 rounded-lg text-[var(--m-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)] hover:bg-white/[0.03] transition text-lg"
      >
        {theme === 'dark' ? 'â˜€' : 'â˜¾'}
      </button>
    )
  }

  return (
    <button
      onClick={toggle}
      className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-[var(--m-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--m-text)] hover:bg-white/[0.03] transition"
    >
      <span className="text-base w-5 text-center opacity-80">
        {theme === 'dark' ? 'â˜€' : 'â˜¾'}
      </span>
      <span className="font-medium">
        {theme === 'dark' ? 'Light mode' : 'Dark mode'}
      </span>
    </button>
  )
}

// FILE: lib/auth.ts
import { cache } from 'react'
import { createClient } from './supabase/server'

export const getCurrentUser = cache(async () => {
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  return user
})

export const getCurrentProfile = cache(async () => {
  const supabase = await createClient()
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) return null
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('profiles')
    .select('*')
    .eq('id', user.id)
    .single()
  return data
})

export function pickQuote(seed?: number): DisciplineQuote {
  const i =
    seed === undefined
      ? Math.floor(Math.random() * DISCIPLINE_QUOTES.length)
      : Math.abs(seed) % DISCIPLINE_QUOTES.length
  return DISCIPLINE_QUOTES[i]
}

================================================================
FILE: lib\navZone.ts

export type NavZone = 'sidebar' | 'content'

type ZoneStore = {
  active: NavZone
  listeners: Set<(z: NavZone) => void>
}

function getStore(): ZoneStore {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return { active: 'content', listeners: new Set() }
  }
  const w = window as unknown as { __meridianZone?: ZoneStore }
  if (!w.__meridianZone) {
    w.__meridianZone = { active: 'content', listeners: new Set() }
  }
  return w.__meridianZone
}

export function getActiveZone(): NavZone {
  return getStore().active
}

export function setActiveZone(zone: NavZone) {
  const store = getStore()
  if (store.active === zone) return
  store.active = zone
  store.listeners.forEach((l) => l(zone))
}

export function onZoneChange(cb: (z: NavZone) => void) {
  const store = getStore()
  store.listeners.add(cb)
  return () => {
    store.listeners.delete(cb)
  }
}

export function installZoneTracker() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {}
  function onClick(e: MouseEvent) {
    const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null
    if (!target) return
    const el = target.closest('[data-nav-zone]') as HTMLElement | null
    if (!el) return
    const zone = el.getAttribute('data-nav-zone')
    if (zone === 'sidebar' || zone === 'content') {
      setActiveZone(zone)
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', onClick, true)
  return () => document.removeEventListener('click', onClick, true)
}

FILE: lib\sounds.ts

type SoundsStore = {
  ctx: AudioContext | null
  unlocked: boolean
}

function getStore(): SoundsStore {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return { ctx: null, unlocked: false }
  }
  const w = window as unknown as { __meridianSounds?: SoundsStore }
  if (!w.__meridianSounds) {
    w.__meridianSounds = { ctx: null, unlocked: false }
  }
  return w.__meridianSounds
}

function getCtx(): AudioContext | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null
  const store = getStore()
  if (!store.ctx) {
    const w = window as unknown as {
      AudioContext?: typeof AudioContext
      webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext
    }
    const Ctor = w.AudioContext || w.webkitAudioContext
    if (!Ctor) return null
    store.ctx = new Ctor()
  }
  if (store.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
    store.ctx.resume().catch(() => {})
  }
  return store.ctx
}

export function unlockAudio() {
  const store = getStore()
  if (store.unlocked) return
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return
  if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
    ctx.resume().then(() => { store.unlocked = true }).catch(() => {})
  } else {
    store.unlocked = true
  }
}

export function isAudioUnlocked() {
  const store = getStore()
  return store.unlocked && store.ctx !== null && store.ctx.state === 'running'
}

export function playInitializingSound(durationMs = 2200) {
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return
  const dur = durationMs / 1000
  const t0 = ctx.currentTime

  const noiseLen = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur)
  const noiseBuffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, noiseLen, ctx.sampleRate)
  const data = noiseBuffer.getChannelData(0)
  for (let i = 0; i < noiseLen; i++) data[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1
  const noise = ctx.createBufferSource()
  noise.buffer = noiseBuffer
  const noiseFilter = ctx.createBiquadFilter()
  noiseFilter.type = 'bandpass'
  noiseFilter.Q.value = 6
  noiseFilter.frequency.setValueAtTime(180, t0)
  noiseFilter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(2800, t0 + dur)
  const noiseGain = ctx.createGain()
  noiseGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0)
  noiseGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.10, t0 + 0.6)
  noiseGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.10, t0 + dur - 0.5)
  noiseGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + dur)
  noise.connect(noiseFilter)
  noiseFilter.connect(noiseGain)
  noiseGain.connect(ctx.destination)
  noise.start(t0)
  noise.stop(t0 + dur)

  const sweep = ctx.createOscillator()
  const sweepGain = ctx.createGain()
  sweep.connect(sweepGain)
  sweepGain.connect(ctx.destination)
  sweep.type = 'sine'
  sweep.frequency.setValueAtTime(180, t0)
  sweep.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(720, t0 + dur)
  sweepGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0)
  sweepGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.08, t0 + 0.5)
  sweepGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, t0 + dur - 0.4)
  sweepGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + dur)
  sweep.start(t0)
  sweep.stop(t0 + dur)

  const sub = ctx.createOscillator()
  const subGain = ctx.createGain()
  sub.connect(subGain)
  subGain.connect(ctx.destination)
  sub.type = 'sine'
  sub.frequency.setValueAtTime(40, t0)
  sub.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(90, t0 + dur)
  subGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0)
  subGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.09, t0 + 0.4)
  subGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.09, t0 + dur - 0.4)
  subGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + dur)
  sub.start(t0)
  sub.stop(t0 + dur)

  const bell = ctx.createOscillator()
  const bellGain = ctx.createGain()
  bell.connect(bellGain)
  bellGain.connect(ctx.destination)
  bell.type = 'sine'
  bell.frequency.value = 1568
  const bellStart = t0 + dur - 0.3
  bellGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, bellStart)
  bellGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.18, bellStart + 0.03)
  bellGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, bellStart + 0.75)
  bell.start(bellStart)
  bell.stop(bellStart + 0.85)
}

export function playTransitionSound(durationMs = 1400) {
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return
  const dur = durationMs / 1000
  const t0 = ctx.currentTime

  const noiseLen = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur)
  const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, noiseLen, ctx.sampleRate)
  const d = buf.getChannelData(0)
  for (let i = 0; i < noiseLen; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1
  const noise = ctx.createBufferSource()
  noise.buffer = buf
  const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter()
  filter.type = 'lowpass'
  filter.Q.value = 4
  filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(2400, t0)
  filter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(400, t0 + dur)
  const nGain = ctx.createGain()
  nGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0)
  nGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.06, t0 + 0.15)
  nGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + dur)
  noise.connect(filter)
  filter.connect(nGain)
  nGain.connect(ctx.destination)
  noise.start(t0)
  noise.stop(t0 + dur)

  const notes = [
    { freq: 880, delay: 0.05, gain: 0.09 },
    { freq: 1318.5, delay: 0.35, gain: 0.08 },
  ]
  notes.forEach(({ freq, delay, gain: peak }) => {
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator()
    const g = ctx.createGain()
    osc.connect(g)
    g.connect(ctx.destination)
    osc.type = 'sine'
    osc.frequency.value = freq
    const t = t0 + delay
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(peak, t + 0.03)
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.9)
    osc.start(t)
    osc.stop(t + 1.0)
  })
}

export function startAmbientHum(): () => void {
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return () => {}

  const osc1 = ctx.createOscillator()
  const osc2 = ctx.createOscillator()
  const osc3 = ctx.createOscillator()
  const gain = ctx.createGain()
  const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter()

  osc1.type = 'sine'
  osc1.frequency.value = 55
  osc2.type = 'sine'
  osc2.frequency.value = 82.5
  osc3.type = 'triangle'
  osc3.frequency.value = 165

  filter.type = 'lowpass'
  filter.frequency.value = 320
  filter.Q.value = 0.7

  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime)
  gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.03, ctx.currentTime + 2.5)

  osc1.connect(filter)
  osc2.connect(filter)
  osc3.connect(filter)
  filter.connect(gain)
  gain.connect(ctx.destination)

  osc1.start()
  osc2.start()
  osc3.start()

  return () => {
    const t = ctx.currentTime
    gain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t)
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(gain.gain.value, t)
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.6)
    setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        osc1.stop()
        osc2.stop()
        osc3.stop()
      } catch {
        /* already stopped */
      }
    }, 800)
  }
}

export function playTickSound(progress = 0) {
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return
  const baseFreq = 1200 + (progress / 100) * 1400
  const t = ctx.currentTime

  const osc = ctx.createOscillator()
  const gain = ctx.createGain()
  osc.connect(gain)
  gain.connect(ctx.destination)
  osc.type = 'sine'
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(baseFreq, t)
  osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(baseFreq * 1.15, t + 0.05)
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.035, t + 0.006)
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.09)
  osc.start(t)
  osc.stop(t + 0.1)

  const sheen = ctx.createOscillator()
  const sheenGain = ctx.createGain()
  sheen.connect(sheenGain)
  sheenGain.connect(ctx.destination)
  sheen.type = 'sine'
  sheen.frequency.value = baseFreq * 2
  sheenGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
  sheenGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.012, t + 0.003)
  sheenGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.06)
  sheen.start(t)
  sheen.stop(t + 0.08)
}

export function playSelectSound() {
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return
  const t = ctx.currentTime

  const osc = ctx.createOscillator()
  const gain = ctx.createGain()
  osc.connect(gain)
  gain.connect(ctx.destination)
  osc.type = 'sine'
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1480, t)
  osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1720, t + 0.06)
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.05, t + 0.008)
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.11)
  osc.start(t)
  osc.stop(t + 0.12)

  const osc2 = ctx.createOscillator()
  const gain2 = ctx.createGain()
  osc2.connect(gain2)
  gain2.connect(ctx.destination)
  osc2.type = 'triangle'
  osc2.frequency.value = 2960
  gain2.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
  gain2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.015, t + 0.004)
  gain2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.07)
  osc2.start(t)
  osc2.stop(t + 0.09)
}

export function playConfirmSound() {
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return
  const t = ctx.currentTime

  const o1 = ctx.createOscillator()
  const g1 = ctx.createGain()
  o1.connect(g1)
  g1.connect(ctx.destination)
  o1.type = 'triangle'
  o1.frequency.setValueAtTime(660, t)
  o1.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(990, t + 0.08)
  g1.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
  g1.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.11, t + 0.01)
  g1.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.15)
  o1.start(t)
  o1.stop(t + 0.18)

  const o2 = ctx.createOscillator()
  const g2 = ctx.createGain()
  o2.connect(g2)
  g2.connect(ctx.destination)
  o2.type = 'triangle'
  o2.frequency.setValueAtTime(990, t + 0.09)
  o2.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1480, t + 0.19)
  g2.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.09)
  g2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.10, t + 0.10)
  g2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.25)
  o2.start(t + 0.09)
  o2.stop(t + 0.28)
}

export function playPassSound() {
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return
  const t0 = ctx.currentTime
  const notes = [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5, 1318.5]
  notes.forEach((freq, i) => {
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator()
    const gain = ctx.createGain()
    osc.connect(gain)
    gain.connect(ctx.destination)
    osc.type = 'triangle'
    osc.frequency.value = freq
    const t = t0 + i * 0.09
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.16, t + 0.015)
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.55)
    osc.start(t)
    osc.stop(t + 0.6)

    const sh = ctx.createOscillator()
    const shG = ctx.createGain()
    sh.connect(shG)
    shG.connect(ctx.destination)
    sh.type = 'sine'
    sh.frequency.value = freq * 2
    shG.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t)
    shG.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.04, t + 0.02)
    shG.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.4)
    sh.start(t)
    sh.stop(t + 0.45)
  })
}

export function playFailSound() {
  const ctx = getCtx()
  if (!ctx) return
  const t0 = ctx.currentTime

  const osc = ctx.createOscillator()
  const gain = ctx.createGain()
  osc.connect(gain)
  gain.connect(ctx.destination)
  osc.type = 'sawtooth'
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(300, t0)
  osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(70, t0 + 1.0)
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0)
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.13, t0 + 0.03)
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + 1.1)
  osc.start(t0)
  osc.stop(t0 + 1.2)

  const rumble = ctx.createOscillator()
  const rumbleGain = ctx.createGain()
  rumble.connect(rumbleGain)
  rumbleGain.connect(ctx.destination)
  rumble.type = 'sine'
  rumble.frequency.value = 55
  rumbleGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0)
  rumbleGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.10, t0 + 0.02)
  rumbleGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + 0.5)
  rumble.start(t0)
  rumble.stop(t0 + 0.55)
}

import { createBrowserClient } from '@supabase/ssr'

export function createClient() {
  return createBrowserClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!
  )
}


================================================================
FILE: lib\supabase\server.ts
================================================================

import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { cookies } from 'next/headers'

export async function createClient() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies()

  return createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return cookieStore.getAll()
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          try {
            cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
              cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
            )
          } catch {}
        },
      },
    }
  )
}

FILE: proxy.ts

// FILE: proxy.ts
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server'
import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr'

export async function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl

  // Public paths â€” never intercept
  const PUBLIC_PATHS = ['/', '/login', '/signup', '/forgot-password', '/reset-password']
  if (PUBLIC_PATHS.some(p => pathname === p || pathname.startsWith(p + '/'))) {
    return NextResponse.next({ request })
  }

  // Skip prefetches
  const isPrefetch =
    request.headers.get('next-router-prefetch') === '1' ||
    request.headers.get('purpose') === 'prefetch'
  if (isPrefetch) return NextResponse.next({ request })

  // Skip if no auth cookie
  const hasAuthCookie = request.cookies
    .getAll()
    .some(c => c.name.startsWith('sb-') && c.name.includes('auth-token'))
  if (!hasAuthCookie) return NextResponse.next({ request })

  let response = NextResponse.next({ request })

  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() { return request.cookies.getAll() },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value }) => request.cookies.set(name, value))
          response = NextResponse.next({ request })
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
            response.cookies.set(name, value, options)
          )
        },
      },
    }
  )

  await supabase.auth.getUser()
  return response
}

export const config = {
  // CRITICAL: exclude all files with extensions (.png .jpg .css .js .svg etc)
  // Next.js 16 proxy intercepts EVERYTHING by default â€” including your images.
  // The `.*\\..*` pattern means "any path containing a dot" â€” that's how we
  // skip every static asset (images, fonts, css, js chunks).
  matcher: [
    '/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\..*).*)',
  ],
}

DO $nuke$
DECLARE c uuid;
BEGIN
  SELECT id INTO c FROM courses WHERE slug = 'english-to-dutch-mastery';
  IF c IS NOT NULL THEN
    DELETE FROM quiz_questions WHERE lesson_id IN (
      SELECT l.id FROM lessons l
      JOIN missions m ON l.mission_id = m.id
      JOIN modules mo ON m.module_id = mo.id
      WHERE mo.course_id = c
    );
    DELETE FROM lesson_progress WHERE lesson_id IN (
      SELECT l.id FROM lessons l
      JOIN missions m ON l.mission_id = m.id
      JOIN modules mo ON m.module_id = mo.id
      WHERE mo.course_id = c
    );
    DELETE FROM lessons WHERE mission_id IN (
      SELECT m.id FROM missions m
      JOIN modules mo ON m.module_id = mo.id
      WHERE mo.course_id = c
    );
    DELETE FROM missions WHERE module_id IN (
      SELECT id FROM modules WHERE course_id = c
    );
    DELETE FROM modules WHERE course_id = c;
    DELETE FROM enrollments WHERE course_id = c;
    DELETE FROM courses WHERE id = c;
  END IF;
END $nuke$;

DO $book1_ch4_6$
DECLARE b_id uuid; c_id uuid;
BEGIN
  SELECT id INTO b_id FROM library_books WHERE slug = 'the-language-architect';

  -- Chapter 4
  INSERT INTO library_chapters (book_id, chapter_number, title, content, word_count)
  VALUES (b_id, 4, 'The Architecture of Meaning', $c$
# The Architecture of Meaning

If syntax is the structure of the building, meaning is the purpose of the rooms. In linguistics, this is called **semantics**.

But meaning is not a fixed object you can point to. It is a relationship. It is the bridge between a word, a concept, and a real-world experience.

## The Triangle of Reference

In the 1920s, linguists developed a model called the Triangle of Reference. It looks like this:

WORD (Symbol) -> CONCEPT (Thought) -> THING (Referent)

Let's break this down with the Dutch word: **Appel**.

- The **Word** is the sound "appel" or the letters A-P-P-E-L.
- The **Thing** is the physical fruit on the table.
- The **Concept** is the mental image of a round, crisp fruit in your brain.

When you hear "appel," the sound hits your ear, travels to your brain, and activates the concept. The concept connects to the physical thing. 

But what if you have never seen an apple? The word has no meaning because the triangle is broken. You have the symbol, but no referent.

## Conceptual Blending

Language does not just point to things. It creates new things by blending concepts.

Look at the Dutch word **"gedachte"** (thought). It is derived from "denken" (to think). But look at **"vrijheid"** (freedom). It is derived from "vrij" (free). 

The suffix **-heid** turns an adjective into a concept. It takes a quality (free) and creates a container for it (freedom). 

This is the magic of morphology. It allows you to build new meaning from existing parts. It is the linguistic equivalent of LEGO.

## Metaphor: The Hidden Architecture

Cognitive linguists George Lakoff and Mark Johnson discovered that human thought is fundamentally metaphorical. We understand abstract concepts through concrete physical experiences.

Look at how we talk about arguments:
- We "attack" a position.
- We "defend" a claim.
- We "shoot down" an idea.

We treat arguments as war. This is a conceptual metaphor.

In Dutch, you see the same thing:
- **"Punt maken"** (to make a point)
- **"Aanvallen"** (to attack)
- **"Verdedigen"** (to defend)

The metaphor is not just a poetic flourish. It is a structural framework that shapes how we think. It is the invisible architecture of our minds.

> Meaning is not a dictionary entry. It is a network of associations, metaphors, and lived experiences.

## The Semantic Network

When you learn a new Dutch word, do not just learn its translation. Learn its network.

Take the word **"gezellig"**. It is often translated as "cozy." But that is a weak translation. 

"Gezellig" is a semantic network:
- Warmth (physical)
- Togetherness (social)
- Comfort (emotional)
- Familiarity (contextual)

To truly understand "gezellig," you must build all these connections. You must see the architecture of the word.

> **Chapter 4 Reflection:** Think of the English word "home." Map out its semantic network. What concepts does it connect to? Safety? Family? A physical building? Now think about how the Dutch word "thuis" is different.

  -- Chapter 5
  INSERT INTO library_chapters (book_id, chapter_number, title, content, word_count)
  VALUES (b_id, 5, 'How Language Shapes Thought', $c$
# How Language Shapes Thought

Does the language you speak determine how you think?

For decades, linguists debated the **Sapir-Whorf hypothesis**, also known as linguistic relativity. The strong version says: if you do not have a word for something, you cannot think about it.

This is largely false. If it were true, you could never learn a new concept that didn't exist in your native language. But we do it all the time.

However, the **weak version** is true: language heavily influences how you think. It is the lens through which you view the world.

## Linguistic Framing

Think about how Dutch frames certain concepts differently than English.

### 1. The Gender of Objects
In English, a bridge is an "it." In Dutch, a bridge is a **"brug"** (a *de*-word). But look at other languages, like German or Spanish. Bridges are feminine in German and masculine in Spanish.

This shapes perception. If you ask native speakers to describe a bridge, German speakers (where it is feminine) tend to use words like "beautiful," "elegant," and "fragile." Spanish speakers (where it is masculine) use words like "strong," "sturdy," and "dangerous."

The grammatical gender is not just a rule. It is a subtle frame that guides your mental image.

### 2. The Position of the Verb
We talked about the V2 rule earlier. In Dutch, the verb is the anchor.

In English, we say: *"I saw the film yesterday."*
In Dutch, you say: *"Ik heb de film gisteren gezien."* (I have the film yesterday seen.)

The Dutch construction places the most important piece of information—the action—at the very end. It creates suspense. It emphasizes the result.

This is not just a grammar rule. It is a cognitive frame. Dutch speakers are trained to hold the action in suspense until the final word.

## Conceptual Metaphor in Dutch

Look at how Dutch speakers use the concept of **"ruimte"** (space) to talk about time. 

In English: "We are moving forward with the project."
In Dutch: "We gaan vooruit met het project."

But look at time specifically. 
English: "The future is ahead of us."
Dutch: "De toekomst ligt voor ons." (The future lies in front of us.)

In Dutch, the future is literally "in front" (voor). The past is "behind" (achter). This is a spatial metaphor for time. It is the architecture of how they perceive history and progress.

## The Architect's Advantage

When you learn Dutch, you are not just learning a new vocabulary. You are learning a new way to perceive reality. You are learning to see the world through the lens of V2 word order, the *de/het* distinction, and the guttural consonants.

This gives you a massive cognitive advantage. You develop **cognitive flexibility**—the ability to switch between different mental models.

> The limits of my language mean the limits of my world. - Ludwig Wittgenstein

By learning Dutch, you expand the limits of your world.

> **Chapter 5 Reflection:** Think about the word "freedom." How does Dutch define it (vrijheid) vs English (freedom)? What cultural histories are embedded in those words?
  $c$, 580) RETURNING id INTO c_id;

  -- Chapter 6
  INSERT INTO library_chapters (book_id, chapter_number, title, content, word_count)
  VALUES (b_id, 6, 'From Human to Machine', $c$
# From Human to Machine

We have explored how human language works: sounds, syntax, semantics, and cognition. Now we enter the realm of the machine.

How do we teach a computer to understand language? We cannot teach it the "feeling" of a word. We cannot teach it semantics through experience. 

We have to teach it **statistics and geometry**.

## The Vector Space Model

Imagine a giant, multi-dimensional space. Every word in the dictionary is a point in this space. 

The position of the word is determined by its **context**. Words that appear in similar contexts are close together in this space. 

- "Dog" is close to "cat" (both animals, both pets).
- "Coffee" is close to "tea" (both drinks).
- "Dog" is far from "democracy."

This is called **Word Embedding**. It is the machine's version of the semantic network.

In Dutch, the embedding maps *hond* close to *kat*, and *koffie* close to *thee*.

## Tokenization

Before a machine can process text, it must break it down into pieces. This is called tokenization.

You might think it breaks words into letters, but it doesn't. It breaks them into **tokens**—sub-word units.

Why? Because if it used letters, it would be too slow. If it used whole words, it would fail on new words (like "unhackable" or "restyling").

So, a modern language model might tokenize "Lopen" as:
- "Lo"
- "pen"

Or it might tokenize "Gezelligheid" as:
- "Ge"
- "zellig"
- "heid"

This allows the machine to handle infinite words by combining a finite set of tokens.

## The Transformer

In 2017, researchers at Google published a paper called "Attention is All You Need." They introduced the **Transformer** architecture.

Before Transformers, machines read text sequentially (left to right, word by word). This meant they often forgot the beginning of a sentence by the time they reached the end.

The Transformer solved this with **Self-Attention**. It looks at all the words in a sentence at once and calculates how much each word should "attend" to the others.

In the sentence:
*"The animal didn't cross the street because it was too tired."*

Self-attention allows the model to figure out that "it" refers to the animal, not the street.

## Large Language Models (LLMs)

When you stack dozens of these Transformer layers on top of each other, and train them on massive amounts of text (trillions of words), you get an LLM (like GPT-4 or Claude).

The model is essentially learning to predict the next token based on the previous ones. It is a massive statistical engine.

But something emergent happens. When the model gets big enough, it develops reasoning capabilities. It learns to translate, summarize, write code, and answer complex questions.

## The Architect's Role

As a Language Architect, you are no longer just a user of these tools. You understand the architecture behind them.

You know that when an LLM produces Dutch text, it is not "thinking" like a human. It is navigating a massive vector space, predicting the next token, and using attention to understand context.

This understanding gives you the power to **build better prompts**, **detect hallucinations**, and **engineer systems** that combine human creativity with machine scale.

> The machine does not understand meaning. It understands geometry. But the results can be indistinguishable.

> **Chapter 6 Reflection:** Try to explain the difference between how a human understands "hond" and how an LLM understands "hond" to a friend. What is the fundamental difference in their representation of reality?
  $c$, 680) RETURNING id INTO c_id;

END $book1_ch4_6$;

-- NUKE all non-Meridian-Dutch-Mastery courses
-- Keeps ONLY: english-to-dutch-mastery
-- â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
DO $nuke$
DECLARE c uuid;
BEGIN
  FOR c IN SELECT id FROM courses WHERE slug <> 'english-to-dutch-mastery' LOOP
    DELETE FROM quiz_questions WHERE lesson_id IN (
      SELECT l.id FROM lessons l
      JOIN missions m ON l.mission_id = m.id
      JOIN modules mo ON m.module_id = mo.id
      WHERE mo.course_id = c
    );
    DELETE FROM lesson_progress WHERE lesson_id IN (
      SELECT l.id FROM lessons l
      JOIN missions m ON l.mission_id = m.id
      JOIN modules mo ON m.module_id = mo.id
      WHERE mo.course_id = c
    );
    DELETE FROM lessons WHERE mission_id IN (
      SELECT m.id FROM missions m
      JOIN modules mo ON m.module_id = mo.id
      WHERE mo.course_id = c
    );
    DELETE FROM missions WHERE module_id IN (
      SELECT id FROM modules WHERE course_id = c
    );
    DELETE FROM modules WHERE course_id = c;
    DELETE FROM enrollments WHERE course_id = c;
    DELETE FROM courses WHERE id = c;
  END LOOP;
END $nuke$;

# âš ï¸ False Friends â€” Words That Trick You

Not every similar word means the same thing. Some look like English but mean something else. Learn to spot them early.

---

## ðŸš¨ 01 The Big Traps

| ðŸ‡³ðŸ‡± Dutch | Looks like | Actually means |
|---|---|---|
| brood | brood | bread |
| room | room | cream |
| bos | boss | forest |
| meer | mere | lake / more |
| wil | will | want |
| rook | rook | smoke |
| pad | pad | path / toad |
| doos | dose | box |

One word can completely change the meaning of a sentence.

---

## ðŸŽ¯ 02 Context Saves You

Even if a false friend trips you up, the sentence rescues you.

- **Ik eet brood met kaas.** â†’ You *eat* brood, so it means **bread**, not offspring.

- **Ik wil een doos.** â†’ You *want* a box, not a dose of medicine.

---

## ðŸ’¡ 03 Meridian Insight

> Meaning lives in the sentence, not the word.

Reading the whole sentence is a superpower. Individual words can lie; sentences tell the truth.

---

## ðŸŽ¯ 04 New Words to Remember

| Dutch | English |
|---|---|
| met | with |
| wil | want |
| het meer | the lake |
| de doos | the box |

---

# ðŸŽ‰ Side Quest Complete

- 8 false-friend warnings
- The habit of reading full sentences
